SiConMo integrates three complementary components: a Token Pyramid Extraction Module for hierarchical multi-scale representation, a Transformer-Branched Depthwise Convolution block for bottleneck-aware context modeling, and a Feature Merging Module that preserves spatial structure while enhancing semantic consistency. Extensive experiments on ADE20K, PASCAL Context, Cityscapes, and COCO-Stuff demonstrate that SiConMo achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency trade-off among lightweight semantic segmentation models, highlighting simplicity as a powerful design principle.

</details>


### [68] [Uncertainty-Aware Art-Historical Dating with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18984)
*Stefanie Schneider,Peter Bell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了博物馆和档案数据集未能完全反映历史艺术创作全貌，而是体现了收藏、保存、编目和数字化等实践中的偶然性历史。作者提出"时间纠缠"（temporal entanglement）概念，指预训练图像表示看似编码了历史时间，实际可能编码了机构条件下物体变得可见的数据化方式。研究通过在时间受控的Wikidata艺术品语料上做不确定性感知的回归任务来验证这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博物馆和档案数据集并非历史艺术生产的直接镜像，而是收藏、保存、编目和数字化等偶然历史的物质化结果。这导致预训练图像表示可能编码的是机构条件而非真实历史时间，对图像年代推断任务构成根本性挑战。

Method: 将艺术品年代推断构造为基于冻结图像嵌入的不确定性感知回归任务；在时间受控的Wikidata艺术品语料上评估多个预训练视觉模型，包括视觉-语言模型（VLMs）和纯视觉自监督基线。

Result: 模型包含可用的时间信息，视觉-语言模型（VLMs）优于纯视觉自监督基线；但定性分析表明这些时间知识受到各种偏差的影响。

Conclusion: 预训练视觉模型虽然包含时间信息，但其时间判断受机构性偏差塑造，印证了"时间纠缠"现象的存在，提示在文化遗产计算任务中需谨慎解读模型的时间表征。

Abstract: Museum and archival datasets do not mirror historical artistic production, but materialize the contingent histories of collecting, preservation, cataloging, and digitization. This has direct consequences for interpreting pretrained image representations: they may appear to encode historical time while actually encoding the institutional conditions under which objects become visible as data. We describe this phenomenon as temporal entanglement and investigate it by formulating artwork dating as an uncertainty-aware regression task over frozen image embeddings. We evaluate several pretrained vision models on a temporally controlled Wikidata corpus of artworks. Our results show that these models contain usable temporal information, with Vision-Language Models (VLMs) outperforming purely visual self-supervised baselines. However, a qualitative analysis indicates that this temporal knowledge is shaped by various biases.

</details>


### [69] [X-LMC: Cross-View Spatiotemporal Collateral Circulation Scoring from DSA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18986)
*Maedeh Hafezi Moghadas,Hakim Baazaoui,Lukas Bastian Otto,Susanne Wegener,Björn Menze,Ezequiel De la Rosa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-LMC是一个用于自动评估软脑膜侧支（LMC）的时空深度学习框架，首次从DSA影像中自动完成ASITN/SIR分级。采用DINOv2骨干编码空间特征、跨视角注意力融合双平面投影、循环网络建模造影剂动力学，在134例M1段闭塞患者的多中心数据集上，5折交叉验证中QWK达0.398，宏F1为0.711，优于静态和时空基线方法，且性能接近临床医师间一致性水平（QWK: 0.314）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA是LMC评估的金标准，但临床通过ASITN/SIR量表进行人工分级耗时且主观变异性大。目前缺乏自动化的LMC评分工具，亟需一个可复现的客观评估框架来减少观察者间差异，并为卒中患者的治疗决策、康复规划和回顾性研究提供可靠的定量依据。

Method: 提出X-LMC时空框架：（1）采用DINOv2视觉骨干编码DSA的空间帧特征；（2）通过token级跨视角注意力模块融合正位和侧位两个正交投影的信息；（3）利用循环神经网络架构建模造影剂团注的时序动态。在134例M1段闭塞患者的多中心数据集上进行5折交叉验证评估。

Result: 与最佳基线方法相比，X-LMC取得更高的性能：加权二次Kappa（QWK）从0.322提升至0.398，二分类宏F1从0.663提升至0.711。其性能与临床观察者间一致性（QWK: 0.314）相当，表明自动化评分已达到临床可接受的一致水平。

Conclusion: X-LMC作为首个自动化LMC评分的DSA研究，证明了多视角时序深度学习能够捕捉侧支特异性的对比剂动力学特征。该工作界定了自动化ASITN/SIR分级的临床模糊性和可实现性能边界，为卒中队列的客观血流动力学表型分析建立了可复现的基础。代码已开源。

Abstract: Digital subtraction angiography (DSA) is the reference standard for leptomeningeal collateral (LMC) assessment, providing critical prognostic insights to guide secondary treatment strategies, neurorehabilitation planning, and retrospective stroke research. However, clinical LMC grading via the ASITN/SIR scale relies on manual, highly variable visual inspection. We introduce X-LMC, a spatiotemporal framework for automated collateral scoring from time-resolved biplane DSA. The proposed architecture encodes spatial frame representations through a DINOv2 backbone, fuses orthogonal projections via a token-level cross-view attention module, and models representations of contrast bolus dynamics using a recurrent network architecture. We evaluate our framework on a multicenter dataset of 134 patients with M1-segment occlusions. In a 5-fold cross-validation setting, X-LMC yields higher point estimates than static architectures and spatiotemporal baselines adapted from related angiographic tasks, achieving a Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) of 0.398 (vs. 0.322) and a dichotomized macro-F1 score of 0.711 (vs. 0.663) against the best-performing baseline. X-LMC performance also aligns with the observed clinical inter-rater agreement (QWK: 0.314). As the first DSA study attempting to automate LMC scoring, we demonstrate that multi-view temporal deep learning can capture collateral-specific contrast kinetics. Ultimately, these benchmarks delineate the clinical ambiguities and achievable performance boundaries of automated ASITN/SIR grading, establishing a reproducible foundation for objective hemodynamic phenotyping in stroke cohorts. Code is available at https://github.com/maedehafezi/X-LMC.

</details>


### [70] [ForeSightGuide: An Anticipatory Framework toward Accurate and Low-Redundancy Guidance for the Visually Impaired](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18993)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Xu Li,Shikang Guo,Wei Meng,Quan Liu,Jie Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeSightGuide，一个结合语义场景理解与预测性危险评估的前瞻性辅助导盲框架，利用视觉语言模型的推理能力预判障碍物运动，过滤非威胁对象以减少误报，在公共基准和新建动态交通场景数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子出行辅助对视觉障碍者的独立移动至关重要，但视觉语言模型在动态场景中常产生过多误报，导致认知过载，影响用户安全与体验。

Method: 提出ForeSightGuide框架，利用VLM的推理能力对障碍物运动进行预判（预测性危险评估），仅对具有威胁的对象提供引导，实现前瞻性而非反应式的辅助；同时构建了一个复杂动态真实交通场景的新数据集用于评估预测能力。

Result: 在公共基准和所提数据集上均达到最先进性能，将冗余告警降至每输出0.299个，同时保持低漏检危险率0.112。

Conclusion: ForeSightGuide通过预测性危险评估有效缓解信息过载，同时保证低漏检率，验证了其在安全步行辅助中的有效性。

Abstract: Electronic travel aids are pivotal for the independent mobility of the visually impaired. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer rich environmental understanding, they often suffer from excessive false positives in dynamic scenarios, leading to cognitive overload. To address this, we present ForeSightGuide, an anticipatory assistive guidance framework that couples semantic scene understanding with predictive hazard assessment. Unlike reactive systems, ForeSightGuide leverages the reasoning capabilities of VLMs to anticipate obstacle motion, effectively filtering out non-threatening objects to provide concise, actionable guidance. To validate our approach, we introduce a novel dataset captured in complex, dynamic real-world traffic scenes, designed to benchmark predictive capabilities. Extensive experiments on both public benchmarks and our proposed dataset demonstrate that ForeSightGuide achieves state-of-the-art performance. Notably, it significantly mitigates information overload by reducing redundant alerts to 0.299 per guidance output while maintaining a low missed-hazard rate of 0.112, proving its efficacy for safe walking assistance.

</details>


### [71] [GrabVG: Graph-Attentive Binding for Visual Grounding in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18996)
*Chaowei Wang,Yan Di,Jingjun Sun,Baozhe Liu,Jiaxu Tian,Yuheng Li,Guangqian Guo,Shan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GrabVG，一个受人类视觉搜索启发的无人机图像视觉定位框架，通过"前注意假设搜索"和"图注意特征绑定"两阶段分解，显著提升了在高度拥挤场景下的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍图像中物体小、密集且视觉相似，造成高视觉冗余和强拓扑歧义。现有方法侧重视觉-语言特征对齐或密集上下文交互，但难以区分细微的实例间差异并有效利用空间拓扑结构，导致在高度拥挤场景下定位不准确。

Method: GrabVG将视觉定位显式分解为两个阶段：(1) 前注意假设搜索阶段，通过蒸馏引导的提议归纳和文本感知的假设过滤生成紧凑可靠的物体假设集；(2) 图注意特征绑定阶段，将这些假设组织成稀疏图，通过图注意力机制联合绑定和传播语言引导的实例内视觉线索与实例间拓扑关系，实现高效空间推理和精确目标定位。

Result: 在AerialVG和AerialSense数据集上，GrabVG分别达到67.31%和80.34%的Acc@0.5，超越相应基线10.55和8.76个百分点，同时实现了良好的精度-速度权衡。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类视觉搜索的"先粗后精"认知过程，GrabVG有效解决了无人机图像中小目标密集分布带来的视觉冗余和拓扑歧义问题，验证了将定位问题分解为假设搜索与特征绑定两阶段的有效性，为拥挤场景下的跨模态定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: Visual grounding in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery aims to localize a target object in complex bird's-eye-view scenes according to a natural language description. However, the abundance of small, densely distributed, and visually similar objects creates high visual redundancy, while repetitive local configurations give rise to strong topological ambiguity. Existing approaches mainly focus on visual--language feature alignment or dense contextual interaction, yet they struggle to distinguish subtle inter-instance differences and effectively exploit spatial topological structures, leading to inaccurate grounding in highly crowded scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{GrabVG}$, a novel visual grounding framework inspired by human visual search. GrabVG explicitly decomposes grounding into two sequential stages: $\textit{preattentive hypothesis search}$ and $\textit{graph-attentive feature binding}$. Specifically, we first generate a compact set of reliable object hypotheses through distillation-guided proposal induction and text-aware hypothesis filtering, substantially reducing background distractions and semantic mismatches. These hypotheses are then organized into a sparse graph, where language-guided intra-instance visual cues and inter-instance topological relationships are jointly bound and propagated via graph attention, enabling efficient spatial reasoning and accurate target localization. Extensive experiments on AerialVG and AerialSense show that GrabVG achieves a favorable accuracy--speed trade-off, reaching 67.31$\%$ and 80.34$\%$ Acc@0.5 and outperforming the corresponding baselines by 10.55 and 8.76 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [72] [Mise-en-Scène: Implicit Layout Emergence in Diffusion Transformers for Human-AI Design Co-Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19000)
*Zipeng Xu,Ryan Murdock,Umberto Michieli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mise-en-Scène，一个两阶段框架，利用预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer隐式生成图形设计布局，无需显式的边界框预测，再通过确定性匹配-放置步骤保证资产保真度，在PrismLayersPlus基准上显著优于LLM布局规划器和专用布局Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将图形设计合成分为两步：先用语言模型预测显式的边界框坐标作为布局，再将资产粘贴进去。这种将空间规划与视觉合成分离的做法容易产生僵硬、比例失调的构图。作者提出疑问：布局是否可以在预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer内部隐式地涌现出来，从而实现更连贯、更协调的整体构图。

Method: Mise-en-Scène包含两个阶段：(1) 第一阶段使用一个仅经过小型knockout选择LoRA适配的扩散Transformer，联合生成完整设计草稿，其中元素排列与渲染画布协同涌现；(2) 第二阶段通过确定性的匹配-放置步骤，将原始高分辨率资产移动到草稿中的对应位置，保证资产保真度，并生成可分层的可编辑设计而非扁平图像。该方法仅需对预训练Transformer进行最小化适配，无需专门为多元素生成设计条件机制。

Result: 在PrismLayersPlus基准上，Mise-en-Scène生成的设计在感知质量上与ground truth最为接近，大幅优于LLM布局规划器和专用布局Transformer。匹配-放置阶段进一步弥补了与ground truth合成之间的保真度差距。

Conclusion: 研究表明，借助预训练图像编辑扩散Transformer的隐式布局涌现能力，可以实现更自然、更协调的图形设计合成，同时通过确定性匹配-放置步骤保证资产保真度和可编辑性，为图形设计自动化提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Automating graphic design synthesis from user-provided elements requires both a coherent overall composition and the exact preservation of each asset. Existing methods predict a layout as explicit bounding-box coordinates with a language model and then paste the assets into it, which separates spatial planning from visual synthesis and tends to produce rigid, mis-scaled compositions. We instead ask whether the layout can emerge implicitly inside a pretrained image-editing diffusion transformer. We present Mise-en-Scène, a two-stage framework. In the first stage, a diffusion transformer adapted with a small, knockout-selected LoRA drafts a complete design in which the arrangement of the elements emerges jointly with the rendered canvas. In the second stage, a deterministic match-and-place step moves the original high-resolution assets to the drafted positions, which guarantees exact asset fidelity and yields an editable, layered design that a designer can keep refining rather than a flat image. Notably, a minimal adaptation of the pretrained transformer already suffices, without the specialized conditioning machinery commonly introduced for multi-element generation. On the large-scale PrismLayersPlus benchmark, the designs produced by Mise-en-Scène are the closest to the ground truth in perceived quality among all compared methods, by a wide margin over both an LLM layout planner and a specialized layout transformer, while our match-and-place stage bridges the remaining fidelity gap to the ground-truth composites.

</details>


### [73] [One-Stage Object Detectors in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19014)
*Jonel Roman,Ryan Sirjue,Peter Nguyen,Daniel Krutky,Juan Jesus,Sudip Dhakal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本篇论文是一篇关于自动驾驶场景下单阶段目标检测器的综合性综述，回顾了从YOLOv1、SSD、RetinaNet、EfficientDet到anchor-free检测器（FCOS、CenterNet）及YOLOv10等主流单阶段检测架构的演进，并从设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数、部署权衡和基准性能等方面进行系统比较，同时总结了常用自动驾驶数据集、评估指标、开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖快速且可靠的感知能力来实时检测周围车辆、行人、骑行者、交通标志等道路物体，而单阶段目标检测器因其速度与精度兼顾的特性在实时自动驾驶感知中扮演关键角色。然而，目前缺乏对单阶段检测器在自动驾驶场景下的系统性综述，研究者需要一份全面的比较分析来了解各架构的优缺点及实际部署中的权衡。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，系统梳理并比较了主要单阶段检测器的演进历程，包括YOLO系列（v1至v10）、SSD、RetinaNet、EfficientDet以及FCOS、CenterNet等anchor-free检测器。比较维度涵盖网络架构设计选择、特征融合策略、损失函数设计、模型部署权衡及基准测试性能，并汇总了自动驾驶领域常用的数据集和评估指标。

Result: 调查结果显示，单阶段检测器在速度、精度、效率和鲁棒性之间需要精细权衡；尽管各架构在基准数据集上取得了显著进展，但基准测试结果与真实世界自动驾驶环境中的可靠性能之间仍存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 该综述强调单阶段检测器在自动驾驶感知中的核心地位，指出其设计核心在于平衡速度、精度、效率和鲁棒性。同时，本文揭示了基准性能与实际自动驾驶可靠性之间的鸿沟，并指出了未来研究方向，包括提升检测器的鲁棒性和泛化能力以应对复杂真实世界场景。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on fast and reliable perception systems to detect surrounding vehicles, pedestrians, cyclists, traffic signs, and other road objects in real time. This paper presents a comprehensive survey and analysis of one-stage object detectors for autonomous driving rather than an implementation of a new detection system. The survey reviews the evolution of major one-stage detectors, including YOLOv1, SSD, RetinaNet, EfficientDet, anchor-free detectors such as FCOS and CenterNet, and recent real-time models such as YOLOv10. It compares these architectures through their design choices, feature-fusion strategies, loss functions, deployment trade-offs, and reported benchmark performance. The paper also summarizes commonly used autonomous-driving datasets, evaluation metrics, open challenges, and future research directions. Overall, this survey highlights how one-stage detectors balance speed, accuracy, efficiency, and robustness, while also emphasizing the remaining gap between benchmark results and dependable real-world autonomous-driving performance.

</details>


### [74] [Orthogonal Polynomial Approximation for Matrix Log Normalization in Global Covariance Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19021)
*Md Rifat Ur Rahman,Md Raihan Khan,Md Sakib Hossain Shovon,Pietro Liò,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出用多项式近似（推荐8阶Chebyshev展开）计算协方差矩阵的矩阵对数，替代传统基于特征分解的谱方法，避免了梯度数值不稳定的问题。通过均值特征值预归一化使谱中心靠近1，所有运算变为GEMM，在三个细粒度基准和ImageNet-1k上比谱对数及平方根近似更快更准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局协方差池化（GCP）能有效提升细粒度识别性能，但在使用欧氏分类器前需要对SPD流形上的协方差矩阵进行归一化。忠实的选择是矩阵对数（MLN-COV），但其基于特征分解的梯度数值不稳定，实践中被矩阵平方根替代。作者希望证明这种不稳定性源于谱分解计算对数的伪影而非对数本身，从而恢复使用矩阵对数这一更忠实的Riemannian映射。

Method: 用有限多项式（推荐8阶Chebyshev展开，通过三项矩阵递推实现）近似矩阵对数，前后向传播均无需特征分解，所有操作化为GEMM。关键技巧是均值特征值预归一化：将谱中心移到1附近（远离log的奇异点），再通过标量后补偿在闭式中恢复log(A)的奇异部分。反向传播采用匹配的反向递推。同时用Legendre、Laguerre、Taylor和Padé展开作为对照组，以隔离基函数和目标函数的作用。

Result: 在三个细粒度基准数据集和ImageNet-1k上，无分解的对数方法比谱对数和平方根近似更快且更准确。在匹配基函数和阶数的条件下，log目标优于平方根目标，证实性能提升来自忠实的Riemannian映射而非更好的多项式族。

Conclusion: 矩阵对数的数值不稳定性可以通过多项式近似和均值特征值预归一化来完全消除，使对数归一化成为实践中可行且优于平方根的选择。这一发现确认了对数才是GCP中更忠实的归一化方案，消除了此前阻碍其使用的数值障碍。

Abstract: Global Covariance Pooling (GCP) improves deep networks by capturing second-order feature statistics, and is especially effective for fine-grained recognition. Because covariance matrices live on the Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) manifold, a normalization step is required before the Euclidean classifier. The faithful choice is the matrix logarithm (MLN-COV), which maps the SPD manifold to its tangent space; in practice it was abandoned in favour of the matrix square root because its eigendecomposition-based gradient is numerically unstable. We show that this instability is an artifact of computing the logarithm spectrally, not of the logarithm itself. Approximating the logarithm with finite polynomials in the covariance matrix removes the eigendecomposition from both passes: every operation becomes a General Matrix Multiplication (GEMM), the gradient stays bounded on the spectral support of the pre-normalized covariance, and the unstable 1/(lambda_i-lambda_j) term never appears. The key ingredient is a mean-eigenvalue pre-normalization that centres the spectrum near 1, away from the singularity of log, with a scalar post-compensation that returns the singular part of log(A) in closed form. Our recommended normalizer is a degree-8 Chebyshev expansion evaluated by a three-term matrix recurrence, with a matching reverse recurrence for the backward pass; Legendre, Laguerre, Taylor and Pade expansions are studied as controls that isolate the roles of the basis and of the target function. On three fine-grained benchmarks and ImageNet-1k the decomposition-free logarithm is both faster and more accurate than the spectral logarithm and than the square-root approximations it replaces, and at matched basis and degree the log target beats the square-root target, confirming that the gain comes from the faithful Riemannian map rather than from a better polynomial family.

</details>


### [75] [Counterfactual Contrastive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19032)
*Yunlong He,Pietro Gori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于对比分析（CA）的分类器无关视觉反事实解释（VCE）方法，通过从数据分布而非决策边界出发，解耦两个不同类别数据集的共同生成因子与各自显著因子，仅交换显著因子来生成反事实图像，避免了传统VCE方法对分类器偏见和失败模式的敏感性，并在三个医学影像数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VCE方法本质上依赖分类器，因此容易受到分类器偏见（如捷径特征敏感性）和校准误差等失败模式的影响，导致生成的反事实解释不可靠。

Method: 基于StyleGAN2的高质量合成能力和F空间（而非通常的W空间）以改善细节保留；将传统对比分析框架扩展为支持每个数据集中多个显著因子的新框架和损失函数，通过仅交换显著因子生成反事实图像。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有VCE方法具有更优的反事实生成质量。

Conclusion: 通过直接在数据分布上操作而非依赖于分类器决策边界，所提出的方法提供了模型无关的VCE，对分类器偏见具有更低的敏感性，为医学影像等领域的可解释性提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Visual Counterfactual Explanations (VCEs) aim to explain image classifiers by generating minimally edited and realistic versions of an input image that change the classifier's prediction. Existing VCE methods are inherently classifier-dependent and therefore susceptible to classifier biases and failure modes, such as sensitivity to shortcut features and calibration errors. In this paper, we propose a classifier-free approach for visual counterfactual generation based on Contrastive Analysis (CA). Given two datasets corresponding to different classes (e.g., healthy and patients), we disentangle the generative factors that are common across the two datasets from those that are salient to each dataset, and generate counterfactual images by swapping only the salient factors. By operating directly on data distributions rather than decision boundaries, our method provides model-agnostic VCEs that are less sensitive to classifier biases. Our approach leverages the high-quality synthesis and well-structured latent space of StyleGAN2. We use the feature space F, instead than the usual W-space, to improve detail preservation. Unlike conventional CA approaches, which typically assume salient factors in only one dataset, we introduce an adapted framework and loss functions for VCE that allow multiple salient factors in each dataset. We evaluate our method on three medical imaging datasets and demonstrate superior counterfactual generation quality compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [76] [USR-Drive: Unified Driving Scene Representation via Joint Denoising of 3D Gaussians and Boxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19036)
*Li-Heng Chen,Haokai Pang,Chengye Su,Jiarun Liu,Qifeng Chen,Ziqian Ni,Jianxin Huang,Shi-Sheng Huang,Hongbo Fu,Sheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: USR-Drive是一个统一的条件生成框架，通过多模态扩散Transformer将密集3D高斯原语和稀疏3D边界框作为对齐的潜在token流联合去噪，在共享场景表示中同时恢复动态几何和实例级物体布局，在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上实现了动态重建和3D检测的最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶空间表示学习方法将动态重建和实例级感知视为两个独立任务，尽管它们共享估计底层3D世界状态的目标。这导致动态重建缺乏约束（under-constrained），而3D检测缺乏几何基础（lacking geometric grounding）。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，统一这两个互补的任务。

Method: USR-Drive采用统一的概率条件生成框架：1) 将密集3D高斯原语和稀疏3D边界框表示为两个对齐的潜在token流；2) 使用统一的多模态扩散Transformer对两者进行联合去噪；3) 提出统一位置编码（UPE）在共享度量时空坐标中对齐异构token，使两种模态互相约束而非将盒子作为外部条件或独立模块预测。

Result: 在nuScenes和VKitti数据集上，USR-Drive在动态重建和3D检测两个任务上均取得了最先进的结果。两种模态相互增强：几何提供密集度量证据辅助边界框预测，边界框提供实例级结构先验帮助保持空间一致性并减少3D几何表示中的歧义。

Conclusion: 通过统一表示和生成式框架，动态重建与实例级感知能够从分离任务转变为相互约束、协同增强的联合任务，为自动驾驶空间表示学习提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Spatial representation learning for autonomous driving aims to map raw visual signals into structured 3D scene representations, where object-centric bounding boxes and rendering-oriented 3D primitives (\eg, 3D Gaussians) serve as two distinct yet highly complementary levels for scene understanding. Existing methods typically treat dynamic reconstruction and instance-level perception as separate tasks, despite their shared goal of estimating the underlying 3D world state. As a result, dynamic reconstruction is under-constrained while 3D detection lacks geometric grounding. To address this gap, we propose USR-Drive, a unified conditional generative framework that, given only posed multi-view driving videos, jointly recovers dense dynamic geometry and instance-level object layouts within a shared scene representation. Specifically, USR-Drive represents dense Gaussian primitives and sparse 3D bounding boxes as two aligned latent token streams and jointly denoises them with a unified multi-modal diffusion Transformer. Unlike prior paradigms that use boxes as external conditions or predict them with detached modules, USR-Drive treats them as mutually constrained state variables with a Unified Positional Encoding (UPE) that aligns heterogeneous tokens within a shared metric spatiotemporal coordinate. Via such unified representation and generative framework, the two modalities reinforce each other: geometry supplies dense metric evidence for box prediction, while boxes provide instance-level structural priors that help preserve spatial consistency and reduce ambiguity in sequential 3D geometric representation. Our approach successfully delivers state-of-the-art results for both dynamic reconstruction and 3D detection on the nuScenes and VKitti datasets.

</details>


### [77] [When Two Tracers Disagree: An Investigation of Multimodal Fusion for Clinical PET/CT Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19063)
*Jack A. Johnson,Bartłomiej W. Papież*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了PSMA和FDG PET/CT双示踪剂多模态融合策略用于全身病灶分割的效果，发现示踪剂专用nnU-Net基线表现最强（PSMA Dice=0.93, FDG=0.81），而各类融合架构（早期融合OEOD/OETD和中间融合DECA-UNet）均未能在PSMA/FDG联合任务上持续超越单示踪剂基线，且融合常导致FDG性能下降，提示现有融合架构难以保留示踪剂特异性表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前列腺癌中PSMA与FDG PET/CT提供互补的生物学信息，两者结合可捕获单一示踪剂可能遗漏的异质性肿瘤表型，但缺乏有效的深度学习融合架构共识，因此需系统评估不同多模态融合策略的可行性。

Method: 基于公开DEEP-PSMA Challenge数据集，训练示踪剂专用的3D nnU-Net基线模型，并比较两种融合策略：(i)早期融合，采用单编码器单解码器(OEOD)或单编码器双解码器(OETD)；(ii)中间融合，采用双编码器交叉注意力U-Net(DECA-UNet)。

Result: 示踪剂专用基线表现优异（PSMA Dice=0.93; FDG=0.81）。融合结果差异较大：OEOD在较简单非示踪剂特定任务上取得联合Dice=0.90；示踪剂特定融合模型上OETD达到PSMA/FDG=0.69/0.64，DECA-UNet为0.76/0.57。融合虽能较好分割PSMA病灶，但FDG性能普遍下降，无任何策略能持续超越单示踪剂基线。

Conclusion: 在所评估的设置下，示踪剂专用模型仍是更强的基线方案；临床应用上有价值的多模态融合收益大概率需要设计能更好保留示踪剂特异性表征的全新架构来实现。

Abstract: PSMA and FDG PET/CT visualise complementary biological information in prostate cancer. Combining both tracers could capture heterogeneous tumour phenotypes that may be missed by either alone, yet there is no consensus on effective deep learning architectures for fusing these modalities. We evaluated multimodal image-fusion strategies for automatic whole-body PET/CT lesion segmentation to estimate total tumour burden. Using the public DEEP-PSMA Challenge dataset, we trained tracer-specific 3D nnU-Net baselines and compared (i) early fusion with a single encoder and one decoder (OEOD) or two decoders (OETD), and (ii) intermediate fusion via a dual-encoder cross-attention U-Net (DECA-UNet). Tracer-specific baselines performed strongly (PSMA Dice = 0.93; FDG = 0.81). Fusion yielded mixed results: OEOD produced a combined Dice of 0.90 (on an easier, non-tracer-specific task), whilst the tracer-specific fusion models reached PSMA/FDG = 0.69/0.64 (OETD) and 0.76/0.57 (DECA-UNet). Whilst fusion often provided reasonable PSMA segmentation, FDG performance degraded and no strategy consistently exceeded the single-tracer baselines. Under the evaluated setting, tracer-specific models remain the stronger baseline; clinically useful gains from multimodal fusion will likely require architectures that better preserve tracer specific representations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/JackJ3636/DEEP_PSMA_code

</details>


### [78] [GS-VLA: Plug-and-Play Viewpoint Canonicalization for Frozen VLA Policies via Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19066)
*Yechan Park,HyunJin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-VLA，一个轻量级即插即用框架，通过在冻结的VLA策略前添加仅4M参数的3D高斯规范化器，在不重新训练策略的情况下增强其对视角偏移的鲁棒性。实验表明，即使相机安装位置发生小位移，LIBERO基准上的成功率可从约90%骤降至约10%，而GS-VLA能在多个策略架构、未见任务套件和扰动尺度下恢复大部分性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA策略隐式假设训练与部署时相机配置相同，但实际中相机安装位置的小偏移即可导致性能大幅下降（成功率从90%降至10%）。现有方案如大规模微调或生成式数据增强计算成本高昂且存在灾难性遗忘风险，因此需要一种无需重训策略的轻量级视角鲁棒性增强方法。

Method: 将视角偏移问题重构为局部新视角合成问题。在"局部性假设"（相机扰动相对工作空间保持在小有界区域内）下，视角归一化可简化为与场景和策略无关的去遮挡任务。具体实现是在冻结的VLA策略前添加一个4M参数的3D高斯规范化器（3D-Gaussian canonicalizer），作为即插即用的视觉模块。

Result: GS-VLA在三个正交维度上均提升了性能：（1）跨不同策略架构、（2）跨未见任务套件、（3）跨不同扰动尺度。该方法不修改任何策略权重，但能恢复视角偏移下大部分丢失的性能。

Conclusion: 轻量级视觉模块无需策略重训练即可显著恢复视角偏移下的性能损失，证明了3D高斯新视角合成用于VLA观测空间自适应的有效性和可行性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight, plug-and-play framework that improves robustness to viewpoint shifts in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies without policy retraining. To our knowledge, this is the first approach to directly leverage 3D Gaussian-based novel-view synthesis for observation-space adaptation in VLA policies. Current VLA performance relies on the implicit assumption that training and deployment camera configurations are identical. Our experiments show that even a small displacement of the camera mount can reduce the success rate on the LIBERO benchmark from about 90% to about 10% in the worst case. Prior approaches, such as large-scale fine-tuning or generative data augmentation, are computationally expensive and risk catastrophic forgetting. To address this, viewpoint shifts are reformulated as a localized novel-view synthesis problem. Under a Locality assumption, that camera perturbations remain within a small bounded region relative to the workspace, viewpoint normalization reduces to a scene- and policy-independent disocclusion task. Our work implements this idea with a 4M-parameter 3D-Gaussian canonicalizer prepended to a frozen VLA policy. Without modifying policy weights, GS-VLA improves performance across three orthogonal axes: (1) Policy architectures, (2) Unseen task suites, and (3) Perturbation scales. These results show that a lightweight visual module can recover a large fraction of the performance lost under viewpoint shift, without policy retraining.

</details>


### [79] [ReWEIGH the Evidence: Calibrating Token-Level Ordinal Visual Evidence to Mitigate Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19075)
*Jihae Jeong,Junha Choi,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReWEIGH是一种无需训练的推理干预方法，通过聚合视觉位置上的词汇排名证据并与无标注图像估计的token特定参考值比较，在解码时对低于参考的候选token施加有界惩罚，从而减少LVLM的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)经常产生幻觉，生成输入图像不支持的内容。现有的解码方法需要一种候选特定的度量来衡量图像对该token的支持强度，而现有的基于视觉token状态的证据聚合方法存在概率量级不可比和token间固有差异等问题。

Method: ReWEIGH利用模型视觉token状态通过输出头投影得到的词汇排名作为尺度不变的聚合基础，并针对每个token用无标注图像估计一个参考值。推理时在prefill阶段缓存图像证据，对低于参考阈值的候选token施加有界惩罚。

Result: 在四个7B骨干模型上，幻觉物体提及最多减少21.3%，同时保留或提升描述性和通用性能。缓存证据后平均每token延迟增加仅1.33%，且效果扩展到六个架构族至32B参数规模。

Conclusion: ReWEIGH通过token特定的参考基准解决了词汇排名证据的系统性差异问题，实现了训练免费、低延迟、跨架构可迁移的幻觉抑制效果。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) often hallucinate, generating content that the input image does not support. Preventing such content during decoding calls for a candidate-specific measure of how strongly the image supports the token under consideration. The model's visual-token states offer a natural source of this evidence because projecting each state through the output head reveals which vocabulary items that position favors. These position-wise readouts cannot be pooled directly because their probability magnitudes are not comparable across visual positions. Vocabulary ranks provide a scale-invariant basis for pooling, but tokens still differ systematically in their typical rank-based evidence. We propose ReWEIGH, a training-free decoding intervention that aggregates these ranks across visual positions and compares each candidate with a token-specific reference estimated from unlabeled images. At inference, ReWEIGH caches the image evidence during prefill and applies a bounded penalty only to candidates that fall below their reference. On four 7B backbones, ReWEIGH reduces hallucinated object mentions by up to 21.3% while largely preserving or improving descriptive and general performance. With evidence cached, the average added latency is 1.33% per token, and the reductions extend across six architecture families to 32B parameters.

</details>


### [80] [Subgroup performance analysis of adaptation strategies for chest X-ray foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19078)
*Dhruv Gupta,Emma A. M. Stanley,Fabio De Sousa Ribeiro,Sujal Desai,Ben Glocker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种参数高效适配方法（线性头、MLP、注意力池化）应用于冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器时，对病理分类性能和子群公平性的影响。发现注意力池化表现最优但也最强编码种族属性，但更强的属性编码并不对应更大的子群差异，公平性影响因任务而异且不可预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型越来越多地被适配用于下游医学成像任务，但适配策略的选择如何影响子群公平性（subgroup fairness）仍然知之甚少。理解不同适配方法对公平性的影响对于确保医学AI系统的公平可靠至关重要。

Method: 在冻结的Rad-DINO胸部X光编码器上应用三种参数高效适配技术：原始CLS token上的线性头、MLP、以及多层级patch特征的注意力池化模块。使用MIMIC-CXR数据集，在保持患病率、人口统计学平衡的测试集上评估八个病理类别，按种族、性别和影像视角子群进行评估，并探测每个适配器编码受保护属性的强度。此外，探索了注意力池化的不同层组合。

Result: 注意力池化实现了最强的整体判别性能，且最强地编码了属性（尤其是种族），但整体性能的提升并未持续一致地减少子群差异。更强的属性编码并未对应更大的差异：早期网络层编码种族最弱却产生了最大的子群性能差距。探索不同的注意力池化层组合也未发现池化层、属性编码强度与子群公平性之间的一致关系。

Conclusion: 更丰富、更具表达力的表示可以在提升准确性的同时，使公平性影响保持任务依赖性和不可预测性。公平性必须直接且按任务进行评估，而不能仅从编码强度或整体性能中推断得出。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly adapted for downstream medical imaging tasks, yet the influence of the chosen adaptation strategy on subgroup fairness remains poorly understood. We investigate how three parameter-efficient adaptation techniques, including linear heads on the raw CLS token, an MLP, and an attention-pooling module over multi-layer patch features, affect both pathology classification performance and subgroup disparities when applied to the frozen Rad-DINO chest X-ray encoder. Using MIMIC-CXR, we evaluate eight pathologies across race, sex, and imaging-view subgroups on a prevalence-preserving, demographically balanced test set, and additionally probe how strongly each adapter encodes protected attributes. We find that attention pooling achieves the strongest overall discriminative performance and encodes attributes, particularly race, most strongly, but that improved overall performance does not consistently reduce subgroup disparities. Notably, stronger attribute encoding did not correspond to larger disparities: early network layers encoded race most weakly yet produced the largest subgroup performance gaps. Exploring different attention-pooling layer combinations further revealed no consistent relationship between the layers pooled, attribute encoding strength, and subgroup fairness. Our results indicate that richer, more expressive representations can improve accuracy while leaving fairness implications task-dependent and unpredictable, which must be assessed directly and per-task rather than inferred from encoding strength or overall performance alone.

</details>


### [81] [SPK: Eliciting Structured Prior Knowledge for Interpretable Out-of-Distribution Detection in Real-Time Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19080)
*Changshun Wu,Weicheng He,Xiaowei Huang,Saddek Bensalem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPK框架，显式地从预训练目标检测器中挖掘与OoD相关的潜在先验知识，将其组织为紧凑的五维结构化表示用于OoD检测，在多种检测器架构和基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标检测器对训练类别之外的物体常产生过度自信预测（OoD幻觉）。现有方法要么直接基于检测表示构造评分函数，要么修改检测器本身抑制幻觉，但检测器表示中隐式编码的潜在先验从未被显式解码用于OoD检测。

Method: 提出Structured Prior Knowledge (SPK)框架：利用分布内数据和幻觉诱导样本作为诊断监督，显式引出检测器决策背后的部件级语义概念（语义先验），并与几何先验和上下文先验集成，形成紧凑的五维SPK表示用于OoD检测。

Result: 在多种目标检测器架构和多个OoD基准上实现SOTA OoD检测性能，证明预训练检测器编码了远比通常利用的更为丰富的潜在知识，且这些知识可以被显式引出并组织为紧凑、结构化、可解释的知识空间。

Conclusion: 预训练目标检测器隐式编码了丰富的潜在先验知识，通过显式挖掘和结构化组织这些知识，可以走出一条提升检测器可靠性的主动式路径（proactive route），而非仅被动拒绝或适配。

Abstract: Object detectors often produce over-confident predictions for objects outside their training categories, leading to so-called out-of-distribution (OoD) hallucinations. Existing approaches for detecting or mitigating such hallucinations typically either construct scoring functions directly over learned object detector representations or modify the object detector itself to suppress hallucination emergence. However, the latent priors implicitly encoded in these representations remain largely unexplored and have not been explicitly decoded for OoD detection. To uncover and exploit these latent priors, we propose Structured Prior Knowledge (SPK), a hallucination-oriented framework that explicitly elicits OoD-relevant priors from pretrained object detectors. Specifically, SPK leverages in-distribution data and hallucination-inducing samples as diagnostic supervision to elicit part-level semantic concepts underlying object detector decision-making, rather than using them merely for rejection or object detector adaptation. The elicited semantic priors are further integrated with geometric and contextual priors to form a compact five-dimensional SPK representation for OoD detection. Extensive experiments across diverse object detector architectures and multiple OoD benchmarks demonstrate that SPK achieves state-of-the-art OoD detection. Our findings reveal that pretrained object detectors already encode substantially richer latent knowledge than is typically exploited for OoD detection. More importantly, this knowledge can be explicitly elicited and organized into a compact, structured, and interpretable knowledge space for prediction reliability analysis. This suggests a promising proactive route for improving object detector reliability by explicitly uncovering and leveraging latent priors. Code and data are available at: https://gricad-gitlab.univ-grenoble-alpes.fr/dnn-safety/spk

</details>


### [82] [Detecting Backdoors in Object Detection via Pre-NMS Prediction Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19088)
*Longtian Wang,Zhengyu Zhao,Chenhao Lin,Le Yang,Shiwei Wang,Yuhan Zhi,Xiaofei Xie,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DistScan是一种基于模型预NMS阶段类别预测分布偏移的后门检测框架，无需权重访问、触发器知识或额外训练，在场景级攻击下相比最优基线提升27.32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测模型的后门检测方法在场景级攻击下泛化能力差，且依赖触发器反演或架构特定假设，缺乏可靠通用的检测方案。

Method: 利用后门注入系统性偏移模型预NMS类别预测分布这一观察，在干净验证集上聚合中间类别预测，并与训练类别频率比较偏差来判定模型是否被后门攻击。

Result: 在MS-COCO和PASCAL VOC上，跨两种架构和三种场景级攻击场景，平均检测准确率比最佳适用基线提高27.32个百分点。

Conclusion: DistScan为场景级后门攻击提供了高效、通用且无需训练的后门检测方案，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Object detection models deployed in safety-critical applications remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks that cause targeted misbehaviors when a hidden trigger is present. Existing detection methods either rely on trigger inversion or exploit architecture-specific assumptions, and critically, representative existing methods fail to generalize reliably to scene-level attacks, where a single trigger induces anomalous behavior across all objects in the scene simultaneously. We present DistScan, a backdoor detection framework based on a simple but previously unexploited observation: backdoor injection systematically shifts a model's pre-NMS prediction class distribution away from its training class frequencies, even on clean inputs without any trigger present. DistScan aggregates intermediate class predictions over a clean validation set and flags a model as backdoored if the resulting distribution deviates significantly from the training class frequencies, requiring no model weight access, no trigger knowledge, and no additional training. Extensive experiments on MS-COCO and PASCAL VOC across two architectures and three scene-level attack scenarios demonstrate that DistScan substantially outperforms existing methods, improving average detection accuracy over the best-performing applicable baseline by 27.32 percentage points.

</details>


### [83] [Image-Guided Pavement Defect Recognition in GPR Data with novel 3D Deep Learning Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19177)
*Yuandong Pan,Linjun Lu,Mudan Wang,Florian Noichl,Fan Xue,Brian Sheil,Lavindra de Silva,André Borrmann,Ioannis Brilakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将正射RGB影像与3D探地雷达(GPR)数据对齐以自动生成标注数据集的方法，并设计了一种融合残差连接、混合卷积核及双重注意力机制的3D CNN架构，用于路面修补和裂缝缺陷的二分类检测，实验表明该方法在多个评估指标上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探地雷达是非破坏性地下检测技术，但在路面状况自动检测的大规模应用中面临两个关键挑战：标注的真实世界数据集稀缺，以及缺乏针对3D GPR数据独特特征设计的深度学习模型。

Method: 首先构建低成本数据准备流程，将正射RGB影像与3D GPR扫描对齐，以路面表面图像为参考将表面可见缺陷标签迁移至对应GPR段，实现高效大规模标注；其次提出结合残差连接、混合卷积核尺寸、深度注意力和通道注意力机制的3D CNN架构进行缺陷分类。

Result: 在运营中高速公路路段采集的真实世界数据集上进行修补和裂缝缺陷的二分类实验，所提网络在多个评估指标上优于基线架构；消融实验验证了各架构组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献了一种可扩展、实用的真实世界数据集生成方法，以及一个新颖的3D深度学习框架，为GPR在路面自动检测中的大规模应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Ground Penetrating Radar (GPR) is a widely adopted non-destructive sensing technology for subsurface inspection in civil and transportation engineering. Despite its potential for pavement condition assessment, the large-scale application of GPR in automated inspection has two key challenges: the scarcity of annotated real-world datasets and the lack of deep learning models designed for the unique characteristics of 3-Dimensional (3D) GPR data. This study addresses these limitations by firstly introducing a cost-effective data preparation pipeline that integrates orthomosaic Red Green Blue (RGB) imagery with 3D GPR scans to generate annotated 3D GPR datasets. The proposed method uses the aligned segments of RGB and GPR data, using pavement surface images as a reference to transfer labels of surface-visible defects to corresponding GPR segments, enabling efficient large-scale annotation in a real-world dataset collected on a highway section under operation. In addition to the dataset contribution, we propose a specialised 3D Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture incorporating residual connections, mixed convolutional kernel sizes, and both depthwise and channelwise attention mechanisms to enhance feature representation and defect classification. The model is evaluated on binary classification tasks for detecting patch and crack defects in pavement structures. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed network outperforms baseline architectures across multiple evaluation metrics. Ablation studies further confirm the effectiveness of the designed architectural components. This work contributes a scalable and practical method for real-world dataset generation, along with a novel deep learning framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [LongNovel: A Multi-Scale Benchmark for Hallucination Detection in Long-Context Novel Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18082)
*Ruizhi Zhang,Jinwei Chen,Xiangju Lu,He Yan,Mo Yu,Junmin Zhu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了LongNovel，一个中英双语多尺度长上下文小说摘要幻觉检测基准，包含29本中文小说（16k-100k tokens）和BookSum章节级数据，设计了8种幻觉类型，并通过多模型仲裁和实体参照幻觉生成确保数据真实性与类别平衡，实验证明其具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏面向长上下文小说摘要的多尺度幻觉检测基准，且未充分探索随着上下文长度增长幻觉如何变化。长篇小说比新闻或论文更适合研究幻觉，因其具有更密集的信息量和详细的事件与对话描述。

Method: 构建包含29本中文小说（16k-100k tokens）及BookSum数据集章节级数据的双语基准；设计8种幻觉类型；采用多模型仲裁（Multi-Model Arbitration）与实体参照幻觉生成（Entity-Referenced Hallucination Generation）相结合的方法，确保数据真实性与幻觉类别分布平衡；对测试集内容进行人工修订以保证可靠性。

Result: 大量实验表明LongNovel是一个具有挑战性的基准，能够有效评估长上下文摘要中的幻觉检测性能。

Conclusion: LongNovel为长上下文小说摘要的幻觉检测提供了一个多尺度、双语的基准资源，已公开发布供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Although context windows have expanded significantly in recent years, hallucinations in long-context summarization remain a challenge. Long novels are better suited than news or papers for researching these hallucinations, due to their intrinsic information and detailed descriptions of events and dialogues. However, current research lacks a multi-scale benchmark for hallucination detection in long-context novel summarization and does not fully explore how hallucinations change as the context grows longer. In this study, we propose LongNovel, a multi-scale long-context bilingual (Chinese and English) novel benchmark for hallucination detection. This benchmark is constructed from 29 Chinese novels (ranging from 16k to 100k tokens) and chapter-level data from the BookSum dataset. We design 8 hallucination types and employ a combination of Multi-Model Arbitration and Entity-Referenced Hallucination Generation to ensure both data authenticity and a balanced distribution of hallucination categories. Furthermore, we manually revise the content in the test set to guarantee data reliability. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LongNovel is a challenging benchmark. We release LongNovel for future research. https://github.com/BDML-lab/LongNovel

</details>


### [85] [Entity tracking emerges in sub-billion parameter language models and exceeds human performance in naturalistic narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18083)
*Karolina Drożdż,Micha Heilbron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过自然叙事语料（而非人工构造任务）比较了语言模型与人类（N=48）在多层级复杂性条件下的实体追踪能力。研究发现：人类实体追踪能力随叙事复杂性（而非长度）而退化；语言模型早在4.1亿参数时即达到人类水平（远低于先前研究指出的数十亿参数规模），并随规模扩大持续提升，当代表现远超人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体追踪评测依赖人工构造的伪任务（artificial tasks），脱离自然语言理解场景，且缺乏与人类表现的直接对比，导致对语言模型是否以类人方式追踪实体这一问题尚无定论。

Method: 使用多层级复杂度的自然叙事语料（naturalistic narratives），对语言模型和人类受试者（N=48）进行实体追踪能力的系统评测与比较。

Result: （1）人类实体追踪能力随叙事复杂性增加而退化，但不受叙事长度影响；（2）语言模型在4.1亿参数规模即已具备人类水平的实体追踪能力；（3）实体追踪性能随模型规模扩大而持续提升，当代大型模型远超人类水平。

Conclusion: 实体追踪作为语言理解的核心组成部分，在远小于先前认知的模型规模下即可涌现（emerge），挑战了此前认为需要数十亿参数或代码专用模型的结论。

Abstract: Understanding language requires tracking entities across discourse - i.e., knowing where things are and how they change, even when not explicitly stated. Whether language models perform such tracking in a human-like fashion remains unclear, in part because existing evaluations rely on artificial tasks, far removed from natural language comprehension, and lack comparisons to humans. Here, we evaluate entity tracking in both language models and humans (N = 48) using naturalistic narratives at multiple levels of complexity. In humans, we find that entity tracking degrades specifically with narrative complexity, not narrative length. In language models, we find that human-level entity tracking is already present at 410 million parameters - well below the multi-billion parameter, code-specialised models identified by prior work - and improves with scale, with contemporary models far exceeding human performance. Together, these results demonstrate that entity tracking, a core component of language understanding, emerges at model scales far smaller than previously thought.

</details>


### [86] [Compiler-Guided Adaptive Proof Search with Cross-Model Synergy on Context-Dependent Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18084)
*Zhuo Liu,Ding Yu,Hangfeng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种编译器引导的证明搜索框架，通过双模型生成和停滞触发重采样来平衡探索与利用，在七个真实Lean 4项目上相比pass@k基线在同等预算下提升12.8个百分点的平均通过率，同时减少21.9%的LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的Lean 4项目中，定理证明往往依赖项目特定上下文，而现有的迭代精炼方法利用编译器错误修复失败证明时，缺乏对失败尝试的搜索控制：不同证明起点的质量差异大，且后续修订可能破坏部分正确的证明。

Method: 提出编译器引导的证明搜索框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 探索方面，通过双模型生成多样化的起始点，并在搜索停滞时触发重采样以跳出局部最优；(2) 利用方面，通过编译器接地(wgrounded)的成对比较选择当前最优证明状态进行精炼，确保每次修订都朝改进方向推进。

Result: 在miniCTX-v2中的七个真实Lean 4项目上，相比pass@k基线，在pass@32预算下平均通过率提升12.8个百分点，同时LLM调用次数减少21.9%，实现了更好的效果-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 双模型生成与停滞触发重采样能有效保持探索多样性，而编译器引导的成对比较精炼策略能防止部分正确证明被后续修订退化，整体框架在真实项目场景下优于传统的pass@k采样基线。

Abstract: Theorem proving in real-world Lean 4 projects is challenging because proofs often depend on project-specific context. While iterative refinement can use compiler errors to repair failed proofs, reusing failed attempts requires careful search control: some proofs provide better starting points than others, and later revisions may degrade a partially correct proof. We propose a compiler-guided proof search framework that balances exploration and exploitation. It explores diverse starting points through dual-model generation and stagnation-triggered resampling, while exploiting promising proof states through current-best refinement guided by compiler-grounded pairwise comparison. Experiments on seven real-world Lean 4 projects from miniCTX-v2 show that our method achieves a better effectiveness--efficiency tradeoff than pass@k baselines. Within the pass@32 budget, our method improves average pass rate by 12.8 percentage points while reducing LLM calls by 21.9%.

</details>


### [87] [Persona-Guided LLM Agents for Task-Oriented Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18085)
*Maryam Shoaeinaeini,Brent Harrison,A. B. Siddique*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨任务导向对话（TOD）中LLM能否在保持任务完成的同时表达人格特质，以及适应用户人格是否提升交互质量。作者构建了无需训练的双LLM框架，在三种知识条件下（Neutral/Try/Oracle）评估系统对用户人格的适应性，发现适应性可提升约束满足、信息率和用户满意度，但会降低真实性，而基于对话线索推理的Try条件最平衡地解决了这一权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明LLM能在开放式文本生成中表达多样化人格特质，但尚不清楚在需要完成具体目标的多轮对话中是否仍能做到而不损害任务完成度，也不确定适应用户人格是否真正改善交互质量。这些问题在任务导向对话中具有重要实际意义。

Method: 构建无需训练（training-free）的框架，模拟两个LLM之间的TOD交互：一个用户代理展示目标人格（基于Big Five特质及其对立面），一个系统代理在完成任务的同时适应用户。通过三种条件隔离适应效果：Neutral（无人格信息）、Try（从对话线索推断）、Oracle（显式给定人格）。在SGD数据集的Hotel和Restaurant对话上评估三个模型（GPT-4o、Qwen3-Next-80B、Gemini 2.0 Flash）。

Result: 用户代理能表达人格且系统保持强任务表现，但部分特质实现可靠性较差。适应用户人格提升约束满足、信息率和用户满意度，但降低真实性，揭示了个性化与任务接地之间的权衡。Oracle的优势在目标特质表达强烈时增大，而Try的收益对实现强度不敏感。Try条件在解决这一权衡上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 基于对话线索推断人格的适应性（Try条件）为无需微调的人格感知TOD提供了更可靠的路径，在个性化与任务接地之间取得了最佳平衡。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can express diverse personality traits in open-ended text generation. However, it remains unclear whether they can do so in a goal-directed dialogue without compromising task completion, and whether adapting to the user's personality improves the interaction quality. We study these questions in task-oriented dialogue (TOD), where a system helps a user accomplish a goal via multi-turn interaction. We build a training-free framework that simulates a TOD interaction between two LLMs: a user agent that exhibits a target personality and a system agent that adapts to the user while completing the task. To isolate the effect of adaptation, we vary how much the system knows about the user's personality across three conditions. In Neutral, the system receives no personality information. In Try, it infers the personality from dialogue cues. In Oracle, it is given the personality explicitly. We evaluate GPT-4o, Qwen3-Next-80B, and Gemini 2.0 Flash on Hotel and Restaurant dialogues from the Schema-Guided Dialogue (SGD) dataset, across the Big Five traits and their opposite poles. We find that the user agent can express personality while the system maintains strong task performance, although some traits are realized far less reliably than others. Adapting to the user's personality improves constraint satisfaction, inform rate, and user satisfaction, but lowers truthfulness, revealing a trade-off between personalization and task-grounding. Oracle's gains grow when the target trait is strongly expressed, whereas Try's gains are largely insensitive to realization strength. Overall, cue-based adaptation in Try best resolves this trade-off and offers a more reliable route to personality-aware TOD without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [88] [SuTRA : Structurally-Unified Tokenization with Root Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18087)
*Vaibhav Rathore,Siddhant Gole,Dadhichi Telwadkar,Rooshil Bhatia,Maulik Ruparel,Siddharth Surekha,Neha Bhargava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SuTRA算法，一种面向印地语、马拉地语和古吉拉特语等形态丰富印度语言的形态感知子词分词器，通过保留akshara完整性并惩罚跨形态边界的合并，减少"形态碎片化"现象，相比BPE在形态对齐和机器翻译上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的子词分词器（如BPE）仅优化统计压缩而忽略语言形态结构，尤其是词根与词缀之间的关系。对形态丰富的印度语言而言，其基本单位是复杂正字法音节（aksharas）而非字母，基于频率的方法会导致过度切分，任意拆分词根和词缀，造成"形态碎片化"（Morphological Shattering）问题。

Method: 提出SuTRA（Structurally-Unified Tokenization with Root Awareness），一种形态感知的分词算法。该算法保证akshara的不可分割性，并引入对跨形态边界合并的惩罚机制，从而在分词过程中保持词根-词缀的结构完整性。同时发布了印地语、马拉地语和古吉拉特语的形态分割数据集。

Result: SuTRA相较于BPE在形态对齐（Boundary F1）上最高提升+14.7%，在语义可恢复性（印地语）上提升+34%，且这些结构性的改进在机器翻译任务中带来了平均+8.08 chrF2的提升。

Conclusion: SuTRA证明形态感知的分词策略能够有效缓解形态丰富语言中的碎片化问题，在保持结构合理性的同时提升下游任务（如机器翻译）的性能，为形态丰富语言的子词分词提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Existing subword tokenizers optimize statistical compression but ignore morphological structure, particularly the relationship between roots and affixes. This is harmful for morphologically rich Indic languages, where basic units are complex orthographic syllables (aksharas) rather than letters. Frequency-based methods over-fragment words, arbitrarily splitting roots and affixes - a phenomenon we term Morphological Shattering. We propose SuTRA (Structurally-Unified Tokenization with Root Awareness), a morphology-aware algorithm that preserves akshara indivisibility and penalizes merges crossing morphological boundaries. We also release a new morphological segmentation dataset for Hindi, Marathi, and Gujarati. SuTRA reduces shattering, achieving peak gains of +14.7% in morphological alignment (Boundary F1) and +34% in semantic recoverability (Hindi) over BPE. These structural gains yield an average improvement of +8.08 chrF2 in machine translation.

</details>


### [89] [Latent Space Refusal Anchoring for Low-Resource African Languages: Mechanistic Safety Recovery Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18089)
*Godwin Abuh Faruna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的"隐空间拒绝锚定"(LSR-Anchoring)方法，通过从英语提示中提取拒绝方向并在推理时将钳制到残差流上，恢复低资源非洲语言(Yoruba、Igbo、Igala、Hausa)的指令微调模型的拒绝行为，无需标注数据或重新训练。主要变体Mean-Activation Steering (MAS)在四种架构上测试，其中SAE-Derived Steering (SDS)解决了Llama-3-8B的过度修正问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型对英语中的有害请求会拒绝，但对Yoruba、Igbo、Igala和Hausa等低资源非洲语言的相同请求却会遵从，表明拒绝机制存在于残差流中但未能对低资源输入激活。传统的修复方法需要标注的目标语言数据和重新训练，而这对大多数非洲语言在规模上不可行，因此需要一种无需训练的方法来恢复拒绝行为。

Method: 提出Latent Space Refusal Anchoring (LSR-Anchoring)方法，从英语提示中提取拒绝方向并在推理时将钳制到残差流上。包含两个变体：(1) Mean-Activation Steering (MAS)，使用密集均值差分方向，在Llama-3-8B、Llama-3.1-70B、Mistral-7B-Instruct和Qwen2.5-7B四种架构上测试；(2) SAE-Derived Steering (SDS)，用单个稀疏自动编码器(SAE)特征替换密集均值差分方向，以解决MAS在Llama-3-8B上的过度修正问题。

Result: MAS在Mistral和Qwen上恢复了安全性，良性降级低于0.08；在Llama-3-8B上过度修正，DPL达到1.00。SDS将KL散度降低了3.5-7倍而不会导致良性崩溃。四种非洲语言正向迁移，但阿拉伯语在所有架构和所有引导幅度下均失败，表明存在几何失配。MMLU准确率在所有有效引导幅度下的下降均低于0.35个百分点。

Conclusion: LSR-Anchoring是一种有效的无需训练的跨语言安全恢复方法，能够在缺乏标注数据的情况下恢复低资源非洲语言的拒绝行为。SAE变体在解决过度修正问题上表现优异。阿拉伯语的系统失败揭示了跨语言拒绝方向存在几何失配，为未来工作提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned models often refuse harmful requests in English but comply with the same requests in Yoruba, Igbo, Igala, and Hausa. This suggests that the refusal mechanism is present in the residual stream but fails to activate for low-resource inputs. Recovering it normally requires labelled target-language data and retraining, neither of which is available at scale for most African languages. We introduce Latent Space Refusal Anchoring (LSR-Anchoring), a training-free method that extracts the refusal direction from English prompts and clamps it onto the residual stream at inference time. The primary variant, Mean-Activation Steering (MAS), operates across the four architectures we tested: Llama-3-8B, Llama-3.1-70B, Mistral-7B-Instruct, and Qwen2.5-7B. On Mistral and Qwen it recovers safety with benign degradation below 0.08. On Llama-3-8B it overcorrects, with Degraded Performance on Legitimate prompts (DPL) reaching 1.00. We address this with SAE-Derived Steering (SDS), which replaces the dense mean-difference direction with a single Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) feature and reduces Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence by 3.5-7x without benign collapse. Four languages transfer positively, but Arabic fails on every architecture and at every steering magnitude, indicating a geometric mismatch rather than a baseline effect. Massive Multitask Language Understanding (MMLU) accuracy drops remain below 0.35 percentage points at every effective steering magnitude.

</details>


### [90] [Nine Emotion Centroids: A Label-Free Valence Axis That Transfers Across Four Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18090)
*Yousef Radwan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种极简的"情绪价值轴（V-axis）"提取方法：仅用9个情绪类别名和每个类别50个短叙事段落，通过冻结编码器中对九类情绪锚定故事嵌入取平均后的主方向，即可在文本、视觉、音频和人类大脑编码器中发现同一条通用情绪效价方向。该方向无需大量监督标签（比常规方法少约1500个标签），在SST-2上达到监督性能的93%（AUC 0.772 vs 0.828），与11,811张EmoSet图像的人类效价评分相关r=0.636，并在音频和EEG数据上表现显著。机制上，删除该方向会使三类LLM的情感准确率下降5.5-37.2个百分点，远超随机方向（z>12）。该方法仅适用于连续属性（对类别概念无效），且模型家族特定的引导效果仅在Llama/Mistral上成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在解决现有情感维度提取依赖大量人工标注（需要约1500个标签）且难以跨模态泛化的问题。他们希望找到一种无需监督训练、仅依赖极少量语义锚点就能提取通用情绪维度的极简方法，并验证该维度是否在多种编码器（文本、视觉、音频、大脑）中共享。

Method: 方法包括三个部分：(1) V-axis提取：在冻结编码器中嵌入9个情绪类别（每个类别50个短叙事段落），计算九个平均嵌入后取第一主成分方向；(2) 跨模态验证：在SST-2（文本）、EmoSet（图像）、ESC-50（音频）和EEG（大脑）上测试该方向的一致性；(3) 机制分析：通过消融实验删除V-axis检验其对情感准确率的影响，并使用2参数分类器进行跨模态迁移测试，同时验证方法仅适用于连续属性（对类别概念为偶然水平）且引导效果具有模型家族特异性。

Result: 结果显示：(1) V-axis在文本上达到监督性能93%（SST-2 AUC 0.772 vs 0.828）；(2) 图像相关的效价相关性r=0.636（11,811张图）；(3) 音频AUC 0.906（p<2.2e-15）；(4) EEG AUC 0.720±0.055（123名受试者，p<3.65e-8）；(5) 消融实验证明该方向机制活跃，删除后准确率下降5.5-37.2个百分点，随机方向最多仅0.88个百分点（z>12）；(6) 2参数分类器从文本标签跨模态迁移成功（图像0.961、音频0.764、脑记录0.828），而16维通用子空间仅0.525；(7) 7次类别概念测试均为偶然水平，表明该方法仅适用于连续属性；(8) 模型家族特异性：Llama/Mistral可引导，Qwen/Gemma不可。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：只需9个情绪词和450个短故事即可从任意冻结编码器中提取出一条通用的情绪效价方向，该方向跨模态（文本/视觉/音频/大脑）共享且机制上因果有效。这一极简配方揭示了情感维度是现代编码器的基本连续属性，而非特定模态的专门训练产物，同时指出该方法边界（仅限连续属性、模型家族特异性）为未来通用连续属性提取提供了新范式。

Abstract: Inside a modern language model sits a single internal direction that tracks how positive or negative a sentence feels. We show how to find this valence axis (V-axis) from just 9 emotion category names plus 50 short narrative paragraphs per emotion -- about 1,500 fewer labels than the usual supervised approach -- and that the same direction appears in vision, audio, and human-brain encoders never jointly trained. The recipe: embed nine emotion-anchored story sets in a frozen encoder, take the top principal direction of the nine averaged embeddings. Projecting new inputs onto it captures 93% of supervised performance on SST-2 (Llama-3-8B-Instruct, AUC 0.772 vs. 0.828), correlates with human valence ratings on 11,811 EmoSet images at r=0.636, reaches AUC 0.906 on ESC-50 audio (p<2.2e-15), and AUC 0.720+/-0.055 on EEG from 123 subjects (p<3.65e-8). The direction is mechanistically active: ablating it collapses sentiment accuracy by 5.5-37.2 pp across three LLMs vs. at most 0.88 pp for matched random directions (z>12). A 2-parameter classifier trained on text labels transfers to images (AUC 0.961), audio (0.764), and brain recordings (0.828) without target-modality labels; a generic 16-D subspace stays at chance (0.525). The recipe is bounded to continuous attributes -- seven tests on categorical concepts return near-chance -- and steering is family-specific (Llama/Mistral yes, Qwen/Gemma no).

</details>


### [91] [Self- and Other-Labels Induce Bidirectional Bias in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18091)
*Songeun Chae,Min Kim,Donghoon Jung,Seojin Choi,Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过让十个LLM评估"叙事约束选择"（而非生成文本）来分离自偏好与生成质量、文体特征的混淆效应。结果发现：在盲评条件下，控制质量和评分严酷度后自偏好基本消失（四个维度中三个消失、一个反转）；但在质量匹配条件下，仅仅贴上"自/他"标签就会双向偏移评分。作者提出两个贡献：作者归属是评估偏差的独立驱动因素；开放无真值任务可作为研究LLM评判行为的受控工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge系统日益普及，但LLM的自偏好（倾向偏爱自身输出）引发评估可靠性担忧。此前研究聚焦于生成文本，难以分离文体特征与响应质量造成的混淆，因此无法真正度量自偏好本身。

Method: 改变评估对象：以"叙事约束选择"替代生成文本作为评判材料（无文体指纹但保留可恢复的模型特定签名）。设计两个实验：(1) 盲评实验——控制选择质量与评估者严酷度后检验自偏好；(2) 质量匹配实验——在不命名模型的情况下仅对选择冠以自/他标签，检验标签对评分的影响。

Result: 盲评条件下，自偏好在三个评分维度上消失，在第四个维度（原创性）上发生反转——裁判给自己的选择评分反而更低。质量匹配条件下，自标签导致评分虚高，他标签导致评分压低，与实际来源无关，即标签本身产生了双向偏差。

Conclusion: 作者归属身份（标签）本身是独立于内容质量的评估偏差驱动因素；同时，开放式、无真值参考的任务可以作为受控实验手段来研究LLM评判行为的内在机制。

Abstract: As LLM-as-a-judge systems become increasingly widespread, self-preference in LLMs -- the tendency to favor one's own outputs -- raises growing concerns about evaluation reliability. However, it has been studied predominantly on generated text, where stylistic features and response quality are inevitably conflated. As a result, existing measurements cannot separate genuine self-preference from these confounds. We address this by changing the object of evaluation: instead of judging generated text, ten LLMs assess narrative constraint selections, which carry no model-specific stylistic fingerprint yet retain a recoverable model-specific signature. We run two experiments that yield distinct findings. Under blind evaluation, self-preference largely disappears once selection quality and evaluator severity are controlled. It vanishes on three of four rubric dimensions and reverses on the fourth, where judges rate their own selections as less original. Under matched quality, however, self- and other-labels alone -- without naming any model -- shift scores bidirectionally: LLM judges inflate scores for self-labeled selections and deflate those for other-labeled ones regardless of the selection's actual source. We make two contributions: 1) authorship attribution is a distinct driver of evaluation bias, and 2) open-ended, ground-truth-free tasks can serve as controlled instruments for studying LLM judge behavior.

</details>


### [92] [Abliteration Mitigation via Refusal Aliases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18093)
*Nathan Truong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AMRA的权重编辑防御方法，通过混淆大语言模型中的拒绝信号来抵御abliteration攻击（即通过投影权重矩阵去除模型的拒绝能力）。AMRA在Llama-3-8B和Gemma-2-9B上显著提升了模型在经受abliteration攻击后的拒绝表现，同时保持了较低的实用性损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Abliteration作为一种仅需少量对比提示即可绕过模型后训练对齐的安全威胁，其核心原因在于拒绝方向（refusal direction）容易被提取。现有防御方法普遍忽视了这一根本原因，未能针对拒绝方向的提取难度进行防护。

Method: 提出一种基于权重编辑的防御方法AMRA，具体包括：(1) 对残差流写入矩阵（residual stream writer matrices）应用秩为k的更新；(2) 将诱发拒绝行为的激活替换为随机别名（random aliases）；(3) 修正下游读取矩阵（reader matrices）以保持模型的原始行为不变。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B上，AMRA将遭受abliteration攻击后的拒绝分数相比未防御基线提升了2.16分，MMLU性能仅下降不到0.5个百分点。在Gemma-2-9B上，遭受攻击后的拒绝表现提升了14.70分，有害输出率与基线相当，但实用性成本更高。

Conclusion: AMRA通过攻击拒绝信号的可提取性这一根源来防御abliteration，在多个模型上实现了显著的防御效果，证明了混淆拒绝信号是抵御该攻击类型的有效方向，但其在Gemma-2-9B上的实用性代价仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: Abliteration, the removal of refusal capabilities from large language models by projecting weight matrices orthogonal to an extracted refusal direction, has emerged as a prominent safety concern through its ability to bypass post-training alignment using only a small set of contrastive prompts. We find that existing defenses commonly overlook the cause of abliteration; that is, how easily the refusal direction can be extracted. To hinder this process, we introduce a weight-editing method that obscures the refusal signal by applying rank-$k$ updates to residual stream writer matrices while replacing refusal-inducing activations with random aliases and correcting downstream reader matrices to preserve the model's original behavior. On Llama-3-8B, AMRA improves post-abliteration refusal scores by $2.16$ points over the undefended baseline with less than $0.5$ percentage points of MMLU degradation. On Gemma-2-9B, it improves the post-abliteration refusal by $14.70$ points over the baseline while keeping harmful output rates similar to the baseline, albeit at a greater utility cost.

</details>


### [93] [NE-BERT: A Multilingual Language Model for Nine Northeast Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18094)
*Badal Nyalang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NE-BERT，一个面向印度东北部9种低资源语言的多语言编码器模型，使用约830万句训练数据及加权数据采样和自定义SentencePiece Unigram分词器，在困惑度和分词效能上显著优于IndicBERT-V2、MuRIL和mBERT，并通过词性标注任务验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练语言模型在主流语言上表现优异，但印度东北部语言作为极度低资源语言在现有多语言模型中代表性严重不足，存在词汇碎片化等问题，阻碍了数字包容和NLP研究发展。

Method: NE-BERT采用自定义SentencePiece Unigram分词器，结合加权数据抽样策略对低资源语言进行激进过采样，在约830万句（覆盖9种印度东北部语言及印地语、英语两个锚定语言）上训练领域特定的多语言编码器模型。

Result: 在全部9种印度东北部语言上，NE-BERT的平均困惑度比IndicBERT-V2低15.97倍、比MuRIL低7.64倍；分词效能（fertility）比mBERT提升1.50倍。词性标注下游任务在3种语言上验证了实用性，资源以CC-BY-4.0协议开源。

Conclusion: NE-BERT证明了针对特定低资源语言区域训练领域专用多语言模型的有效性，同时通过激进抽样处理和开源协议发布，推动印度东北部社区的数字包容与NLP研究发展。

Abstract: Large pretrained language models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse languages, yet critically underrepresented low-resource languages remain marginalized. We present NE-BERT, a domain-specific multilingual encoder model trained on approximately 8.3 million sentences spanning 9 Northeast Indian languages and 2 anchor languages (Hindi, English), a linguistically diverse region with minimal representation in existing multilingual models. By employing weighted data sampling and a custom SentencePiece Unigram tokenizer, NE-BERT outperforms IndicBERT-V2 and MuRIL across all 9 Northeast Indian languages, achieving 15.97X and 7.64X lower average perplexity respectively, with 1.50X better tokenization fertility than mBERT. We address critical vocabulary fragmentation issues in extremely low-resource languages such as Pnar (1,002 sentences) and Kokborok (2,463 sentences) through aggressive upsampling strategies. Downstream evaluation on part-of-speech tagging validates practical utility on three Northeast Indian languages. We release NE-BERT, test sets, and training corpus under CC-BY-4.0 to support NLP research and digital inclusion for Northeast Indian communities.

</details>


### [94] [Backdoor Learning in Language Models and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18095)
*Weimin Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文围绕可信AI和高效多模态表征学习两大维度展开：一是分析、检测和设计NLP与视觉-语言模型中的后门攻击以确保安全性；二是面向临床和医学影像应用开发先进的多模态表征方法来提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在NLP和视觉-语言模型（VLMs）上的进步带来了强大能力，但同时也引入了后门攻击等严重安全威胁，亟需在可信AI框架下系统性地分析、检测并设计防御方案；同时，多模态表征学习在临床和医学影像应用中的效率问题也亟待解决。

Method: 论文采用双轨研究路径：（1）安全性方向——对NLP和VLMs中的后门攻击进行分析、检测及防御机制设计；（2）效率方向——面向临床和医学影像场景，开发先进的多模态表征学习方法。

Result: 该论文提出了一套涵盖后门攻击分析与检测的安全框架，同时构建了适用于临床和医学影像应用的高效多模态表征方法，在可信AI和高效多模态学习两个维度均有实质性贡献。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地连接了可信AI（安全维度）与高效多模态表征学习（效率维度），为NLP和VLM系统的安全可靠部署以及医学影像领域的多模态应用提供了全面的研究基础与实践指导。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have significantly enhanced the capabilities of Natural Language Processing (NLP) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs). However, these advancements come with increased vulnerabilities, notably through backdoor attacks that pose severe security threats. This thesis addresses two critical dimensions of Trustworthy AI and Efficient Multimodal Representation Learning: (1) security through analyzing, detecting, and designing backdoor attacks in NLP and VLMs, and (2) efficiency through advanced multimodal representation methods tailored for clinical and medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [95] [MAVEN: A Macro-Societal Value Evaluation Framework of Multimodal Content with Compact Aligned Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18096)
*Zijuan Zhao,Zheren Fu,Hou Xia,Licheng Zhang,Yi Liu,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAVEN，一个基于国际人权文书和文化价值理论的分层框架，用于多模态内容的社会宏观价值评估。MAVEN包含6个主维度和72个次级指标，支持多层次定量评分。作者构建了人工验证的多模态基准和软匹配指标，并提出span自适应多级偏好优化（SA-MDPO）的评估器蒸馏方法及多角色共识推理策略。实验表明，2B参数的紧凑评估器性能可与同系列8B模型媲美，接近前沿闭源VLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态价值评估框架主要局限于安全导向的分类体系、仅文本的心理测量探针或单标签分类，缺乏对宏观社会价值（如和平、正义、自由）的系统性、多层次的评估框架，因此需要一种更全面、可扩展的评估方法。

Method: 1）提出MAVEN分层框架，将宏观社会价值组织为6个主维度和72个二级指标，支持多级定量评分；2）基于MAVEN构建人工验证的多模态基准（MacroValue-Bench）和软匹配指标；3）提出span自适应多级偏好优化（SA-MDPO）用于评估器蒸馏；4）提出训练免费的多角色共识推理策略。

Result: 实验评估了现有开源和闭源VLM在该基准上的表现，揭示了宏观社会价值判断中的共享趋势和明显差异。紧凑的2B评估器与同系列8B模型性能相当，并接近前沿闭源VLM的表现。

Conclusion: MAVEN框架为多模态内容的宏观社会价值评估提供了实用且可扩展的路径，其紧凑评估器在保持高性能的同时降低了计算成本，为大规模宏观社会价值评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Assessing whether multimodal content aligns with macro-societal values, such as peace, justice, and freedom, has become an increasingly urgent challenge. Existing frameworks are largely confined to safety-oriented taxonomies, text-only psychometric probes, or single-label classification. Therefore, we propose MAVEN, a hierarchical framework for macro-societal value evaluation of multimodal content, grounded in international human-rights instruments and cultural value theory. MAVEN organizes values into 6 primary dimensions and 72 secondary indicators, supporting multi-level quantitative scoring. Building on MAVEN, we construct a human-verified multimodal benchmark and a soft-match metric to evaluate VLMs' assessments across value dimensions. For evaluator optimization, we propose a span-adaptive variant of multi-level preference optimization for evaluator distillation, together with a training-free multi-role consensus strategy at inference time. We evaluate existing open- and closed-source VLMs on our benchmark, revealing shared tendencies and clear differences in macro-societal value judgments. Experiments show that our compact 2B evaluator matches its 8B counterpart in the same family and approaches frontier closed-source VLMs, offering a practical path toward scalable macro-societal value evaluation. Our SA-MDPO implementation and MacroValue-Bench are available at https://github.com/zzzzzzzzjj/MAVEN.

</details>


### [96] [FrenchNews-7: Benchmarking Cross-Publisher French News Editorial Desk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18097)
*Amr Sobhy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了FrenchNews-7，一个面向法国出版物的法语新闻编辑台分类基准，包含多出版源语料库、七类别URL衍生分类体系以及微调的CamemBERT分类器。分类标签通过混合流程（URL slug + LLM标注）生成，经人类与LLM的评审研究验证。最佳模型CamemBERT-base在全文输入下超越了标题输入和三个零样本LLM基线（总体召回率0.799）。跨出版源评估揭示了类别边界不稳定性，其中Economie和Societe类别存在编辑惯例性歧义而非可恢复的模型性能空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有新闻分类基准多集中于英语或单一出版源，缺乏面向法语、跨出版源且具有可验证标签质量的新闻编辑台分类基准。作者旨在构建一个包含严格标签审计、跨出版源泛化评估和类别边界稳定性分析的法语新闻分类基准，以推动该领域的研究进展。

Method: 构建了一个多出版源法语新闻语料库，采用混合管道分配标签：优先使用出版源URL slug，对结构歧义案例使用LLM标注；通过2名人类+2个LLM的交叉评审研究验证标签质量（成对kappa≥0.766）。评估了词法、多语言和法语专用分类器在分布内和保留出版源两种设置下的性能，并与三个零样本LLM基线（GPT-OSS-120B、Mistral Small 3.2、Llama-3.3-70B）在保留出版源池上进行对比。

Result: CamemBERT-base在全文输入上表现最佳，总体召回率达0.799，优于标题输入和所有三个零样本LLM基线；该模型能泛化到未见出版源。跨出版源评估显示Sport、Culture & Loisirs和International类别迁移良好，而Economie（召回率0.517）接近人类盲评一致性（0.55），Societe（精确率0.577）吸收了边界歧义。

Conclusion: FrenchNews-7揭示了部分类别的分类困难源于新闻编辑台的编辑惯例差异而非模型能力不足，标注了明确的类别可靠性层级。资源（模型、数据集、参考脚本）已公开，为该领域的后续研究提供了可复现的基础设施。

Abstract: We present FrenchNews-7, a cross-publisher France-based French-language news editorial desk classification benchmark combining a large multi-outlet corpus, a URL-derived seven-class taxonomy, and a fine-tuned CamemBERT classifier. Labels are assigned via a hybrid pipeline combining publisher URL slugs with LLM annotation for structurally ambiguous cases, audited through an inter-rater study (2 humans + 2 LLMs; pairwise $κ\geq 0.766$, human--human $κ= 0.806$). We evaluate lexical, multilingual, and French-specific trained classifiers under both in-distribution and held-out-publisher settings, with additional comparison against zero-shot LLM baselines (GPT-OSS-120B, Mistral Small 3.2, Llama-3.3-70B) on the held-out pool. The strongest model, CamemBERT-base on full article text, outperforms headline-only input, generalizes to unseen outlets, and exceeds all three zero-shot LLM baselines on overall recall (0.799), with the gap concentrated in the ambiguous editorial-boundary categories Economie and Societe. Cross-publisher evaluation reveals uneven boundary stability: Sport, Culture & Loisirs, and International transfer cleanly, while Economie (recall = 0.517) is close to blinded human agreement (0.55), and Societe (precision = 0.577) absorbs boundary ambiguity, both suggesting editorial conventions rather than recoverable classifier headroom. The fine-tuned CamemBERT-base model, labeled manifest, reference collection scripts, and a reliability-tier guidance table are available at https://huggingface.co/LeFrenchNewsLab/camembert-base-frenchnews7 (model) and https://huggingface.co/datasets/LeFrenchNewsLab/frenchnews-7 (dataset).

</details>


### [97] [Fractional Decay KV-Cache: Ownership-Aware Memory Management for Improved Inference Relevancy in Dialog Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18098)
*Sukanta Ganguly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FD-KVC（Fractional Decay KV-Cache），一种用于Transformer对话系统的双通道键值缓存管理算法，通过累积注意力通道和衰减加权相关通道的联合评分机制，在保留历史重要token的同时快速适应对话主题漂移，在五个多轮对话场景中相较SOTA基线H2O提升+6.7%复合对齐精度，主题适应速度快3.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存策略对所有缓存条目统一对待或采用粗糙的淘汰启发式方法，无法随对话主题演变而自适应调整。这导致在长对话系统中，缓存管理无法平衡历史重要信息的保留与当前主题相关性的更新需求。

Method: FD-KVC采用双通道评分机制：1）累积注意力通道（类似H2O）追踪全局重要性；2）衰减加权相关通道，通过时间衰减和强化学习式更新来反映当前主题相关性。引入由归属损失函数驱动的自适应学习率确保收敛无振荡。算法完全在CPU上运行，开销可忽略。

Result: 在五个多轮对话场景（每个含600段对话）中，FD-KVC相较SOTA基线H2O：复合后期对齐指标提升+6.7%；主题转移场景提升+127%；渐次演变场景提升+87%；混合主题场景提升+30%。新主题适应速度快3.6倍，主题多样性达80.6%（所有方法中最高）。消融实验验证了各组件贡献。

Conclusion: FD-KVC通过双通道评分与自适应学习率的有机结合，有效解决了KV缓存中历史重要性保持与主题动态适应之间的权衡问题，为长对话Transformer系统提供了一种高效的缓存管理新方案。主题多样性指标表明该方法在维持对话连贯性的同时能更好地覆盖多样化主题。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) caching is essential for efficient autoregressive inference in transformer based dialog systems, yet existing strategies treat all cached entries uniformly or apply coarse eviction heuristics that fail to adapt as dialog topics evolve. We propose Fractional Decay KV-Cache (FD-KVC), a novel algorithm that maintains a dual-channel scoring mechanism for each cached KV pair: a cumulative attention channel that tracks aggregate importance (akin to H2O), and a recency-weighted relevance channel governed by temporal decay and reinforcement-inspired updates. The combination enables FD-KVC to both preserve historically important tokens and rapidly adapt when dialog topics shift. An adaptive learning rate driven by an ownership loss function ensures convergence without oscillation. FD-KVC operates entirely on CPU with negligible overhead. Across five diverse multi-turn dialog scenarios with 600 dialogs each, FD-KVC outperforms H2O, the state-of-the-art heavy-hitter baseline, by +6.7% on composite late-turn alignment, with improvements of +127% on topic-shift, +87% on gradual evolution, and +30% on mixed-topic dialogs. FD-KVC adapts to new topics 3.6X faster than H2O and achieves the highest topic diversity (80.6%) across all methods. Ablation studies confirm the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [98] [Computational Orientalism: Measuring Structural Discourse Bias in Large Language Models Using the Middle East Cultural Sensitivity Score (MECSS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18100)
*Maha Shahid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出中东文化敏感度评分（MECSS），将萨义德的七种东方主义操作转化为可量化的维度，用于检测AI系统在关于中东的对话中是否系统性地复现东方主义结构。通过对GPT-4和Falcon3-7B-Instruct的1,120组对话交换进行分析，发现两个模型均系统性地复现了东方主义模式，并提出"Said-washing"一词指代模型否认泛化却复现其所否认结构的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统深刻影响数十亿人对异文化的认知，但其训练数据以西方和英语为主，导致对中东的呈现可能存在结构性偏见。标准公平性指标无法捕捉这种偏见，因为它们只检测显式歧视而非结构性框架问题，因此需要新的评估框架。

Method: 作者将萨义德在《东方主义》中提出的七种东方主义操作转化为可测量的评估维度，构建了MECSS框架；引入"Said-washing"概念描述模型否认泛化却复现东方主义结构的失败模式；对GPT-4和Falcon3-7B-Instruct进行280组对话（共1,120次交换）的系统性评估。

Result: 两个模型均系统性地通过结构性定位而非公开刻板印象复现东方主义模式。GPT-4的平均MECSS为1.73（中等），Falcon3-7B-Instruct为2.18（较高）；"认识论中心"维度（将西方框架视为无标记的普遍标准）两个模型均接近量表顶端；Said-washing出现在87.9%的GPT-4对话中。值得注意的是，在阿布扎比构建并含阿拉伯语训练的Falcon3-7B-Instruct东方主义程度更高，但模型规模和来源地无法隔离，不能确定地理因素为原因。

Conclusion: 减少这种偏见需要改变模型的学习来源，而不仅仅是增加语言或迁移机构所在地。旨在消除东方主义偏见的AI开发必须面对训练数据中的结构性框架问题，而非依赖表面上的多样性措施。

Abstract: AI systems now shape how hundreds of millions of people learn about cultures other than their own. When someone asks one of these systems about the Middle East, they do not receive neutral facts. They receive a representation shaped by the frameworks embedded in training data, and that data is overwhelmingly Western and English-language. This paper asks whether that representation is Orientalist in Said's sense: whether it denies agency to Middle Eastern actors, treats Western frameworks as neutral while marking non-Western knowledge as particular, and explains the region through categories it did not produce. Standard fairness metrics cannot answer this, because they detect explicit prejudice rather than structural framing. This paper introduces the Middle East Cultural Sensitivity Score (MECSS), a framework that turns Said's seven Orientalist operations into measurable dimensions, and the term "Said-washing" for a specific failure: a model that disclaims generalization, then reproduces the structure it disclaimed. Across 280 conversations (1,120 exchanges), GPT-4 and Falcon3-7B-Instruct both reproduce Orientalist patterns systematically, through structural positioning rather than open stereotyping. GPT-4 scores moderately (mean MECSS 1.73); Falcon3-7B-Instruct scores higher (2.18), even though it was built in Abu Dhabi and trained with Arabic content. This is evidence against the assumption that building a model regionally makes it less Orientalist, though the models differ in size as well as origin, so geography cannot be isolated as the cause. Epistemic Center, the treatment of Western frameworks as unmarked universals, scores near the top of the scale for both models. Said-washing appears in 87.9% of GPT-4 conversations, a pattern existing metrics cannot see. Reducing this bias requires changing what models learn from, not only adding languages or relocating institutions.

</details>


### [99] [BERTilda: Explainable Topic Lifecycle Tracking with Split/Merge Detection via Similarity-and-Flow Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18101)
*Cláudia Oliveira,Álvaro Figueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BERTilda是一个可解释的框架，通过嵌入主题模型在时间窗口内独立发现主题，并构建跨窗口的主题图来建模主题生命周期（持续、分裂、合并、消失），在政治语料上达到最高87%的多数据标注一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向文本流中的主题不仅会发生平滑漂移，还会出现离散的结构重组（分裂/合并），但现有动态主题模型过于强调平滑演化，而快照式主题模型又缺乏对跨时间窗口对应关系的明确建模。

Method: BERTilda在每个时间窗口内使用基于嵌入的主题模型独立发现主题；然后构建跨相邻窗口的主题图，通过两种互补信号建立链接：(i)主题表示间的语义相似度，(ii)通过跨窗口推文到主题归属计算的双向覆盖信号（估计文档流出量和流入量）。基于图的规则对持续、分裂、合并、消失和不确定转换进行标注。

Result: 在政治语料（美国国会推文和历史演讲数据集）上，BERTilda在标注子集上达到最高87%的多数一致率，在对比方法中取得最高宏平均一致率，尤其在消失检测方面显著优于仅相似度和仅前向基线。

Conclusion: 同时利用语义相似性和双向覆盖信号可以有效提升主题生命周期标注的准确性，特别是对主题消失这类难以仅靠相似性判别的转换类型。

Abstract: Longitudinal text streams exhibit topic birth and death, but also discrete structural reorganizations in which themes split into subtopics or merge into broader narratives. Many dynamic topic models emphasize smooth drift, while snapshot topic models (fit independently per time window) leave temporal correspondence underspecified. We present BERTilda, an explainable framework that discovers topics independently in each window (using an embedding-based topic model) and then constructs a temporal topic graph linking topics across adjacent windows. Links are supported by two complementary signals: (i) semantic similarity between topic representations and (ii) a bidirectional coverage signal that estimates document outflow (where a topic goes) and inflow (where a topic comes from) via cross-window tweet-to-topic attribution. Graph-based rules label continuations, splits, merges, disappearances, and unclear transitions. We evaluate BERTilda on political corpora, including U.S. congressional tweets and historical speech datasets, report topic-quality and temporal-stability diagnostics, and validate lifecycle labels on a gold-standard subset annotated by three independent annotators. On the annotated subset, BERTilda reaches majority agreement rates up to 87% and attains the highest macro-average agreement across the compared methods, with particularly strong disappearance detection relative to similarity-only and forward-only baselines.

</details>


### [100] [Stability-Aware Feature Design for Robust Watermark Detection in Machine-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18102)
*Sina Mansouri,Mohit Marvania,Abolfazl Safikhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Pattern Stability Score (PSS)，一种基于局部统计特征、游程高阶统计量、自相关信号和释义深度稳定性分数的新型文本水印检测框架，在多重释义和短文本场景下显著优于现有方法，AUC提升10-15个百分点，且无需重新训练即可跨模型、跨释义器、跨领域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的广泛使用使得区分人类与机器生成文本变得至关重要。水印检测是可行路径，但现有检测器在多重释义和短文本场景下性能急剧下降，亟需更稳健的检测方法。

Method: 提出PSS框架，融合全局与局部z-score特征、游程长度模式的高阶统计量、自相关信号，以及跨释义深度计算稳定性分数，组合成综合检测特征。

Result: 在PG-19、CNN/DailyMail、WikiText三个数据集上，使用Llama-3-8B、Qwen2-7B等LLM及多种释义器测试，最高达8轮释义攻击下，检测AUC相比基线提升10-15个百分点。跨域实验表明单一通用分类器在不同LLM、释义器和文本域间无需重训即保持87.8%以上AUC。

Conclusion: PSS通过利用局部统计特征与释义稳定性动力学，有效解决了水印检测在多重释义和短文本下的鲁棒性瓶颈，并展现出良好的跨域泛化能力，为文本水印检测提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of large language models (LLMs) has intensified the demand for principled methods to distinguish human from machine-generated text. Watermarking provides a promising avenue, yet existing detectors exhibit sharp performance deterioration under multiple paraphrasing and when applied to shorter texts. We introduce Pattern Stability Score (PSS), a novel detection framework that leverages local statistical features and stability dynamics across paraphrased variants. Specifically, the proposed method combines global and local z-score features with higher-order statistics of run-length patterns, enriched by autocorrelation signals and stability scores computed over paraphrase depth. Numerical evaluations are performed on three benchmark datasets (PG-19, CNN/DailyMail, and WikiText) using multiple LLMs (Llama-3-8B, Qwen2-7B) and paraphrasers (Mistral-7B, Qwen2-7B, Gemma-7B), systematically stress-testing robustness under up to eight rounds of paraphrasing. Compared to prior z-score thresholding baselines and some state-of-the-art deep learning methods, our approach improves detection AUC (area under the receiver operating characteristic curve) by over 10-15 percentage points across different token lengths. Additionally, extensive cross-domain experiments demonstrate that a single universal classifier generalizes across different LLMs, paraphrasers, and text domains without retraining, maintaining above 87.8% AUC even when all components differ from training.

</details>


### [101] [DeepTCM1.0: A Multi-Expert AI Agent for Deciphering Mechanisms of Chinese Herbal Formulae Based on General Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18103)
*Wenxin Duan,Hanwei Wang,Zhongying Peng,Zhonghua Lu,Jiayi An,Fan Song,Yong Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeepTCM1.0多智能体框架，基于DeepSeek V3.2通用大语言模型构建三层协同架构和三轮迭代质控流程，模拟11个跨学科智能体的协同分析过程，对桂枝汤进行经典中医理论与现代科学双视角的机制解析，并通过双盲五维评分、ICC可靠性检验等进行了系统评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中医复方机制的阐释面临挑战：数据挖掘和网络药理学方法难以实现经典中医理论与现代科学的深度融合，而通用大语言模型的直接问答又缺乏对中医理论框架的适配且易产生推理幻觉。因此亟需开发符合中医整体观原则的智能分析方法。

Method: 基于DeepSeek V3.2构建DeepTCM1.0框架，采用三层协同架构与三轮迭代质量控制工作流，模拟11个跨学科智能体的协同分析过程。以桂枝汤为载体，从经典中医理论与现代科学研究双视角进行机制阐释。通过双盲五维评分、组内相关系数可靠性检验、Mann-Whitney U检验及效应量分析进行全面性能评估，使用4个独立大语言模型作为评估者，对5份匿名报告进行每轮5次重复评分，共获得100次独立评分。

Result: 文章摘要呈现了DeepTCM1.0框架的构建流程与评估方案，但未给出具体的评分结果与机制解析结论。评估设计包含100次独立评分以验证框架的可靠性、有效性和效应规模。

Conclusion: DeepTCM1.0旨在建立符合中医整体观的多专家智能体框架，弥合经典中医理论与现代生命科学之间的鸿沟，为中医复方提供系统化、可解释的机制分析途径，但具体效应量及显著性结果需详读全文获取。

Abstract: Background: Mechanistic elucidation of traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) compound formulas remains a central challenge in the modernization of TCM. Conventional approaches, including data mining and network pharmacology, are insufficient for achieving deep integration between classical TCM theory and modern scientific research. In addition, direct question-answering using general-purpose artificial intelligence large language models is limited by inadequate adaptation to TCM theoretical frameworks and susceptibility to reasoning hallucinations. Consequently, there is an urgent need to develop intelligent analytical methods aligned with the holistic principles of TCM.
