Result: 在多个多轮智能体RL基准测试上，RTPO相对轨迹级和逐轮级基线分别获得21.50%和10.76%的性能提升，展现了提升工具使用智能体训练稳定性的潜力。

Conclusion: RTPO通过反向逐轮优化策略有效解决了多轮RL训练的三个核心不稳定性来源，提供了理论保障和实验验证，为多轮智能体稳定训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Training multi-turn agentic workflows with reinforcement learning (RL) enables large language models to perform complex reasoning, use external tools, and conduct iterative search beyond single-turn settings. Yet multi-turn RL training remains highly unstable, often causing severe performance degradation as the number of turns increases. Through theoretical analysis, we identify three tightly coupled sources of instability: rollout-training context mismatch, weak turn-level credit assignment under sparse terminal rewards, and asynchronous policy drift when short and long trajectories are optimized under different policy versions. We show that these issues share a common structural origin in flattened trajectory optimization and address them through a unified reverse-turn formulation. We propose Reverse-Turn Policy Optimization (RTPO), which organizes multi-turn rollouts as sparse reverse trees and performs turn-level policy updates in temporal reverse order, aligning each decision with its downstream continuation. RTPO enables causally consistent turn-level credit assignment and on-policy continuation to control asynchronous drift. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that RTPO eliminates context mismatch and asynchronous drift under the proposed turn-level formulation, reduces credit bias, and converges to recursive optimality. Experiments on multi-turn agentic RL benchmarks show that RTPO improves upon trajectory- and turn-level baselines by 21.50% and 10.76%, respectively, highlighting its potential to support more stable training for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [202] [Competence, Not Accuracy: A Diagnostic for Reference-Free Judge Gates in Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18719)
*Chenle Chen,Yangbo Wei,Chao Yao,Shaoqiang Lu,Junhong Qian,Chen Wu,Lei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预部署诊断方法，用于判断LLM-judge是否能在文本空间技能优化的验证门中有效区分正确与不正确答案。通过将参考无关的judge建模为潜在求解器，推导出判别力（ROC-AUC）的闭式界，并证明存在必要条件 c > 1/k。实验表明，当能力接近底限时判别力趋近随机，高于底限时可用；benchmark准确率会高估实际相关能力；该诊断能预测门控错误的类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本空间技能优化依赖自动验证器，限制了适用范围。用LLM-judge替代验证器可解除此限制，但judge信号是否有用未经检验。因此需要在将judge放入循环之前，判断其分数是否能在正确与错误答案间产生分离。

Method: 将参考无关的judge建模为潜在求解器（latent solver），推导其判别能力（ROC-AUC）关于judge能力c和答案空间大小k的闭式界。提出非干预式探针（non-intervening probe）在真实优化运行中记录judge分数而不改变任何决策，并区分边际AUC与题内（within-question）估计器的混淆效应。

Result: 当judge能力接近底限（c ≈ 1/k）时判别力为随机水平；高于底限时判别力可用。benchmark准确率会高估与门控相关的实际能力。闭环实验表明该筛查方法能预测具体哪种门控错误会发生。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个廉价、可在部署前执行的诊断工具，用于筛选LLM-judge门控的有效性，扩展了文本空间技能优化到无自动验证器的任务范围。

Abstract: Text-space skill optimization adapts a frozen agent by evolving a natural-language skill document, accepting each candidate through a validation gate. Existing gates rely on verifiable rewards, confining these methods to tasks with an automatic verifier. Replacing the verifier with an LLM-judge gate would lift that restriction, but whether such a gate carries usable signal is untested. We ask a prior question: can we tell, before placing a judge in the loop, whether its scores separate correct from incorrect answers at all? We formalize a reference-free judge as a latent solver -- its verdict rests on agreement with whatever it would itself conclude, so its capacity to evaluate is bounded by its capacity to solve. The model yields a closed-form bound on discriminability (ROC-AUC) in the judge's competence $c$ and answer-space size $k$, a necessary condition $c > 1/k$, and the result that the marginal AUC is confounded by item difficulty while a within-question estimator is not. A non-intervening probe records judge scores on genuine optimization runs without altering any decision. We find discriminability at chance where competence sits near the floor and usable above it; that a judge's benchmark accuracy overstates the competence that matters; and, in a closed-loop study, that the screen predicts which kind of gating error occurs. The result is a cheap pre-deployment diagnostic for judge gates.

</details>


### [203] [A Multi-Agent Platform for Automated Enterprise Analytics and Insight Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18740)
*Manoj N M,Vijayakrishna S,Manjunath Srinivas,Rohit Pahan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于CrewAI的多智能体框架，用于会话式商业智能分析。五个专职AI代理以顺序流水线方式处理自然语言查询、检索和分析数据、通过MCP生成可视化并输出行动洞察。在300个端到端测试用例中达到95.3%功能准确率、24秒平均延迟和4.52/5.0的响应质量评分，较单智能体基线提升22.6个百分点准确率和20.2%质量增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统会话式商业智能系统通常依赖单一模型处理全部任务，导致在复杂查询下分析深度不足、准确率受限，且缺乏数据隔离的安全保障与跨数据集泛化能力。需要一种模块化、可扩展的多代理协作架构来提升自然语言到数据分析的整体质量和可靠性。

Method: 采用CrewAI构建五种专职代理（数据处理、检索分析、可视化、报告聚合等）组成顺序流水线；通过Model Context Protocol (MCP)实现可视化生成；设计纵深防御安全架构支持多租户数据隔离；引入查询参数化机制将对话洞察转化为可复用仪表盘组件；采用LLM-as-a-Judge评估框架和消融研究验证各代理贡献。

Result: 在300个跨合成与生产数据的端到端测试中实现95.3%功能准确率、24秒平均响应延迟、4.52/5.0响应质量得分及93.0%无幻觉率；相比单智能体基线提升22.6个百分点准确率和20.2%质量。跨四个LLM后端及人类专家验证确认泛化性，消融实验表明数据分析和报告聚合代理是质量主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作架构显著优于单智能体基线，具备跨模型泛化能力；纵深防御与查询参数化提供了企业级安全性与可复用性；消融分析明确了关键代理模块的贡献，为后续改进指明方向。

Abstract: This paper proposes a multi-agent framework built on CrewAI [1] for conversational business intelligence. Five specialized AI agents operate in a sequential pipeline to process natural language queries, retrieve and analyze data, generate visualizations via the Model Context Protocol (MCP) [2], and deliver actionable insights. The platform features a defense-in-depth security architecture for multi-tenant data isolation and a query parameterization mechanism for transforming conversational insights into reusable dashboard components. Evaluation across 300 end-to-end test cases spanning synthetic and production enterprise datasets demonstrates 95.3% functional accuracy, a mean response latency of 24 seconds, and a response quality score of 4.52/5.0 as assessed by an LLM-as-a-Judge framework, with a 93.0% hallucination-free rate, representing a 22.6 percentage point accuracy improvement and 20.2% quality gain over a single-agent baseline. Cross-model evaluation across four LLM backends and human expert validation confirm architectural generalizability and evaluator reliability. An ablation study confirms that the Data Analysis and Report Aggregation agents are the primary drivers of output quality.

</details>


### [204] [Metrics That Write Themselves: Evolving an Evaluator from Its Own Blind Spots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18744)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Zhihao Lin,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvalCEGAR方法，借鉴程序验证中的反例引导抽象精化（CEGAR）技术，自动演化出一个小型Python算子池作为评估度量（metric）。该度量通过让算子对候选答案的缺陷进行投票打分，在无需人工编写评分标准的情况下自动生成评估器。在MBPP+和HumanEval+上，该方法生成的55行算子关闭了从无筛选到完美筛选之间15.4%的差距，且其效果优于15个人工撰写算子组合成的过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体（agent）需要可靠的自动度量才能快速改进，而没有度量时则会停滞。报告生成等最需要度量的应用领域恰恰没有人知道如何编写评分标准。直接让模型生成度量算子效果不佳（183个候选仅产生96种不同行为，且集中于巨大空间的一个狭小区域），因此需要一种更系统的方法来自动构建评估指标。

Method: EvalCEGAR借鉴程序验证中的反例引导抽象精化（CEGAR）思想：将算子池视为一个抽象（abstraction），搜索碰撞（collision）——即两个答案被算子评分相同但一个正确一个错误。该碰撞对作为生成新算子的指令（而非提示词），当碰撞无法被解决时，扩大算子可读取的信息范围而非重新采样。算子池中的每个算子标记候选答案的一个命名缺陷或弃权，通过投票汇总评分。

Result: 在MBPP+和HumanEval+上，循环写出了55行的算子，在428个未见任务上关闭了从无筛选到完美过滤之间15.4%的差距（+0.0065, p=0.0010），且输出仅为人手编写最佳算子的1/4。在未见基准上，该算子与最佳算子有1/3的标记完全匹配。八次运行中六次生成了有效算子且全部对样本外有帮助；而15个人工算子组合成一个过滤器反而损失了准确性。

Conclusion: EvalCEGAR证明了度量可以自我编写：通过系统地搜索评分不一致的碰撞案例来驱动算子设计，可以自动演化出比人工编写更好的评估器。与LLM裁判相比，该算子无需每次候选都调用模型，成本为零且效果相当。该方法开辟了自动生成评估指标的新途径。

Abstract: Agents improve quickly against a reliable automatic metric and stall without one, and the applications that need them most, report generation among them, are the ones nobody knows how to score. Can the metric write itself? Saying what makes an answer good is hard; pointing at something wrong with one is easier, so the metric we evolve is a pool of small Python operators that each flag a candidate for one named defect, or abstain, and vote. Asking a model for operators directly does not work: 183 candidates realise only 96 distinct behaviours, from one narrow region of an enormous space. EvalCEGAR instead borrows counterexample-guided abstraction refinement from program verification. It reads the pool as an abstraction and searches for a collision, two answers the operators score identically, one correct and one not. That pair, not a prompt, is the authoring request, and when a collision defeats every attempt the loop widens what an operator may read rather than resampling. On MBPP+ and HumanEval+, a sandbox whose hidden unit tests give exact ground truth, the loop writes a 55-line operator that closes 15.4% of the gap between flagging nothing and a perfect filter on 428 unseen tasks (+0.0065, p=0.0010) at a quarter of our best hand-written operator's flags. On the benchmark it never saw it matches that operator's effect exactly on a third of the flags. Six of eight runs admit such an operator and all six help out of sample; our 15 hand-written operators applied together as one filter lose accuracy. An LLM judge on the same information ties that delta on a nearly disjoint set of candidates, and charges a model call per candidate forever where the operator charges none.

</details>


### [205] [Pairwise Logical Selection of Enthymeme Completions under Semantic-Link Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18820)
*Xuyao Feng,Antonis Bikakis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PWAL（Possible-World Atom-Link Formalization）框架，用于在省略推理（enthymeme）中选择缺失的逻辑成分。PWAL基于加权Partial MaxSAT，通过对跨公式语义链接配置的边缘化来计算逻辑阻力（logical-resistance）分数，替代传统二元蕴含判定。在ARCT、CDED、iDebate、AAE2和alphaNLI五个任务上，相较Top-Link基线，PWAL严格准确率提升2.95-30.86个百分点，平局率下降4.57-58.00个百分点，并提供透明的推理可追溯记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论辩中的省略推理（enthymeme）常隐含前提或结论，现有自然语言方法虽能识别或生成候选成分，但无法揭示所选候选如何完成推理；逻辑方法虽能提供推理解释，却通常假设所需公式和背景知识是现成的。这一缺口促使研究者探索一种既能解释推理过程又不过度依赖前提假设的神经符号方法。

Method: 本文扩展了先前从缺失前提选择到缺失结论选择的神经符号管线，将二值蕴含结果替换为逻辑阻力（logical-resistance）分数。Top-Link采用单配置下最高置信度语义链接的加权Partial MaxSAT；PWAL则保持翻译后的公式不变，在备选跨公式语义链接配置上对逻辑阻力进行边缘化。PWAL还为每次比较记录翻译公式、采样链接配置和阻力分量，提供透明可追踪的评分过程。

Result: 在五个任务（ARCT和CDED用于缺失前提选择，iDebate和AAE2用于缺失结论选择，alphaNLI用于反事实假设选择）上进行评估。相较Top-Link，PWAL在全部任务上严格准确率提升2.95-30.86个百分点，平局率降低4.57-58.00个百分点；平局计半分时准确率仍提升0.45-6.04个百分点。

Conclusion: PWAL作为一种神经符号框架，通过边缘化语义链接配置来评估省略推理中的逻辑选择，在准确性和平局消解能力上都显著优于单一配置的Top-Link方法，同时保留了完整的推理追踪记录，兼顾了性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Arguments often omit premises or claims, forming enthymemes. We study pairwise logical selection between two candidates for the omitted component. Existing natural language methods can identify or generate candidates but often do not expose how the selected candidate completes the inference, while logic-based approaches usually assume that the required formulae and background knowledge are available. We extend a prior neuro-symbolic pipeline from missing-premise to missing-claim selection and replace binary entailment outcomes with logical-resistance scores. Top-Link uses weighted Partial MaxSAT under a single configuration of highest-confidence semantic links. We then introduce Possible-World Atom-Link Formalization (PWAL), which keeps translated formulae fixed and marginalizes logical resistance over alternative cross-formula semantic-link configurations. We evaluate PWAL on five tasks: ARCT and a CDED-derived task for missing-premise selection, iDebate- and AAE2-derived tasks for missing-claim selection, and alphaNLI for abductive hypothesis selection. Relative to Top-Link, PWAL raises strict accuracy by 2.95-30.86 percentage points and reduces tie rates by 4.57-58.00 percentage points on all five tasks. When ties receive half credit, accuracy still increases by 0.45-6.04 percentage points. PWAL also records the translated formulae, sampled link configurations, and resistance components for every comparison, providing a transparent trace of each score.

</details>


### [206] [Verifiable abstention makes AI leak diagnosis accountable in water distribution networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18836)
*Tianwei Mu,Yue Wang,Mingzhe Yuan,Manhong Huang,Wenhong Wang,Xuerui Yin,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Hui Yang,Jun Li,Dan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将供水管网漏损定位问题重新定义为"可验证弃权下的决策制定"：通过一个基于物理的"执行者代理"在数字孪生上对泄漏、需求、传感器、阀门等假设进行证伪；再由一个独立的"监督者代理"配合大语言模型审计器，依据可代码验证的契约检查证据，最终决定派工、索取更多证据或主动弃权。在田间级噪声下，强制基线32%的正确率提升至动作事件96%的决策精度；独立基准测试中仅在33个泄漏中动作4个且全部正确；194条经审计的真实泄漏记录（含孪生模拟的压力与流量）产生5次开挖派工，其中3次正确，并实现44%的巡线恢复率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供水企业因泄漏损失大量处理水，但不敢信任AI定位器派工，因为"到处猜测"无法为开挖提供依据。核心差距在于问责性而非精度——现有方法无法证明自己何时不应采取行动。作者希望通过引入可验证的弃权机制，为自主水务运行建立可辩护的决策路径。

Method: 将泄漏定位重构为"可验证弃权下的决策"问题。采用双代理架构：1) 物理驱动的执行者代理在数字孪生上对泄漏、需水量、传感器、阀门等假设进行系统性证伪；2) 独立的监督者代理配合LLM审计器，依据代码可验证的契约核查证据链，输出三种决策——认证派工、请求补充证据或弃权。

Result: 在田间级噪声条件下，32%的强制决策基线提升至96%的动作事件决策精度。在独立生成的基准测试中，模型仅在33个泄漏中动作4个且全部正确。在194条经审计的真实泄漏记录（配以孪生模拟压力与流量）上，产生5次开挖派工、3次正确，并实现44%的巡线恢复率和全区域级精度。

Conclusion: "可问责弃权"机制为自主水务基础设施运行提供了一条在逻辑上可辩护的路径：让AI在证据充分时认证并派工、证据不足时主动弃权，从而在保证高精度的同时建立可信赖的问责机制。

Abstract: Utilities lose a substantial share of treated water to leakage, yet rarely trust artificial-intelligence localizers to dispatch crews: guessing everywhere cannot justify excavation. The gap is accountability, not accuracy: no method proves when it should not act. Here we recast leak localization as decision-making under verifiable abstention. A physics-grounded executor agent falsifies hypotheses (leak, demand, sensor, valve) against a digital twin; an independent supervisor agent, with a large-language-model (LLM) auditor, checks evidence against a code-verifiable contract, then certifies a dispatch, requests evidence or abstains. Under field-grade noise, a 32% forced baseline becomes 96% decision precision on acted events. On an independently generated benchmark it acts on only 4 of 33 leaks, all correct. A 194-event register of audited real leak locations with twin-simulated pressures and flows yields five excavation dispatches, three correct, and 44% survey recovery at full district precision. Accountable abstention offers a defensible route to autonomous water-infrastructure operation.

</details>


### [207] [ORBITER: Conflict-Aware Decision-Making for Agentic Last-Mile Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18846)
*Mingzhao Li,Chenxi Liu,Yan Zhao,Hao Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ORBITER是一个用于最后一英里配送中下一订单决策的智能代理系统，通过决策点建模配送员服务过程，结合固定排序器、结构化报告和LLM工具推理，在四个城市数据上相比现有方法平均提升最高9.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的方法虽然能建模订单间的时空依赖并预测配送员服务序列，但缺乏对单个下一订单决策的显式解释；而直接使用LLM作为预测器对任务呈现方式敏感且决策不可靠，因此需要一种既能发挥LLM推理能力又保证决策可靠性的框架。

Method: ORBITER通过决策点（decision points）建模配送服务，每个决策点包含配送员的时空状态和可见订单，暴露局部权衡用于建模和验证。固定排序器（fixed proposers）对候选订单排序，结构化报告识别排序分歧点；LLM利用特定任务工具收集关键候选替代方案的证据，独立的评判器（critic）根据证据核查最终决策。

Result: 在四个城市的数据集上进行广泛评估，ORBITER相比现有最先进基线平均提升最高达9.2%。

Conclusion: ORBITER通过将LLM的显式时空推理与固定排序器的稳定性结合，并引入独立评判机制，有效地解决了LLM作为直接预测器时的不可靠性问题，证明了其在最后一英里配送下一订单决策任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Last-mile delivery aims to handle dynamically arriving orders with couriers while modeling complex spatial and temporal correlations. Recent learning-based methods model spatiotemporal dependencies among orders to predict courier service sequences, but leave next-order decision making unexplained. Describing the current delivery state in language allows LLMs to reason explicitly about the spatial, temporal, and behavioral cues behind an individual decision. As direct predictors, however, LLMs remain sensitive to task presentation and often produce unreliable decisions. To address these challenges, we introduce ORBITER, an agentic Order Arbiter for next-order decision-making in last-mile delivery. ORBITER models courier service through decision points, each containing the courier's spatiotemporal state and visible orders and exposing local trade-offs for modeling and verification. Fixed proposers rank the candidates, and a structured report identifies where their rankings disagree. The LLM uses task-specific tools to gather evidence on the leading alternatives, while an independent critic checks the resulting decision against that evidence. We conduct extensive evaluations on data in four cities, where ORBITER outperforms existing state-of-the-art baselines by up to 9.2% on average showing its effectiveness.

</details>


### [208] [SkillGate: Training In-Policy Skill Selection in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18852)
*Qingyao Li,Wenxiang Jiao,Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillGate方法，解决智能体框架中"技能选择"这一策略层面决策无法被现有信号训练的问题。作者发现默认的outcome-rewarded RL会遭受selector credit starvation（选择器信用匮乏）：在广播式的序列级优势信号下，命名所选技能的少量token承担几乎为零的损失份额，且随着轨迹变长，其继承的信用错误符号概率增大——即使选择本身是正确的，只要后续执行失败，选择也会受到惩罚。SkillGate通过将token支持集划分为两个不相交的信用通道从结构上消除该问题：结果信用仅到达执行token，独立的动作局部优势恰好到达技能命名token，且仅当轨迹中唯一一次读取为正确时为正。在五个智能体基准的16候选技能设置下，SkillGate将9B策略的试验成功率从40.8%提升至53.2%，远超同等预算的outcome reward，并减少三分之二的误导性候选暴露，且读取更少的技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体框架中技能选择是策略在回合中途做出的关键决策，但没有任何已有的训练信号专门用于训练该决策；默认的outcome-rewarded RL在结构上无法教会它，存在selector credit starvation问题——正确的技能选择会因后续执行失败而受到错误惩罚。

Method: SkillGate将token支持集划分为两个不相交的信用通道：outcome credit只到达执行token；独立的action-local advantage恰好到达技能命名token，且仅当轨迹中唯一一次读取为正确的技能时该优势为正。

Result: 在五个agentic基准、16候选技能设置下，SkillGate将9B策略的试验成功率从40.8%提升至53.2%，高于同等outcome reward预算的基线，误导性候选暴露减少三分之二，且读取技能数更少。审计已完成的训练工件确认了selector credit starvation的三个属性，且每个属性随horizon单调恶化。

Conclusion: SkillGate通过信用通道分离从结构上消除了选择器信用匮乏问题，使技能选择决策得到准确、专门的训练信号，显著提升智能体在大型技能库中的表现。

Abstract: Agent frameworks increasingly package procedural knowledge as skills: instruction files an agent reads on demand, while public libraries now hold thousands of them. Which skill to read has thus become a decision the policy itself makes in the middle of an episode, yet no existing signal trains it. We show that the default remedy, outcome-rewarded RL over the candidate slate, cannot teach it, for a structural reason we identify and name selector credit starvation: under a broadcast, sequence-level advantage, the few tokens that name the chosen skill carry a vanishing share of the loss, and the credit they inherit is increasingly wrong-signed as trajectories lengthen. A correct choice is punished whenever the execution after it fails, even though the choice itself is among the most valuable decisions in the trajectory. Auditing a completed run's own training artifacts confirms all three properties, each worsening monotonically with horizon. SkillGate removes the failure by construction: it partitions the token support into two disjoint credit channels, outcome credit reaching only execution tokens, and a separate action-local advantage reaching exactly the skill-naming tokens, positive only when a trajectory's single read is the correct one. On five agentic benchmarks under a 16-candidate slate, SkillGate lifts a 9B policy from 40.8% to 53.2% trial success, well ahead of the identical budget spent on outcome reward alone, while cutting exposure to misleading candidates by two thirds and reading fewer skills.

</details>


### [209] [DentAgent: Evidence-Centric Multi-Agent Coordination for Multimodal Dental Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18878)
*Zijie Meng,Xiwei Dai,Yixuan Tang,Jin Hao,Yang Feng,Fudong Zhu,Xiaoqiang Liu,Shaosheng Cao,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DentAgent是一个以证据为中心的智能体框架，通过编排五个跨模态专家智能体，将牙科领域知识、X光片、口内照片和3D牙科数据统一转换为结构化证据记录，实现了可追溯的多模态牙科推理，在四项基准测试中表现领先，多标签诊断准确率超越资深专家17.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔疾病影响全球数十亿人，亟需整合多模态异质证据的准确可靠的牙科评估系统。然而现有牙科AI系统大多局限于单一模态或单一任务，而近期视觉语言模型虽然支持灵活的牙科问答，但直接生成的回答缺乏隐式证据且不可追溯。

Method: DentAgent采用证据中心的多智能体框架，包含一个编排者（Orchestrator）协调五个覆盖不同模态的专家智能体。每个专家智能体利用领域工具将观测结果转换为结构化证据记录，并通过证据黑板（Evidence Blackboard）管理这些记录为共享证据状态，跟踪覆盖度、缺口和冲突后再生成响应。

Result: 在四项基准测试中，DentAgent均展示了领先性能，在多标签诊断任务上甚至超越资深专家17.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 标准化证据表示将孤立的牙科能力整合到统一的智能体工作流中，验证了该方法在广泛适用且可追溯的多模态牙科推理中的价值，并凸显其作为人口口腔健康评估与管理技术基础的潜力。

Abstract: Oral diseases affect billions of people worldwide, underscoring a pressing need for accurate and reliable dental assessment that integrates heterogeneous evidence from domain knowledge, radiographs, intraoral photographs, and 3D dental data. Most existing dental AI systems remain modality- or task-specific. Although recent vision-language models support flexible dental question answering, directly generated response leaves evidence implicit and untraceable. To address these limitations, we introduce DentAgent, an evidence-centric multi-agent framework, in which the Orchestrator coordinate five specialized agents spanning various modalities. Each specialist utilizes domain tools to convert observations into structured evidence records. The Evidence Blackboard manages these records as a shared evidence state, tracking coverage, gaps, and conflicts before response generation. This standardized evidence representation integrates isolated dental capabilities into a unified agentic workflow. Across four benchmarks, DentAgent demonstrates leading performance, even surpassing the senior specialists by 17.3 percentage points on multi-label diagnosis, which supports its value for broadly applicable and traceable multimodal dental reasoning, and highlights its potential as a technical foundation for population oral health assessment and management.

</details>


### [210] [Training-Free Inference-Time Self-Reflection and Cost-Bounded Early Stopping for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18884)
*Wei Yu,Suxing Liu,Minjie Yu,Jiahao Wang,Zhijian Zheng,Haocheng Deng,Bing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoResearcher，一种无需训练的推理时协议，通过向单个冻结LLM注入有成本约束的自我反思循环（生成→自批判→修订），使模型能够以零梯度更新获得自我验证能力，在固定推理预算内实现与完整训练管线相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练推理型LLM（如GRPO）成本高昂且需要可控环境，每一项贡献都必须投入完整的训练流程，导致研究迭代效率低下。作者旨在探索一种无需训练、推理时的替代方案，使单个冻结LLM即可获得自我反思与自我验证能力。

Method: EvoResearcher协议迭代执行生成→自我批判→修订的循环，直到达到最大深度D或批判返回CONFIRMED哨兵（隐式早停）。协议设计了四个自我反思元奖励组件（正确性、效率、反思深度、工具调用多样性）作为设计原则，以提示级机制实例化，无需任何梯度更新。在最大推理成本内让骨干网络自我验证答案。

Result: 在Big-Bench Hard（100题）上验证协议，并在GSM8K（500）和MATH（500）上建立跨领域行为，使用同一冻结骨干并在Qwen2.5-72B上复现。在干净BBH上准确率未提升至95% Wilson区间以上；核心价值在于成本约束的自我验证，CONFIRMED早停在相等准确率下终止了82-88%的样本，平均每问约2.1次生成。

Conclusion: EvoResearcher证明了无需训练即可获得成本受限的自我验证能力，通过早停机制高效终止推理过程，其价值不在于提升干净基准的准确率，而在于在固定计算预算内实现可靠的自我确认。工具调用多样性与多智能体扩展作为设计蓝图留给未来工作。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning training of reasoning LLMs (e.g., GRPO) is expensive and requires a controllable environment, committing every contribution to a full training pipeline. We present EvoResearcher, a training-free, inference-time protocol that adds cost-bounded self-reflection to a single frozen LLM backbone. The protocol iterates generate -> self-critique -> revise until a maximum depth D is reached or the critique returns the CONFIRMED sentinel, an implicit early stop that lets the backbone self-verify its answer under a strict compute budget. Four self-reflective meta-reward components (correctness, efficiency, reflection depth, tool-call diversity) act as design principles instantiated as prompt-level mechanisms, so their benefits accrue with zero gradient updates. We validate the protocol on Big-Bench Hard (100 questions) and establish cross-domain behavior on GSM8K (500) and MATH (500) on the same frozen backbone, with cross-model replication on Qwen2.5-72B. All experiments use pure-reasoning benchmarks; the tool-call diversity component is validated in prompt-level form, and the environment-level and multi-agent extensions are design blueprints left to future work. On clean BBH the protocol does not raise accuracy beyond the 95% Wilson interval; its value is cost-bounded self-verification, with the CONFIRMED early stop terminating 82-88% of items at equal accuracy (about 2.1 generations per question).

</details>


### [211] [Syntactic Simplification of OWL Class Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18899)
*Alkid Baci,N'Dah Jean Kouagou,Caglar Demir,Axel-Cyrille Ngonga Ngomo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CES（类表达式简化器）算法，用于在描述逻辑中对OWL类表达式进行句法简化，在保持形式语义不变的前提下，通过系统地应用重写规则消除冗余，生成更紧凑、更易读的等价表达式，从而提升推理效率并降低表达冗长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类表达式学习常常产生复杂难以解释和推理的OWL类表达式，这阻碍了本体驱动应用的可访问性、可维护性和可扩展性。因此需要一种基于理论基础上的简化原则来降低这种复杂性。

Method: CES算法在描述逻辑框架下对类表达式进行句法简化。它系统地应用重写规则来消除冗余，识别更简单但语义等价的表达式，在不改变逻辑蕴含的前提下生成更紧凑和人类可读的表示。

Result: 在两个中型本体上学习的类表达式上评估，CES展示了可测量的推理效率提升和冗长度降低。

Conclusion: 这项工作有助于使本体驱动应用更易访问、可维护和可扩展，对知识图谱构建、语义搜索和Web规模推理有直接意义。CES已作为开源Python框架OWLAPY的一部分公开提供。

Abstract: Class expression learning often produces complex OWL class expressions that are difficult to interpret and reason over. However, by following theoretically grounded simplification principles, this complexity can be reduced. In this paper, we propose Class Expression Simplifier (CES), a novel algorithm for the syntactic simplification of class expressions in Description Logics (DL). CES aims to preserve formal semantics while reducing representational complexity. It systematically applies rewriting rules to eliminate redundancies and identify simpler yet equivalent expressions, thereby producing more compact and human-readable representations without altering logical entailments. We evaluate the effectiveness of CES on class expressions learned from two medium-sized ontologies, demonstrating measurable improvements in reasoning efficiency and reductions in verbosity. This work contributes to the broader goal of making ontology-driven applications more accessible, maintainable, and scalable, with direct implications for knowledge graph construction, semantic search, and Web-scale reasoning. CES is implemented within the open-source Python framework OWLAPY and is publicly available.

</details>


### [212] [\textsc{TestifAI}: Tomography-Based Testing for Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18900)
*Arooj Arif,Tobias Hartung,Elena Botoeva,Alexandros Koliousis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TestifAI是一个深度学习测试框架，通过"部分模型断层扫描"技术，仅利用低阶扰动（1-2种）测试结果训练辅助模型，即可高效准确地估计高阶组合扰动（3-4种）下的模型鲁棒性，在五种图像和语言分类任务中实现鲁棒性估计误差小于7%，同时减少60-80%的推理次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在安全关键领域（如自动驾驶）的部署，深度学习模型的测试需求日益迫切。然而现有测试框架无法系统性地探索和总结组合扰动空间中的模型鲁棒性，因为穷举所有扰动组合需要指数级数量的测试，计算成本过高。

Method: TestifAI提出了"部分模型断层扫描"（partial model tomography）方法：用户可指定语义输入扰动（如图像模糊、亮度、缩放）及离散严重级别，系统仅运行涉及少量扰动（1-2种）的测试，训练一个辅助模型来预测高阶（3-4种）扰动组合下的测试结果，从而避免执行指数级数量的测试。

Result: 在五个图像和语言分类任务上的实验表明，TestifAI能够从低阶（1-2种）观测中预测高阶（3-4种）扰动测试结果，聚合鲁棒性估计误差低于7%，同时将推理次数减少60-80%。

Conclusion: TestifAI有效解决了组合扰动空间下鲁棒性测试的计算爆炸问题，以显著降低的计算成本实现了准确的鲁棒性估计，为安全关键场景中的深度学习模型测试提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly deployed in safety-critical application domains (e.g., autonomous driving), associated risks increase too. Deep learning models underlying modern AI systems, therefore, must undergo thorough testing to ensure their correct behaviour. A single robustness test involves thousands of inferences to empirically verify if a model's outputs remain stable under a bounded perturbation of its inputs. However, existing testing frameworks lack the means to systematically explore and summarise robustness across a combinatorial space of perturbations.
  We propose TestifAI, a deep learning testing framework for efficient and accurate estimation of robustness against combinations of perturbations. TestifAI enables users to specify operational conditions as structured spaces of semantic input perturbations (e.g., image blur, brightness and zoom) and discrete severity levels (e.g., low, medium and high). Users can query model robustness for any combination (e.g., "low blur, high brightness, and medium zoom"). To achieve efficiency and accuracy, TestifAI introduces partial model tomography, a novel approach to reconstructing model behaviour in a multi-perturbation space from tests that apply only a small number of perturbations (lower-order projections). To estimate robustness against at least three perturbations, TestifAI trains an auxiliary model on the results of tests involving up to two perturbations only, avoiding execution of an exponential number of tests. Our experiments on five image and language classification tasks show that TestifAI can predict higher-order (3 and 4 perturbations) test outcomes from low-order (1 and 2 perturbations) observations with an aggregate robustness estimation error of less than 7%, while reducing the number of inferences by 60-80%.

</details>


### [213] [Breaking the weakest link to evade vision language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18938)
*Ilan Zini,Boussad Addad,Katarzyna Kapusta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型（VLM）在面对针对视觉输入的对抗性扰动时的脆弱性，提出了一种仅在视觉编码器上进行梯度优化的高效攻击方法，在多个开源VLM（Qwen2.5-VL、Granite-Vision、FastVLM、Phi-3.5-Vision）上验证了微小的、人眼不可感知的扰动即可显著改变模型生成的文本语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM作为多模态AI系统的关键组件被广泛部署于现实世界和安全关键应用中，但对其对抗性鲁棒性的研究（尤其是针对多模态对齐的规避攻击）仍不充分。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度的攻击方法，仅对VLM的视觉编码器进行优化而非对整个多模态架构进行优化，以降低攻击的计算成本和资源需求。研究包括两种攻击设置：非定向攻击（破坏模型对原始图像的理解）和定向攻击（迫使模型生成与原始图像无关的特定语义描述）。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL、Granite-Vision、FastVLM和Phi-3.5-Vision等开源VLM上的实验表明，小幅且人眼不可感知的对抗性扰动即可显著改变模型生成的文本解释，验证了该方法的高效性和有效性。

Conclusion: 现代VLM在多模态对齐方面对抗性操纵极为脆弱，亟需在多模态AI系统中加强鲁棒性与安全机制。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a critical component of multimodal AI systems, enabling joint reasoning over visual and textual inputs in real-world and safety-critical applications. Despite their growing deployment, the robustness of VLMs against adversarial threats remains insufficiently explored, particularly in the context of evasion attacks targeting multimodal alignment. In this work, we investigate the vulnerability of VLMs to adversarial perturbations applied to visual inputs and study two attack settings: untargeted attacks, where the goal is to disrupt the model's interpretation of the original image, and targeted attacks, where the adversary aims to force the model to generate a specific semantic description unrelated to the original image. To efficiently generate adversarial examples, we propose a gradient-based attack method that performs optimization exclusively on the vision encoder of the VLM rather than on the entire multimodal architecture. This design significantly reduces the computational cost and resource requirements of the attack while maintaining strong effectiveness. We evaluate our approach on several open-source VLMs, including Qwen2.5-VL, Granite-Vision, FastVLM, and Phi-3.5-Vision, and show that small, human-imperceptible perturbations can substantially alter the textual interpretation produced by the models. Our findings highlight the vulnerability of modern VLMs to adversarial manipulation and emphasize the need for improved robustness and security mechanisms in multimodal AI systems.

</details>


### [214] [A Theory of Post-hoc Debate Judgement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19002)
*Xiang Yin,Adam Dejl,Antonio Rago,Lihu Chen,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验了一种适用于代理辩论场景的辩论评判理论，系统性地识别了辩论评判应满足的正式属性（可复现性、鲁棒性、扎实性和可解释性），并在声明验证任务中对两种评判方法（LLM作为评判者和计算论证形式语义）进行了形式化与实验性对比，结果表明两者准确率相近，但论证语义能提供LLM评判者所缺乏的正式保证，是辩论驱动AI中更具原则性的评判者候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辩论已成为提升代理AI性能、可解释性和用户参与度的有效方法，但辩论结果通常由外部裁判（常为LLM）事后决定，缺乏系统的评判理论指导，需要建立统一的正式框架来评估和比较不同辩论评判方法的可靠性。

Method: 作者首先形式化地定义了辩论评判应满足的四类性质（可复现性、鲁棒性、扎实性、可解释性），随后在声明验证场景中对两种评判方法——基于LLM的评判者变体与源自计算论证的正式语义——进行形式化推理和实验验证，比较二者在准确率及正式保证上的差异。

Result: 两种评判方法在准确率上表现相近，但基于LLM的评判方法可能缺乏正式保证，而计算论证语义能够提供这些保证。实验和理论分析共同表明论证语义是更符合原则的评判方法。

Conclusion: 计算论证语义是辩论驱动AI中理想的评判者方案，为辩论评判提供了可复现性、鲁棒性、扎实性和可解释性的正式基础，而纯LLM评判方法在这些方面存在局限。

Abstract: Debates have recently emerged as a useful methodology for agentic AI to improve performance as well as to aid explainability and user engagement. For example, LLM-empowered agents may debate internally (with themselves) and/or externally (with other agents). In many settings where debates are used, debates' outcomes and resulting outputs are determined post-hoc by external judges, often LLMs. In this paper we develop and test a novel theory of debate judgement applicable to all settings where agents engage in debates by providing pros and cons for their opinions therein. Specifically, we identify a number of formal properties that debate judgement may be required to satisfy in general, as concerns reproducibility, robustness, groundedness and explainability. Then, we explore their satisfaction formally and/or experimentally, for claim verification settings, for two specific alternative debate judgement methods: variants of the LLMs as a judge idea and formal semantics drawn from computational argumentation. We show that the two methods give similar accuracy performances but the former may lack formal guarantees that the latter brings. Overall, our study indicates argumentation semantics as an ideal candidate for principled judges in debate-driven AI.

</details>


### [215] [Self-prompting and cross-model consensus enable reproducible data extraction from scientific literature with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19025)
*Valentin Romanov,Monique Bax,Steven Niederer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿浏览器型大语言模型（LLMs）在从科研文章中提取高度上下文相关信息方面的表现，提出了四种递进式工作流，并最终定义了一种"专家设定证据标准、模型交叉核对、研究者裁决争议"的可审计分工模式，为规模化科学数据策展提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从科研文章中准确提取细微且上下文相关的数据既费时又费力，传统方法难以规模化。作者旨在探索前沿LLM能否胜任这一任务，并明确其能力边界与局限。

Method: 作者设计了四种递进式工作流进行系统评估：1）使用专家精心设计的提示词配合研究文章进行数据提取；2）仅给出简单指令，让LLM自行编写提示词；3）测试LLM自主发现研究文献的能力；4）让LLM基于已发布指南创建新的数据集，并与人类专家评判结果对比。

Result: ①在专家提示词下，多数前沿LLM数据提取表现良好，但在科学语境和细微差别的解读上存在困难；②LLM自编提示词的提取效果几乎与专家编写的提示词相当；③自主文献发现困难重重，智能体要么遗漏参考文献，要么产生幻觉性引用；④LLM可根据指南创建与人类专家评判高度吻合的数据集，但仍需人机协同（human-in-the-loop）。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种可审计的分工模式：专家负责指定证据标准，模型执行交叉核对式的重复提取，研究人员处理争议案例。这种模式在保留专家监督的同时，为大规模扩展科学数据策展提供了切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Accurately extracting nuanced, contextualized data from research articles is laborious and time intensive. Here, we investigate the performance of frontier, browser-based large language models (LLMs) to extract highly contextualized information. We demonstrate four escalating workflows, 1) given an expert curated prompt and research articles, most frontier LLMs perform well at data extraction, however can struggle with interpreting scientific context and nuance, 2) given simple instructions, LLMs can author their own prompts which were almost as eNective as expert-written prompts, 3) autonomous discovery of research literature was diNicult, agents either missed or hallucinated references, and 4) LLMs can create new datasets from published guidelines that closely match human-expert judges, but still require a human-in-the-loop. Together, these findings define an auditable division of labour in which experts specify the evidence standard, models cross-check repeated extractions and researchers resolve disputed cases, providing a practical route to scaling scientific data curation without relinquishing expert oversight.

</details>


### [216] [Adaptive Memory and Reflection Multi-Agent System for Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19029)
*Pradeep Murugesan,Luoxiao Yang,Xueli Chen,Xinqi Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR（自适应记忆与反思）智能体系统，一种多智能体医疗问答框架，通过专门的智能体使用独立记忆和反思反馈来检索先例并改进推理。复杂评估路由问题通过单独、协作或升级工作流处理，共识与伦理审查模块支持推理整合与输出审查。在MedQA和MedMCQA上表现优于多个基线，消融实验证明智能体记忆、反思与外部检索的组合最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗问答系统基于单智能体架构和静态检索，缺乏适应性、持久记忆和结构化决策能力。面对复杂医疗案例，需要事实知识和细致推理，而现有方法在这些方面存在明显不足。

Method: 提出AMR（Adaptive Memory and Reflection）智能体系统，采用多智能体框架：1）专门智能体配备专用记忆和反思反馈以检索相关先例并改进推理；2）复杂度评估模块将问题路由至单独协作或升级工作流；3）共识模块和伦理监督模块支持推理整合和输出审查。

Result: 在MedQA和MedMCQA基准测试上，AMR系统相比多个基线表现出更强性能。消融实验表明，结合智能体特定记忆、反思和外部检索的组合能获得最强性能。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆与反馈机制对开发更值得信赖的医疗智能体具有重要潜力。源代码已在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: Accurate and responsible medical question answering (QA) is important in healthcare, where complex cases require factual knowledge and nuanced reasoning. Existing medical QA systems, typically based on single-agent architectures and static retrieval, often lack adaptability, persistent memory, and structured decision-making. This work introduces an adaptive memory and reflection (AMR) agentic system, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents use dedicated memory and reflection-based feedback to retrieve relevant prior cases and improve subsequent reasoning. Complexity assessment routes questions through solo, collaborative, or escalated workflows, while consensus and ethical overseer modules support reasoning consolidation and output review. Evaluation on MedQA and MedMCQA demonstrates strong performance compared with several baselines. Ablation studies show that combining agent-specific memory, reflection, and external retrieval yields the strongest performance. These findings highlight the potential of structured memory and feedback for developing more trustworthy medical agents. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mm-air/AMR-Agent.

</details>


### [217] [Eureka: Task-Conditioned Meta-Agent Orchestration for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19047)
*Alizer Wong,Heng Cui,Yi Tan,Xiongchao Zhan,Liang Lin,Yuxiang Guo,Zhaorong Dai,Zixin Zeng,Wenyuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Eureka，一种任务条件化的元智能体架构，通过动态义务图（obligation graphs）将长时程任务编译为显式接受语义的执行流程。Eureka在运行中通过滚动时域规划、架构提升和最小充分编译形成具有专用状态、记忆、算子、工具、验证器和局部拓扑的宏智能体（Macro-Agents），并在瓶颈重复出现时通过成本-收益门控的演化机制在约束下更新局部架构。实验表明其在170/170递归任务上完全成功，生成3,948个证书且无误接受，大幅压缩上下文和计算冗余，并能实例化为理论发现智能体和数学/猜想智能体，分别在量子过程与时空理论及黎曼猜想相关数学问题中取得结构性和定量进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程、递归性科学任务对现有大模型智能体构成严峻挑战：单一固定架构难以匹配多样任务的认知结构，导致规划失效、上下文膨胀和计算冗余。作者旨在提出一种能够根据任务结构动态组织架构、并在执行中自我演化的元智能体框架，以验证科学智能体的能力不仅取决于基础模型，还取决于架构能否匹配任务的认知结构。

Method: Eureka采用三层核心机制：(1) 任务条件化编译——将长时程任务解析为带显式接受语义的动态义务图；(2) 滚动时域规划与架构提升——通过最小充分编译形成宏智能体，每个宏智能体拥有专用的状态、记忆、算子、工具、验证器和局部拓扑；(3) 成本-收益门控演化——当瓶颈反复出现时，在约束条件下对局部架构进行更新。理论上建立了关于遗憾上界、规划失效、摊销、子树接口、可串行化和验证等性质的形式化结果。

Result: 实验上，Eureka在170/170个递归任务上全部成功，生成3,948个无假接受证书；主动上下文压缩将输入中位数从9,490降至4,005 token；增量处理在12,000个任务中避免了65.38%的重复计算；16,000个并发执行保持一致的串行化。同一元智能体实例化为理论发现智能体（在量子过程和时空理论中获得结构性结果）和数学/猜想智能体（识别黎曼猜想研究的瓶颈，将Suzuki局部化Weil二次型的正性证书推进至0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345，达到(log 2)/2的约99.55%）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，科学智能体的效能高度依赖于架构与任务认知结构的匹配程度，而不仅仅是基础模型的能力。Eureka通过动态编译、局部演化和形式化保证，为构建可扩展、可验证的长时程科学推理智能体提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: We present Eureka, a task-conditioned Meta-Agent architecture that compiles long-horizon tasks into dynamic obligation graphs with explicit acceptance semantics. During execution, Eureka forms Macro-Agents with specialized state, memory, operators, tools, verifiers, and local topology via receding-horizon planning, architecture promotion, and minimal-sufficient compilation. When bottlenecks recur, cost-benefit-gated evolution updates the local architecture under constraints. Theoretically, we establish results on regret, planning invalidation, amortization, subtree interfaces, serializability, and verification. Experimentally, Eureka completes 170/170 recursive tasks and generates 3,948 certificates with no false acceptances. Active context compresses median input from 9,490 to 4,005 tokens; incremental processing avoids 65.38% recomputation across 12,000 tasks; 16,000 concurrent executions serialize consistently. The same Meta-Agent instantiates a Theory-Discovery Agent and a Math/Conjecture Agent. The former yields structural results in quantum-process and spacetime theory. The latter identifies bottlenecks in Riemann Hypothesis research and advances a positivity certificate for Suzuki's localized Weil quadratic form to 0 < a <= 69/200 = 0.345, reaching ~99.55% of (log 2)/2. These results suggest that scientific-agent capability depends not only on the base model but on whether an architecture can be formed to match the task's cognitive structure.

</details>


### [218] [What is Missing from AI Post-Training AI: An Empirical Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19072)
*Joy Jia Yin Lim,Xin Huang,Hao Peng,Yaxi Lu,Xin Cong,Zhong Zhang,Maosong Sun,Yankai Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析大量公开的post-training轨迹，发现LLM智能体在端到端训练中的策略选择在开始时即被锁定，后续所有预算都花在局部调整上。通过三种干预实验（经验驱动脚手架、人类引导、增加推理计算），作者证明智能体缺乏的不是经验、引导或推理算力，而是执行过程中自发重新评估策略的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可以端到端地post-train模型（写代码、启动训练、评估checkpoint），但作者指出当前研究混淆了两种不同能力：执行级别能力（在选定训练策略内迭代）与策略级别能力（随实验证据积累修订高层判断）。区分这两种能力对于理解AI-for-AI的真正潜力至关重要。

Method: 作者首先分析大规模公开的post-training轨迹语料，发现策略在训练开始时即被锁定。随后采用三种逐步升级的干预实验： (1) 经验驱动脚手架（提供更多经验）提升执行能力； (2) 人类引导（提供导向）重新定向初始策略； (3) 增加推理计算。通过对比这些干预的效果来检验三种候选解释。

Result: 实验结果显示：(1) 经验驱动脚手架在GSM8K上提升+12.6分、HumanEval上提升+40.8分，但策略仍然保持静态；(2) 人类引导能有效重新定向初始策略，但训练开始后智能体又陷入局部调整循环；(3) 额外推理计算在简单任务上有效，但对最难任务几乎无增益。

Conclusion: 智能体真正缺乏的不是经验、引导或推理算力，而是一种在执行过程中自发重新评估（spontaneously reevaluating）策略的机制。这是策略级别能力的核心缺失，也是未来AI智能体自主训练模型的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now post-train an LLM end-to-end. They can write code, launch training, evaluate checkpoints, and improve downstream performance, raising the prospect of AI-for-AI. We argue that this picture conflates two distinct capabilities: execution-level capability, iterating within a selected training strategy; and strategy-level capability, revising the high-level judgment as experimental evidence accumulates. Analyzing a large corpus of publicly released post-training trajectories, we find that across different tasks, the agent's training strategy is locked in at the very beginning, and the entire remaining budget is spent on local adjustments within the selected strategy. We then examine three natural explanations--missing experience, missing guidance, and insufficient reasoning--with escalating interventions. Extensive experiments show that (1) an experience-driven scaffold improves execution across the board (+12.6 points on GSM8K and +40.8 on HumanEval) but leaves the strategy static; (2) human guidance effectively redirects the initial strategy, yet the agent falls back into local adjustment loops once training starts; and (3) additional inference compute pays off on easier tasks but yields almost no gain on the hardest one. In conclusion, what agents lack is neither experience, guidance, nor reasoning compute, but a mechanism for spontaneously reevaluating their strategy during execution.

</details>


### [219] [Robust Risk Under Evolving Uncertainty: A Wasserstein Counterpart of the Entropic Value-at-Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19073)
*Deep Kumar Ganguly,Jan Křetínský*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein熵值风险（Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk），用最优传输球替代相对熵球来定义鲁棒风险度量，使代理能够对冲熵值风险度量认为"不可能"的可达灾难，并给出变分对偶、风险层级定位、闭合形式鲁棒动态规划算子及安全性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵值风险（entropic value-at-risk）通过鲁棒优化恒等式以置信水平固定相对熵球的半径，但该球无法覆盖名义模型认为不可能发生的灾难事件，而一个安全代理恰恰需要对这些事件进行对冲，因此需要更具一般性的风险度量。

Method: 用最优传输（optimal-transport）球替代相对熵球，研究其诱导的相干风险度量Wasserstein熵值风险。导出与熵公式对应的变分对偶（逆温度变为传输价格），确定其在风险层级中的位置，并从理论上证明其能覆盖熵度量忽略的可达灾难（同时数值验证两种对偶性）。进一步以信念熵驱动传输半径，构建闭合形式的鲁棒动态规划算子，其谨慎程度随信念收敛而收缩。

Result: Wasserstein熵值风险具有与熵公式镜像对应的变分对偶，在风险层级中占据明确位置，能涵盖熵度量无法触及的可达灾难；信念熵驱动的鲁棒动态规划算子具有闭合形式，并具备经过认证的安全性夹层（safety sandwich）和尖锐的安全性切换（safety switch）。

Conclusion: Wasserstein熵值风险为强化学习中的"无知谨慎、自信大胆"提供了理论坚实的风险度量基础，兼顾灾难对冲能力与计算可处理性，为安全学习代理提供了更强的保障机制。

Abstract: An agent still learning its environment should be cautious while ignorant and bold once confident. The entropic value-at-risk captures this through a robust-optimization identity---a confidence level fixes the radius of a relative-entropy ball of alternative models---but that ball cannot reach catastrophes the nominal deems impossible, precisely what a safe agent must hedge. We instead use an optimal-transport ball and study the coherent risk measure it induces, the Wasserstein entropic value-at-risk. It has a variational dual mirroring the entropic formula (an inverse temperature becomes a transport price), occupies a definite place in the risk hierarchy, and provably accounts for the reachable catastrophes the entropic measure ignores; we verify both dualities numerically. Driving the transport radius by belief entropy then yields a closed-form robust dynamic-programming operator whose caution contracts as the belief sharpens, with a certified safety sandwich and a sharp safety switch.

</details>


### [220] [Tuning the Stochastic Machine: A Systems Engineer's Operating Model for Human-AI Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19125)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从一个拥有三十年经验的系统工程师视角提出：LLM助手纠正错误后错误并未真正消除，这是因为缺乏系统化的"规则治理纪律"（版本溯源、复发监控、反指标、规则退役），而非工具缺乏。作者将LLM堆栈映射到传统机器架构，识别映射失败点，推导出七原则运行纪律，并展示了实际案例与测量框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家纠正LLM助手的错误后，纠正往往随会话结束而消失，同类错误会再次出现。作者认为这是一个运维（operations）问题而非工具问题——虽然存在持久化纠正的机制，但缺乏治理这些机制的纪律规范。

Method: 作者以三十年系统工程师经验，将LLM技术栈类比映射到传统机器体系（固化硅片、固件、可加载模块、持久配置、易失内存），识别映射失效点（随机生成、仅概率绑定的配置、默认无通用退役/验证阶段），并据此推导出以错误循环为核心的七原则运行纪律。

Result: 通过三个实际案例（包括一个控制机制悄无声息地变成它原本要防止的危害）演示了该机制，并提出了相应的测量框架及所需的实验室研究方案。

Conclusion: LLM错误纠正的持久化需要通过将传统系统工程中的版本溯源、复发监控、反指标和规则退役等运维纪律移植到LLM堆栈来实现，并以七原则运行纪律和测量框架作为支撑。

Abstract: When an expert corrects an LLM assistant's error, the correction usually dies with the session, and the error class returns. I argue this is an operations problem, not a tooling problem: mechanisms for persisting corrections exist and are shipping, but the discipline for governing them -- versioning with provenance, recurrence monitoring, counter-metrics, retirement of stale rules -- does not. Writing as a systems engineer of thirty years, I map the LLM stack onto the machines my profession already operates (frozen silicon, firmware, loadable modules, persistent configuration, volatile memory), identify where the mapping fails (stochastic generation, configuration that binds only probabilistically, no general-purpose retirement (verification) stage by default), and derive from the failures a seven-principle operating discipline with an error loop at its core. Three cases from my own practice illustrate the mechanism, among them a control that silently became the exact harm it was built to prevent. I close with the measurement framework this view implies and the lab study required to test it.

</details>


### [221] [Grouping the Stochastic Machine: Precision, Not Capability, as the Frontier Metric for AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19140)
*George Andrikopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"精度"(precision)而非"能力"(capability)才是前沿语言模型之间真正的区分维度。作者借鉴射手比喻，主张应测量同一请求下模型输出的集中度(弹着群大小)，而非平均落点。文章提出了一种低成本、无循环的测量方法(固定温度重复运行确定性打分任务并计算逐任务一致性)，并区分了"系统性偏移"与"分散性失败"两类问题，分别对应"瞄准调整"与"更换武器"两种解决路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前沿语言模型在能力(平均输出准确率)上已经饱和，中央趋势指标无法再区分系统优劣。论文认为基准文化系统性地忽略了散布(spread)这一维度，导致无法测量真正的系统间差异，也无法为实际部署中的决策提供指导。

Method: 提出"分组度量"(grouping metric)：在固定温度下，对一组确定性评分的任务多次重复运行，逐任务计算输出结果的一致性(而非用模型做评判器)。据此将失败分为两类——集中但偏移的失败(可用操作纪律即"瞄准调整"纠正)和分散的失败(需更换模型或采样策略即"弹药/武器问题")。同时设计了一个可在人-AI协作过程中持续追踪分组随时间变化的框架。

Result: 首次真实运行(已被复现)展示了方法的有效性及其关键局限：一个测量出的差距被单一规则完全弥补(0/5提升到5/5)；但基于该规则本身构造的任务套件没有发现额外价值，因为前沿模型已经内化了显式的最佳实践。这证明一个纪律的价值必须通过对真实工作的测量来确认，而非从它自己的规则手册构造而来。

Conclusion: 精度是比能力更具区分力的系统评价维度；该测量方法廉价、可操作且能指导决策，但其关键在于必须针对真实工作任务进行测量，而不能在规则自身的封闭循环中自我验证。

Abstract: Frontier language models are compared, marketed, and benchmarked on capability -- what their best or average output can achieve. I argue this measures the wrong axis. The models have saturated accuracy: their mean output lands on the target. What now separates one system from another in practice is precision: how tightly concentrated their outputs are around that target across repeated, identical requests. Borrowing the marksman's distinction, capability is where the average shot lands; reliability is the size of the group. I make three claims. First, precision, not capability, is the frontier differentiator between systems, and benchmark culture systematically fails to measure it, reporting central tendency rather than spread. Second, precision is measurable, cheaply and without circularity, by running a fixed suite of deterministically scored tasks many times at fixed temperature and computing the per-task consistency of outcomes -- no model-in-the-loop grader required. Third, the measurement is not merely descriptive but decision-guiding: it separates consistent failures (a tight group off-centre, correctable by the operating discipline of Paper 1 -- a sight adjustment) from scattered failures (a wide group, correctable only by changing the model or its sampling -- a rifle problem). I define a grouping metric, specify a harness, and show how tracking a human-AI pair's grouping over time yields the compounding signal that Paper 1's field study requires. A first real run, since replicated, illustrates both the method and its most important limit: one measured gap was closed completely by a single rule (0/5 -> 5/5), while a suite of tasks authored from the rules themselves found no value, because a frontier model already embodies explicit good practice -- establishing that a discipline's worth is found by measurement on real work, not constructed from its own rulebook.

</details>


### [222] [Beyond the Transcript: Detecting Covert Co ordination in Latent Multi-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19161)
*Ramneet Kaur,Pradyumna Chari,Ramesh Raskar,Jugad Singh,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Verifiable Latent Alignments (VLA)框架，用于监控和引导语言模型智能体之间的隐蔽隐状态通信通道，在拍卖基准上实现高AUROC监测性能和有效的白色盒干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体可以通过公共转录中不可见的连续隐状态进行通信，这为隐蔽的恶意协调创造了机会。目前缺乏能够监控和干预这些私有通信通道的框架。

Method: VLA框架包含三部分：(1) 无攻击样本的三层中性监控器，结合表征异常检测、反事实动作分布影响分析和稀疏自编码器解释支持；(2) 可转向性框架，涵盖黑盒行为指令和白盒匹配中性反事实；(3) 在可控多智能体拍卖基准上的评估，覆盖同构/异构模型对、多智能体规模及干预效果。

Result: 序列监控器在同构智能体上达到0.993的平均AUROC，异构对为0.854；在Qwen3-0.6B的25-100竞拍者拍卖场景中，全白色盒转向实现100%出价分布恢复，并将串通低价行为降低47.3个百分点。

Conclusion: VLA框架表明私有通道攻击可以在不针对攻击示例训练主监控器的情况下被有效监测，并在具备匹配反事实访问时得到缓解。全白色盒转向的精确恢复本身即为一种构建意义上的合理性检查。

Abstract: Language-model agents can communicate through continuous hidden states that are invisible in public transcripts, creating opportunities for covert harmful coordination. We introduce Verifiable Latent Alignments (VLA), an activation-aware framework for monitoring and steering these private communication channels. For every monitored decision, VLA links the private latent-state record and channel status to the resulting public action using a shared event identifier, enabling matched causal analysis. Our first contribution is a neutral-only three-layer monitor combining representation anomaly detection, counterfactual action-distribution influence, and sparse-autoencoder interpretation support. Our second contribution is a steerability framework spanning black-box behavioral instructions and white-box matched-neutral counterfactuals. Our third contribution is an evaluation on a controlled multi-agent auction benchmark covering homogeneous and heterogeneous model pairs, many-agent scalability, and intervention effectiveness. The sequential monitor achieves mean area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.993 for homogeneous agents and 0.854 for heterogeneous pairs when text- and latent-collusion rows are pooled as positives. In Qwen3-0.6B auctions with 25-100 bidders, monitoring requires only a small normalized load relative to all possible directed pairs, while full white-box steering achieves 100% bid-distribution recovery and reduces collusive low-bid behavior by 47.3 percentage points. Because full white-box steering replays the matched neutral counterfactual, its exact recovery is a sanity check by construction. Overall, the controlled study shows that the evaluated private channel attacks can be monitored without training the primary monitor on attack examples and mitigated when matched counterfactual access is available.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [Online Multi-Agent Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18241)
*Paul Dütting,Michal Feldman,Yoav Gal-Tzur,Thomas Kesselheim*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体合约模型的在线变体：智能体逐个到达（各自以一定概率活跃），委托人在每个智能体到达时提供线性合约，智能体在到达后达到纯纳什均衡。主要结果是：对次模奖励函数设计了O(1)-竞争比的在线策略（与离线最优相比），并证明该结果在两方面是紧的——若要求智能体当场决策则任何策略都是Ω(log n/(log log n)^2)-竞争的；对更广泛的XOS奖励类，任何在线策略都是Ω(log log n/log log log n)-竞争的，揭示了次模与XOS奖励类之间的惊人分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合约设计（contract design）是经济学和算法博弈论中的核心问题，而多智能体合约设定的在线变体尚无系统研究。作者旨在引入在线多智能体合约模型，探索在不确定性（随机到达、活跃概率）下委托人如何自适应地提供合约，以及不同奖励函数类别（次模与XOS）对可达竞争比的本质影响。

Method: 作者构建了在线合约模型，其中智能体以一定概率活跃并按顺序到达，委托人针对每个智能体给出线性合约α_i。技术路线包括：1）针对次模奖励设计O(1)-竞争的在线策略；2）通过构造对抗性实例证明"当场决策"约束下任意策略的下界为Ω(log n/(log log n)^2)；3）通过构造XOS奖励下的反例证明任意在线策略的下界为Ω(log log n/log log log n)，从而证明次模与XOS之间的竞争比差距。

Result: 主要结果为：次模奖励下存在O(1)-竞争比的在线策略，且该结果在两种意义上都是紧的。第一，若智能体必须当场决策（不能等待），则任何策略的竞争比为Ω(log n/log log n^2)；第二，对于XOS奖励，任何在线策略的竞争比为Ω(log log n/log log log n)。

Conclusion: 在线合约设定中，次模奖励可实现常数因子竞争比，但这一优势无法扩展到XOS奖励类。这与其他相关设定（如离线合约设计和先知不等式）不同——在那些设定中次模的常数保证可以无缝扩展到XOS，而在线合约设定则明确地将这两个奖励类区分开来，揭示了该问题特有的结构性分离。

Abstract: We introduce and study an online variant of the multi-agent contract model. In our model, agents arrive one-by-one and are active with a certain probability. Upon arrival of agent $i$, the principal offers a linear contract $α_i$, specifying the fraction of the principal's reward transferred to agent $i$. Agents can either exert effort or not, incurring a cost if they do. The set of agents that exert effort determines the principal's expected reward through a reward function $f$. After all agents have arrived, the agents form a (pure) Nash equilibrium. As our main result we design an $O(1)$-competitive policy for submodular rewards, compared to the offline optimum. We also show that this result is tight in two ways. First, if we require that agents make decisions on the spot, then for submodular rewards any policy is $Ω((\log n)/(\log \log n)^2)$-competitive. Second, for the broader class of XOS (a.k.a., fractionally subadditive) rewards, any online policy is $Ω((\log \log n)/(\log \log \log n))$-competitive. The latter result reveals a surprising separation between submodular and XOS rewards: unlike related settings such as offline contract design and prophet inequalities, where constant-factor guarantees for submodular rewards extend to XOS rewards, the online contract setting separates the two classes.

</details>


### [224] [Fair, Efficient and Connected Allocations on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18703)
*Susobhan Bandopadhyay,Anish Datta,Palash Dey,Ashlesha Hota,Abhishek Sahu*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究了图上的高效连通分配问题（包括平等主义与功利主义福利最大化）的经典复杂度与参数化复杂度。经典情形下两类问题在一般图及路径等受限图类上均为NP-hard，但在星图上可多项式求解。参数化框架下，以代理人数量为参数在树上取得FPT；功利主义版本对树宽和树深度均为FPT，平等主义版本在树深度为2时仍为para-NP-hard；连通分量数方面，除星图集合外问题保持困难，星图集合上平等主义为para-NP-hard，功利主义为W[2]-hard并存在XP算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分配问题（如公平分配资源）是计算社会选择与算法博弈论的核心问题。本文旨在系统刻画高效连通分配问题在经典与参数化两个复杂度框架下的计算复杂性边界，明确哪些图结构能使问题可解，哪些结构即使很受限也保持困难，从而为实际应用中的算法设计提供理论指导。

Method: 文章采用经典复杂度分析（NP-hardness归约与多项式算法）与参数化复杂度分析相结合的方法。具体包括：在路径、星图、不相交星图等结构上建立二分性；以代理人数量、树宽、树深度、连通分量数等为参数，分别研究FPT、W[2]-hard、para-NP-hard与XP算法的边界，并识别出连通图类的可解性可延伸至其不相交并集的鲁棒性现象。

Result: (1) 经典情形：两类问题在一般图、路径、树、环上均为NP-hard，在星图上多项式可解，但不适用于两个不相交星图。(2) 参数化情形：以代理人数为参数在树上为FPT；连通图类的可解性可推广至不相交并集。(3) 功利主义版本对树宽与树深度均为FPT，平等主义版本在树深度2时仍为para-NP-hard。(4) 以连通分量数为参数时，除星图集合外保持困难；星图集合上平等主义为para-NP-hard，功利主义为W[2]-hard并有XP算法。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了高效连通分配问题在图类与参数两个维度上的复杂度全景图：从经典NP-hard到FPT的可解边界，以及不同福利准则（平等主义与功利主义）之间显著的复杂度不对称性。这些结果为连通约束下的分配问题提供了精确的理论边界，并为后续算法设计指明了可行方向。

Abstract: We study the classical and parameterized complexity of efficient connected allocation problems on graphs, where efficiency is measured by egalitarian and utilitarian welfare maximization. We first establish a sharp complexity dichotomy in the classical setting: both problems are NP-hard in general and remain hard even on very restricted graph classes such as paths, and consequently trees and cycles. In contrast, they are polynomial-time solvable on stars, but this tractability does not extend even to the case of two disjoint stars. Motivated by these boundaries, we move to the parameterized complexity framework, where we study the problem with respect to the number of agents. We obtain fixed-parameter tractability (FPT) on trees and, more generally, identify a robust phenomenon whereby tractability on a connected graph class extends to disjoint unions of graphs from that class. We further investigate the parameters treewidth and treedepth, showing that the utilitarian version is FPT for both, whereas the egalitarian version remains para-NP-hard even on graphs of treedepth two. Finally, we analyze the number of connected components and show that except for the collection of stars, the problems remain hard. For the collection of stars,while we obtain para-NP-hardness for the egalitarian case, the utilitarian case gives W[2]-hardness together with an XP algorithm.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [Near-MDS codes of lengths q+6 and q+7 from conics in PG(2,q), q odd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18192)
*Sinan Donmez,Atilla Akpinar,Rumi Melih Pelen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对奇素数幂q，通过对PG(2,q)中圆锥曲线添加五个合适的点，构造了一族[q+6,3,q+3]近极大距离可分（NMDS）码，并完全确定了其重量分布；进一步通过添加第六个点得到[q+7,3,q+4] NMDS码。所有构造的码均为局部性为2的最优局部可修复码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMDS码在编码理论和密码学中具有重要价值，而维数3、长度n的NMDS码与PG(2,q)中的(n,3)-弧等价。该研究旨在通过几何方法构造新的NMDS码族，并与已有族进行比较和不变量区分，同时探索更长的NMDS码构造。

Method: 作者采用纯几何的方法：在PG(2,q)的圆锥曲线上添加五个点构造NMDS码，利用三割线简单计数恒等式分析点的几何结构；通过两个显式的二次特征条件区分三种不同的重量枚举子；对于[q+7,3,q+4]码的构造，结合精确和Weil型特征和估计与有限计算机验证；使用几何数据三元组作为不变量来证明新族与已有Fan-Wang-Xu族的不等价性。

Result: 对每个奇素数幂q构造了[q+6,3,q+3] NMDS码族，完全确定了重量分布（存在三种不同重量枚举子）；证明新族与Fan-Wang-Xu族不等价；对q>=11构造了[q+7,3,q+4] NMDS码并给出重量分布（q<=9时不存在可容许参数）；所有码都是局部性2的最优LRC码。

Conclusion: 通过圆锥曲线扩展的纯几何方法，成功构造了两族新的NMDS码并完全刻画了其重量分布，建立了相关的几何不变量用于区分不同码族，并将构造推广到更长的码长，为NMDS码与局部可修复码的理论提供了新的构造途径。

Abstract: Near maximum distance separable (NMDS) codes of dimension 3 and length n over the finite field with q elements are equivalent to (n,3)-arcs in PG(2,q). For every odd prime power q we construct, by adding five suitable points to a conic of PG(2,q), a family of [q+6,3,q+3] NMDS codes and determine their weight distributions completely; three distinct weight enumerators occur, governed by two explicit quadratic-character conditions on the parameters. For every odd prime power q, no code of our family is monomially equivalent to a code of the recent [q+6,3,q+3] NMDS family of Fan, Wang and Xu, even where the weight enumerators of the two families coincide: the separating invariant is a triple of geometric data attached to the underlying arc. Extending the configuration by a sixth point on a distinguished external line, we further obtain [q+7,3,q+4] NMDS codes, together with their weight distributions, for every odd prime power q >= 11 (admissible parameters exist for no q <= 9); the existence proof combines exact and Weil-type character sum estimates with a finite computer verification. Our proofs are purely geometric and rest on a simple counting identity for the trisecant lines of a point set obtained by extending a conic. All the codes constructed are optimal locally recoverable codes with locality 2.

</details>


### [226] [Second-Order Asymptotics for the Gaussian Multiple-Access Channel at Corner Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18231)
*Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了两用户高斯多址信道容量区域两个角点的二阶编码速率区域。对于任意平均错误概率ε∈(0,1)，作者表征了围绕每个角点的n^{-1/2}尺度速率波动，证明了与已知可达界匹配的逆命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯多址信道的容量区域已知，但其二阶（有限长）编码速率区域的精确刻画尚未完成。特别是角点附近的二阶速率波动缺乏紧的逆命题，需要新的数学工具来建立与可达界匹配的下界。

Method: 首先提取保持两个发射码字独立性的矩形子码；利用Polyanskiy--Verdú好码输出分布定理得到对数行列式约束，从而对修剪后的码本进行谱分解为扩散子空间和例外子空间；使用熵Brascamp--Lieb投影不等式控制扩散子空间上的码字内积，而采用方差膨胀高斯输出分布处理低维例外子空间；最后结合两种处理方法得到联合高斯极限。

Result: 在两个角点处精确刻画了任意平均错误概率ε∈(0,1)下的二阶编码速率区域，即n^{-1/2}尺度上的速率波动范围，并证明了与已知可达界相匹配的逆命题。

Conclusion: 通过谱分解与熵Brascamp--Lieb投影不等式的结合，成功建立了高斯多址信道角点处二阶速率区域的紧逆命题，完成了该场景下有限长编码理论的精确刻画。

Abstract: We establish exact second-order coding rate regions at the two corner points of the capacity region of the two-user Gaussian multiple-access channel. For any average error probability $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, we characterize the $n^{-1/2}$-scale fluctuations of achievable rates around each corner point, proving a converse that matches the previously known achievability bound. The proof first extracts a rectangular subcode that preserves the independence of the two transmitted codewords. The Polyanskiy--Verdú good-code output distribution theorem then yields log-determinant constraints that induce a spectral decomposition of a trimmed codebook into diffuse and exceptional subspaces. An entropic Brascamp--Lieb projection inequality controls the codeword inner product on the diffuse subspace, while variance-inflated Gaussian output distributions handle the low-dimensional exceptional subspace. Combining these two treatments yields the joint Gaussian limit required for the matching second-order converse.

</details>


### [227] [Quickest Change Detection in Parametric Models With 1-Bit Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18391)
*Liyan Xie,Martina Cardone*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究基于1比特量化观测的快速变化检测问题，提出自适应量化阈值CUSUM（AQuTe-CUSUM）算法，在线估计变化后分布的未知参数并自适应选择量化阈值以最大化Kullback-Leibler散度。在正则条件下，作者刻画了平均运行长度和最坏情况下的平均检测延迟，证明其在平均运行长度趋于无穷时一阶渐近最优，并在高斯和泊松分布上进行数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限或通信受限的场景中，传感器只能传输1比特量化数据而非完整的连续观测值，这给快速变化检测带来了挑战。已有的变化检测方法大多假设完全观测或已知的变化后分布，而本文处理的是后变化分布具有未知参数的1比特量化场景，同时需要联合设计量化阈值与检测统计量，这在实际应用（如无线传感器网络、物联网）中具有重要意义。

Method: 提出自适应量化阈值CUSUM算法（AQuTe-CUSUM）。该算法在线估计变化后的未知参数，并自适应地调整量化阈值以最大化诱导的Kullback-Leibler散度，从而增强检测能力。作者在合适的正则性条件下推导了该过程的平均运行长度和最坏情况平均检测延迟的刻画。

Result: 理论结果表明，当平均运行长度趋于无穷时，AQuTe-CUSUM过程在一阶意义上渐近最优。数值实验针对高斯分布和泊松分布两种典型场景验证了所提算法的性能。

Conclusion: 作者证明在1比特量化观测下，通过自适应选择量化阈值并在线估计未知参数，可以达到渐近最优的快速变化检测性能，为资源受限场景下的变化检测提供了理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: We consider the quickest change detection from 1-bit quantized observations, where the post-change distribution is a parametric model with unknown parameters and the quantization thresholds are jointly chosen with the detection statistic. We propose an Adaptive-Quantization-Threshold CUSUM (AQuTeCUSUM) algorithm, which estimates the post-change parameter online and adaptively selects the quantization threshold to maximize the induced Kullback-Leibler divergence. Under suitable regularity conditions, we characterize the average run length and worst-case average detection delay of the AQuTe-CUSUM procedure, and show that it is asymptotically optimal in first-order as the average run length goes to infinity. Finally, we assess the performance of AQuTe-CUSUM for two distributions, namely the Gaussian and Poisson.

</details>


### [228] [Joint Beamforming and Phase Shifts Design for RIS-Enabled RSMA-ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18458)
*Xuejun Cheng,Qian Zhang,Yuhui Jiao,Yufei Zhao,Zheng Dong,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究RIS辅助的RSMA-ISAC系统的感知中心设计，提出了一种新的波束增益近似方法以增强感知波束增益，并设计了一种基于约束分离的交替优化算法（CS-AO），实现所有子问题的闭式解，在保持与SCA和SDR基准相当性能的同时，分别实现120倍和50倍的运行时间缩减，且相比SDMA方案展现出显著的感知波束增益优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS赋能的RSMA-ISAC系统中，波束赋形向量与RIS相移的联合优化高度耦合且非凸，现有方法依赖通用优化求解器，计算复杂度高，难以满足实际部署的实时性要求。

Method: 提出一种新的波束增益近似方法以增强感知波束增益并满足通信QoS约束；针对非凸联合优化问题，提出约束分离的交替优化算法（CS-AO），通过解耦优化变量实现所有子问题的闭式求解，显著降低计算复杂度。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提算法在感知波束增益性能上与SCA和SDR基准相当，同时实现超过120倍和50倍的运行时间缩减；与SDMA方案相比，所提设计展现出显著的感知波束增益优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的CS-AO算法在保持感知性能的同时大幅降低计算开销，为RIS赋能的RSMA-ISAC系统提供了一种高效、实用的感知中心设计方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates the sensing-centric design of reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS)-enabled rate-splitting multiple access-integrated sensing and communication (RSMA-ISAC) systems. Specifically, we propose a new beam-gain approximation method to enhance the sensing beam gain while satisfying communication quality-of-service (QoS) constraints.Since the joint optimization of the beamforming vectors and RIS phase shifts is highly coupled and non-convex, existing methods typically rely on generic optimization solvers involving substantial computational complexity. To address this issue, we propose an efficient constraints-separation-based alternating optimization algorithm (CS-AO). Our proposed algorithm effectively decouples the optimization variables and yields closed-form solutions for all subproblems, thereby significantly reducing the computational burden. Simulation results show that the proposed algorithm achieves sensing beam-gain performance comparable to successive convex approximation (SCA) and semidefinite relaxation (SDR) benchmarks, while achieving more than 120-fold and 50-fold runtime reductions. In addition, compared with conventional space-division multiple access (SDMA) schemes, the proposed design exhibits substantial sensing beam gain.

</details>


### [229] [A Tutorial on Weight Structure of Polar Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18493)
*Mohamamd Rowshan,Vlad-Florin Dragoi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文是一篇教程，介绍极码权重结构背后的代数基础，通过单项式多项式形式说明极码和RM码可作为递减单项式码来研究，并为读者提供进入极码权重分布技术文献的准备知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极码的权重分布是一个重要但技术门槛较高的研究领域，缺乏可访问的入门材料。本文旨在以教程形式降低这一领域的理解门槛，帮助读者掌握仿射自同构、轨道描述和权重枚举等核心概念，以便深入阅读相关技术文献。

Method: 采用基于单项式的多项式形式体系，从代数的角度建立极码的结构框架；通过说明极码和RM码作为递减单项式码的统一视角，系统化地表征和枚举低权重码字；结合示例和递归/陪集技术的高层概述来讲解仿射自同构与轨道方法。

Result: 提供了一套完整的代数工具路径，使读者能够理解如何系统地表征和枚举低权重码字，并通过示例和高层概述为深入学习极码权重分布的现代文献做好铺垫。

Conclusion: 该教程作为通往极码权重分布前沿研究的桥梁，以可访问的方式建立了从代数基础到先进技术的知识框架，有助于降低该领域的学习曲线。

Abstract: This tutorial introduces the algebraic foundations underlying the weight structure of polar codes. Using a monomial-based polynomial formalism, we explain how polar and Reed-Muller codes can be viewed as decreasing monomial codes, enabling systematic characterization and enumeration of low-weight codewords. Its goal is to provide an accessible introduction to affine automorphisms, orbit-based descriptions of minimum and low-weight codewords, and their role in weight enumeration. Through illustrative examples and high-level overviews of recursive and coset-based techniques, the paper aims to prepare readers for deeper engagement with the recent technical literature on the weight distribution of polar codes.

</details>


### [230] [GCNO: Gramian Chebyshev Neural Operator for Physics-Based Compression of Wireless Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18522)
*Rafid Umayer Murshed,Shahab Hamidi-Rad,Elahe Soltanaghai,Akshay Malhotra*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出GCNO（Gramian Chebyshev神经算子），一种基于物理的可变速率信道压缩器，通过识别每个信道样本中少数主导传播路径的方向和强度来压缩大规模天线阵列信道，以更低的负载实现更高的重建精度，且无需重新训练即可迁移到不同的天线数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天线阵列虽能提升用户容量和数据速率，但信道反馈代价高昂——接收端需反复向基站报告大型复值信道矩阵。现有神经压缩器将矩阵视为图像，使用固定长度编码，导致码字无法适应信道复杂度变化，且天线数量改变时需要重新训练。作者希望设备仅报告每个信道背后的少数主导传播路径，实现自适应和可迁移的压缩。

Method: GCNO利用收发信道结构来定位传播路径，采用一阶泰勒修正来处理位于网格点之间的路径方向，并使用最小二乘法恢复路径的复值增益。该模型无需路径标签即可训练，基站通过解析方式从传输的路径元组重建信道，而非通过学习式解码器。

Result: 在三个射线追踪环境中，GCNO在相同负载下达到比神经反馈基线更好的重建精度，或在相同精度下使用更低的负载，并且无需重新训练即可迁移到未见过天线数量的场景。

Conclusion: GCNO证明了基于物理的可变速率信道压缩方案优于传统神经压缩方法，通过路径级表示实现了自适应的码字长度和对天线数量的零成本泛化，为大规模天线阵列信道反馈提供了一种更高效、更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Large antenna arrays allow wireless systems to serve more users and achieve higher data rates, but they also make channel feedback expensive: the receiving device must repeatedly report a large complex-valued channel matrix to the base station. Most neural compressors treat this matrix like an image and replace it with a fixed-length code that only a matched neural decoder can interpret. The message therefore does not adapt to channel complexity, and changing the antenna count typically requires retraining. We ask whether a device can instead report only the few dominant propagation paths underlying each channel. We introduce the Gramian Chebyshev Neural Operator (GCNO), a physics-based, variable-rate compressor that identifies a sample-dependent set of path directions. GCNO uses receive-transmit channel structure to locate paths, a first-order Taylor correction to refine directions that fall between grid points, and least squares to recover their complex strengths. It is trained without path labels, and the base station reconstructs the channel analytically from the transmitted path tuples rather than through a learned decoder. Across three ray-traced environments, GCNO achieves better reconstruction accuracy at the same payload - or lower payload at the same accuracy - than neural feedback baselines, and transfers to unseen antenna counts without retraining.

</details>


### [231] [Constructing Good Abelian Codes via Shift Bounds and Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18664)
*Cong Yu,Hao Chen,Zhonghua Sun,Shixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域上通过阿贝尔码构造线性码的方法，利用多元多项式商环的代数结构，通过推广的移位界（推广了经典van Lint-Wilson界）推导最小距离的下界。作者显式构造了若干无限族阿贝尔码（包括二元和三元情形），并引入遗传算法在定义集（用分圆陪集的二进制染色体表示）上搜索，以最小距离与最优已知线性码（BKLC）界的比较作为适应度函数。搜索在F_3和F_4上发现了多个破纪录码，超越Grassl表。此外，嵌套结构使Construction X得以应用，进一步获得参数更优的线性码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索阿贝尔码作为构造线性码的推广框架，利用其比循环码更丰富的代数结构，结合启发式搜索发现参数未知或超越已知最优的线性码，弥补传统循环码构造的局限性。

Method: (1) 基于多元多项式商环的代数结构，定义广义移位界以推导阿贝尔码最小距离的下界；(2) 显式构造二元和三元无限族阿贝尔码；(3) 设计遗传算法，将定义集编码为分圆陪集的二进制染色体，以最小距离与BKLC界的比较作为适应度函数进行搜索；(4) 利用码的嵌套结构应用Construction X进一步获得更优参数。

Result: 在F_3和F_4上搜索到多个破纪录码，参数优于Grassl表中的已知最优码；通过Construction X从嵌套阿贝尔码中额外获得若干参数改进的线性码。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔码结合启发式搜索算法是发现具有未知或更优参数线性码的有效途径，推广了基于循环码的经典构造框架。

Abstract: This paper investigates the construction of linear codes via abelian codes over finite fields. By exploiting the algebraic structure of multivariate polynomial quotient rings, we derive lower bounds on the minimum distance using a generalized shift bound, which extends the classical van Lint-Wilson bound for cyclic codes. Several infinite families of abelian codes are explicitly constructed, including binary and ternary cases that extend previously known cyclic constructions. To find more abelian codes with good parameters, we apply a genetic algorithm that searches over defining sets represented as binary chromosomes of cyclotomic cosets; the fitness function compares the computed minimum distance against the best known linear code (BKLC) bounds. The search yields multiple record-breaking codes over F_3 and F_4, with improvements over Grassl's tables. Furthermore, the nested structure of these codes enables the application of Construction X, yielding additional linear codes with improved parameters. The results demonstrate that abelian codes, combined with heuristic search, form a viable way for discovering linear codes with unknown parameters.

</details>


### [232] [Joint Power Allocation and Phase-Shift Design for Beyond-Diagonal Stacked Intelligent Metasurfaces-Aided ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18713)
*Yuhui Jiao,Qian Zhang,Xuejun Cheng,Meihui Liu,Jiancheng An,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种超越对角堆叠智能超表面（BD-SIM）架构用于集成感知与通信（ISAC），通过可重构阻抗网络实现可控的层内耦合，增强波域处理灵活性，并开发了适用于全连接、群连接和对角SIM架构的统一交替优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对角SIM仅提供逐元素的相位控制，在平衡多用户通信与感知性能时可能需要额外的层级，导致硬件开销增加和性能受限。

Method: 提出BD-SIM架构，通过可重构阻抗网络实现层内耦合控制；开发统一的交替优化框架（适用于全连接、群连接和对角SIM架构）；推导闭合形式的功率分配规则；提出用于多层相移设计的高效变量分离算法。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的BD-SIM在通信-感知权衡方面表现更优，并且达到与传统SIM相当的性能所需的层数更少。

Conclusion: BD-SIM通过引入层内耦合控制，在保持少量射频链的前提下，有效提升了ISAC系统中的波域处理灵活性，改善了通信与感知性能的权衡，并减少了所需层级数。

Abstract: Stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM) provide an efficient architecture for integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) with few radio-frequency (RF) chains. However, diagonal SIM provide only element-wise phase control, so balancing multiuser communication and sensing performance may require additional layers. In this letter, we propose a beyond-diagonal SIM (BD-SIM) architecture for ISAC, enabling controllable intra-layer coupling through reconfigurable impedance networks, thereby enhancing wave-domain processing flexibility. We develop a unified alternating optimization framework applicable to fully-connected, group-connected, and diagonal SIM architectures. Within this framework, we derive a closed-form power allocation rule and propose an effective variable separation algorithm for multi-layer phase-shift design. Simulation results show that the proposed BD-SIM achieve a better communication-sensing trade-off and require fewer layers to attain performance comparable to conventional SIM.

</details>


### [233] [A Framework for Enterprise Network Dimensioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18796)
*Gourab Ghatak*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对室内企业网络中的无线节点（RN）部署问题，提出了一种将随机几何（SG）、整数线性规划（ILP）和聚类算法（特别是加权k-调和均值WKHM及顺序最小割SeqMinCut）相结合的全新框架，用于推导SINR的元分布（MD）、估计RN数量、初始化RN位置并合并小区以优化SINR，是首个整合SG统计分析与优化聚类的企业5G系统设计研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内企业网络中的RN部署面临双重挑战：SG能估计所需RN数量但无法给出具体位置，而ILP虽能优化位置却计算代价高且容易陷入次优局部解。因此需要一种既能准确估计RN数量又能高效确定其位置的综合方法。

Method: 作者首先利用随机几何（SG）推导测试用户设备（UE）在有/无室外宏基站（MBS）协作情况下的SINR元分布（MD），并与ILP结果对比。在此基础上，提出基于UE位置分布的聚类算法初始化RN位置，其中核心创新是加权k-调和均值（WKHM）聚类策略，旨在最大化SINR；随后引入带约束的顺序最小割算法SeqMinCut，将多个RN合并为更大的小区以进一步提升SINR。

Result: 该方法同时克服了SG无法给出RN具体位置和ILP计算复杂度过高、易陷入局部最优的缺陷，实现了RN数量估计、位置初始化与小区合并的联合优化，在计算效率和SINR性能之间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究首次将随机几何统计建模、整数线性规划优化与聚类算法三者融合，为企业5G室内网络的系统设计提供了维度规则、规划策略和关键设计洞察。

Abstract: We study radio node (RN) placement for indoor enterprise networks. Using stochastic geometry (SG), we derive the meta-distribution (MD) of the SINR for a test user equipment (UE), with and without cooperation from outdoor macro base stations (MBSs), and compare these results with an integer linear programming (ILP) approach. SG provides an estimate of the required number of RNs but not their locations, while ILP can yield inaccurate local optima and requires high computational power. To address this, we investigate clustering-based algorithms for initializing RN locations using UE location distributions. Along with standard methods, we propose a weighted $k$-harmonic means (WKHM) clustering strategy tailored to maximize SINR. We then introduce a constrained sequential minimum cut algorithm, \texttt{SeqMinCut}, to merge multiple RNs into larger cells and further improve SINR. This is the first work that integrates SG-based statistical analysis, optimization, and clustering to obtain system design insights, dimensioning rules, and planning strategies for enterprise 5G.

</details>


### [234] [Integrated Sensing and Communications over Hierarchical Cellular and Cell-Free MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18873)
*Wei Jiang,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究混合蜂窝与分布式无小区接入点的集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统，提出一种分层ISAC架构：中央基站同时服务近端用户并作为单站雷达探测空中目标，而空闲的分布式接入点充当低成本双站接收器，实现了比传统无小区ISAC更高的吞吐量和更好的感知精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无小区ISAC需要所有接入点参与通信与感知处理，导致前传开销巨大；同时分布式接入点大量空闲未被利用。本文旨在构建一种分层架构，利用中央基站统一处理通信与多站感知融合，降低前传负担，同时借助空闲接入点提升感知性能。

Method: 提出一种分层ISAC架构：中央基站（CBS）既服务近端用户又作为单站雷达探测空中目标，通过用户中心式聚类下大量空闲的分布式接入点作为无额外成本的双站接收器。CBS统一处理通信和多站感知融合。设计了五阶段时分双工（TDD）工作流程以精确分配ISAC角色，并推导了频谱效率和多站感知信噪比的闭式表达式，用以刻画通信-感知帕累托前沿。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提出的分层设计相比传统无小区ISAC，同时实现了更高的总吞吐量和更优的感知精度。

Conclusion: 本文的分层混合ISAC架构在降低前传开销的同时，利用空闲分布式接入点实现了比传统无小区ISAC更优的通信与感知性能权衡，展示了混合蜂窝与无小区系统在ISAC应用中的潜力。

Abstract: This paper studies integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) over a hybrid system that seamlessly combines legacy cellular base stations with distributed cell-free (CF) access points (APs). We propose a hierarchical ISAC architecture where a central base station (CBS) serves its near users and simultaneously operates as a monostatic radar for aerial target detection, while distributed APs---many idle under user-centric clustering---act as cost-free bistatic receivers. The CBS jointly handles communication processing and multi-static sensing fusion, reducing fronthaul overhead compared to conventional cell-free ISAC. To achieve this, a five-phase time-division duplexing workflow with precise ISAC role assignment is specified. Closed-form expressions for spectral efficiency and multi-static sensing signal-to-noise ratio analytically characterize the communications--sensing Pareto frontier. Numerical results confirm that the proposed hierarchical design simultaneously achieves higher sum throughput and superior sensing accuracy than conventional cell-free ISAC.

</details>


### [235] [New Constructions of Additive MDS TRS Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18904)
*Anuj Kumar Bhagat,Ankit Yadav,Ritumoni Sarma*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上的加法扭转Reed-Solomon（TRS）码，针对twist t=2和twist向量(1,2)分别给出加法MDS码的充要条件，并通过Schur平方技术证明这些码与加法RS码不等价，同时给出奇偶校验矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加法码是线性码的推广，加法MDS码是线性MDS码的自然延伸。已有工作（Jiayu Ma等人）构造了t=2、h=k-1情形的加法非RS型MDS码，但其他参数情形的加法MDS码尚未被系统研究，且需要探索能产生更大码长的构造。

Method: (1) 针对twist t=2且hook任意的加法TRS码，建立加法MDS的充要条件，推广已有结论；(2) 针对twist向量t=(1,2)、hook向量h=(0,0)的加法TRS码，推导加法MDS的充要条件并证明存在性；(3) 利用Schur平方技术给出构造族与加法RS码不等价的温和条件；(4) 确定两类加法MDS码的奇偶校验矩阵。

Result: 获得了t=2情形下加法TRS码为加法MDS的更一般充要条件，且h=0时码长大于已有h=k-1情形的码长；同时证明了t=(1,2)、h=(0,0)情形下加法MDS码的存在性，并通过Schur平方方法得到与加法RS码不等价的充分条件，并给出了两族码的奇偶校验矩阵。

Conclusion: 本文系统推广了加法TRS码的MDS构造，覆盖了更广泛的参数范围并获得更大码长的新族加法非RS型MDS码，为加法MDS码的研究提供了新的构造途径和理论保障。

Abstract: Additive codes over finite fields generalize linear codes, and additive MDS codes provide a natural extension of linear MDS codes. In this article, we study additive twisted Reed--Solomon (TRS) codes and obtain new constructions of additive MDS codes. First, for additive TRS codes with twist $t=2$ and an arbitrary hook, we establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the codes to be additive MDS, thereby generalizing the results in Section 3 of [Jiayu Ma et al., New families of additive non-Reed-Solomon MDS codes]. In particular, we show that the existence of an additive MDS TRS code with $t=2$ and hook $h=0$ yields codes of larger lengths than those obtained for $t=2$ and $h=k-1$ in [Jiayu Ma et al., New families of additive non-Reed-Solomon MDS codes]. Next, we consider additive TRS codes with twist vector $\mathbf{t}=(1,2)$ and hook vector $\mathbf{h}=(0,0)$, and derive necessary and sufficient conditions for them to be additive MDS. We further establish the existence of such codes. Using the Schur square technique, we obtain mild conditions under which the constructed families are inequivalent to additive Reed--Solomon (RS) codes. Finally, we determine parity-check matrices for both families of additive MDS codes considered in this article.

</details>


### [236] [The Equality Cases of the Weak Simplex Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19093)
*Mengwei Su,Kaiwen Yang,Hao Xu,Chih-Lin I*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文确定了Shannon信号几何问题中等号成立的全部情况：除正则单纯形外，任何信号集在任意正信噪比下都严格低于正则单纯形给出的正确解码概率上界。由此推出若干刚性结论——匹配该界一次即意味着信号集必为正则单纯形（至多相差顶点重标号和正交映射）；相关矩阵中除单位阵外，任何矩阵在任何有限阈值下其下正交概率都严格高于独立情形；能量预算下唯一最优码本为正则单纯形；Simplex Mean Width猜想中的等号也仅在正则单纯形处成立。证明通过Royen相关定理强化了Mulgund论证的第一步自卷积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shannon在1950年提出：在n+1个等概等能量信号、加性高斯白噪声和最大似然解码下，何种排列使正确解码概率最大化？Mulgund（2026）证明了正则单纯形值对任意信号集和任意信噪比都构成上界，但遗留了最优化唯一性问题。本文旨在完全刻画等号情形，回答这个开放问题。

Method: 证明的核心是强化Mulgund论证中的第一步自卷积操作：采用Royen相关定理来处理相关矩阵的显式相关性。关键洞见是：若信号集的相关矩阵非单位阵，则通过Royen定理可得到其下正交概率严格大于独立情形的对应概率，从而在任意有限阈值下都严格低于正则单纯形的上界。整个单参数刚性论证在Lean 4证明助手中完成了机器验证。

Result: 获得了比唯一性强得多的等号情形刻画：每个非正则单纯形的信号集在每一个正信噪比下都严格低于上界；因此一个码只需在一个正工作点上达到上界，就足以推出其必为正则单纯形（至多差顶点重标号和正交映射）。相关矩阵层面，除单位阵外任何矩阵给出的下正交概率在任何有限阈值下都严格高于独立对应值，即完全没有平凡等号的空间。环境维度低于n的码无一能达到该界。能量预算E下无块长限制时唯一最优码本为正则单纯形（外接半径√E），每个最优码字都耗尽全部能量。Simplex Mean Width猜想中的等号同样只出现在正则单纯形。

Conclusion: 本文彻底解决了Shannon问题的等号刻画，确立了正则单纯形不仅是正确解码概率的上界，更是唯一的最优构型（在等价意义下）。这一刚性结果同时为相关矩阵的下正交概率比较、有限能量最优码本设计以及Simplex Mean Width猜想提供了统一且严格的答案，并用Lean 4做了形式化机器验证。

Abstract: Among $n+1$ equiprobable equal-energy signals in $\R^n$ under additive white Gaussian noise with maximum-likelihood decoding, which arrangement maximizes the probability of correct decoding? The question is Shannon's, recorded by Rice in 1950. Mulgund proved in 2026 that the regular-simplex value bounds the correct-decoding probability of every signal set at every signal-to-noise ratio, leaving open whether the simplex is the only maximizer.
