This paper determines the equality cases in a form stronger than uniqueness. A signal set other than a regular simplex falls strictly below the bound at every positive signal-to-noise ratio. Hence a code meeting the bound at one positive operating point is already a regular simplex, up to vertex relabeling and an orthogonal map. In probabilistic form, among the correlation matrices that signal sets induce, any matrix other than the identity gives a lower-orthant probability strictly above its independent counterpart at every finite threshold, leaving no room for a nontrivial equality. No code of ambient dimension below $n$ attains the bound. Under an energy budget $E$ with unrestricted blocklength the optimal codebook is uniquely the regular simplex of circumradius $\sqrt{E}$. Every optimal codeword therefore exhausts its allowance. Equality in the Simplex Mean Width Conjecture likewise occurs only at the regular simplex. The proof strengthens the first self-convolution step of Mulgund's argument with Royen's correlation theorem. The single-parameter rigidity is machine-checked in Lean 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [237] [Multi-Objective Optimization Under Uncertainty of Part Quality in Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18429)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Paromita Nath,Matthew Sato,Sankaran Mahadevan,Paul Witherell*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动的方法，用于熔融沉积成型（FFF）工艺参数的多目标不确定性优化。通过构建贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）模型预测几何偏差和丝材粘合质量，综合考虑模型认知不确定性和输入随机性，在不同稳健性设计优化公式下求解多目标问题，并构建Pareto曲面以权衡各目标，最终通过实际制造验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FFF工艺中，工艺参数（如喷嘴温度、喷嘴速度和层厚）对零件质量（几何精度和粘合质量）有显著影响，且现实中存在工艺参数输入的不确定性及预测模型参数的不确定性。传统确定性优化方法无法充分处理这些不确定性，需要一种能够量化并纳入不确定性因素的多目标优化方法，以更稳健地确定最佳工艺参数组合。

Method: 首先进行实验以收集零件质量数据；然后构建贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）模型，将几何偏差和粘合质量建模为工艺参数的函数，该模型同时捕捉由神经元权重未知导致的认知不确定性和输入参数固有随机性引起的偶然不确定性；接着基于BNN的随机预测结果，研究不同的基于稳健性的设计优化公式，将喷嘴温度、喷嘴速度和层厚作为优化变量，针对不同的多目标场景进行不确定性下的优化；最后构建Pareto曲面以评估各目标间的权衡关系。

Result: 通过实际制造零件对BNN模型及所提出的优化方法的有效性进行了验证，成功获得了多目标场景下的Pareto前沿，实现了在不确定性条件下对FFF工艺参数的稳健优化。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据驱动优化方法能够有效地在FFF工艺中同时考虑认知不确定性和偶然不确定性进行多目标优化，通过BNN建模和Pareto分析提供了工艺参数选择的稳健决策支持。

Abstract: This work presents a data-driven methodology for multi-objective optimization under uncertainty of process parameters in the fused filament fabrication (FFF) process. The proposed approach optimizes the process parameters with the objectives of minimizing the geometric inaccuracy and maximizing the filament bond quality of the manufactured part. First, experiments are conducted to collect data pertaining to the part quality. Then, Bayesian neural network (BNN) models are constructed to predict the geometric inaccuracy and bond quality as functions of the process parameters. The BNN model captures the model uncertainty caused by the lack of knowledge about model parameters (neuron weights) and the input variability due to the intrinsic randomness in the input parameters. Using the stochastic predictions from these models, different robustness-based design optimization formulations are investigated, wherein process parameters such as nozzle temperature, nozzle speed, and layer thickness are optimized under uncertainty for different multi-objective scenarios. Epistemic uncertainty in the prediction model and the aleatory uncertainty in the input is considered in the optimization. Finally, Pareto surfaces are constructed to estimate the tradeoffs between the objectives. Both the BNN models and the effectiveness of the proposed optimization methodology are validated using the actual manufacturing of the parts.

</details>


### [238] [Distribution-Agnostic Isocontour Confidence Bounds for Robust Uncertainty Visualization of Scalar Field Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18220)
*Timbwaoga A. J. Ouermi,Nina M. Gottschling,Alex Gorczowski,Tushar M. Athawale*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hoeffding不等式的稳健、分布无关的等值线置信带方法，用于标量场特征的不确定性可视化。该方法通过顶点级Hoeffding置信界传播生成等值线置信带，在合成和真实集成数据集上表现出松但准确的置信界，能够捕捉Gaussian和bootstrap方法可能遗漏的真实值，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性可视化方法存在不足：均值等值线缺乏空间不确定性指示，spaghetti图易混乱；基于Gaussian分布假设和非参数bootstrap的方法虽能提供紧凑无杂乱的置信界，但在样本数量有限的集成数据中可能低估不确定性，从而产生具有误导性的不确定性界。

Method: 提出分布无关的Hoeffding置信带方法，作为现有技术的补充而非替代。具体包括：(1) 利用Hoeffding不等式构造逐顶点的置信界；(2) 将这些顶点级置信界传播生成等值线置信带。

Result: 在合成和真实集成数据集上的实验表明：Hoeffding置信带较为宽松(loss)，但能准确捕获Gaussian和bootstrap替代方法可能遗漏的真实值，同时保持计算效率。

Conclusion: Hoeffding置信带作为一种分布无关的补充技术，能够缓解基于分布假设方法可能产生的误导性不确定性界，尤其在样本量有限的集成场景下更具可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertainty visualization has been shown to be pivotal for conveying the reliability of features extracted from scalar fields. Features represented by isocontours, mean isocontours lack an indication of spatial uncertainty, whereas spaghetti isocontour plots can become cluttered and difficult to interpret. Existing methods relying on specific distribution assumptions, such as Gaussian and nonparametric bootstrap, to provide compact clutter-free spatial confidence bounds but may underestimate uncertainty for ensembles with a limited number of samples. We introduce a robust, distribution-agnostic Hoeffding confidence band as a novel complementary (and not competitive) technique to mitigate potentially misleading uncertainty bounds that may arise from distribution-based assumptions. The approach constructs vertex-wise confidence bounds using Hoeffding's inequality and propagates them to generate isocontour confidence bands. Results on synthetic and real ensemble datasets show that the Hoeffding confidence bands are loose but accurately capture underlying true values that may be missed by the Gaussian and bootstrap alternatives while remaining computationally efficient.

</details>


### [239] [Process Optimization Under Uncertainty for Improving the Bond Quality of Polymer Filaments in Fused Filament Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18431)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Matthew Sato,Sankaran Mahadevan,Paul Witherell*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个计算框架，在熔融沉积成型（FFF）工艺中优化工艺参数以最大化挤出聚合物丝材之间的粘结质量。该框架将瞬态传热分析与烧结颈生长模型耦合，评估相邻丝材界面的粘结质量，并系统量化粘结质量模型预测中的不确定性和模型偏差，从而在考虑不确定性的情况下进行工艺参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FFF工艺中，预测工艺变异性对于实现制造零件的主动质量控制至关重要，但现有模型受假设和近似的影响，存在不确定性和模型偏差。需要系统量化这些不确定性来源（包括随机性和认知性），并将其纳入工艺参数优化中，以确保零件粘结质量。

Method: 1) 将瞬态传热分析与烧结颈生长模型耦合以评估丝材间粘结质量；2) 采用基于Sobol指标的方差敏感性分析量化不同不确定性来源对粘结质量的相对贡献；3) 构建高斯过程（GP）代理模型来计算并纳入模型偏差；4) 通过物理实验对物理模型进行校准和验证，并验证最优解。

Result: 结果表明，所提出的考虑不确定性的工艺参数优化方法能够使FFF产品的相邻丝材之间获得较高的粘结质量，同时有效处理了随机性和认知性不确定性以及模型偏差的影响。

Conclusion: 本文提出的不确定性量化与工艺参数优化框架能有效提升FFF工艺中零件粘结质量，为制造过程的前瞻性质量控制提供了系统化的计算工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a computational framework to optimize the process parameters such that the bond quality between extruded polymer filaments is maximized in fused filament fabrication (FFF). A transient heat transfer analysis providing an estimate of the temperature profile of the filaments is coupled with a sintering neck growth model to assess the bond quality that occurs at the interfaces between adjacent filaments. Predicting the variability in the FFF process is essential for achieving proactive quality control of the manufactured part; however, the models used to predict the variability are affected by assumptions and approximations. This paper systematically quantifies the uncertainty in the bond quality model prediction due to various sources of uncertainty, both aleatory and epistemic, and includes the uncertainty and the model discrepancy in the process parameter optimization. Variance-based sensitivity analysis based on Sobol indices is used to quantify the relative contributions of the different uncertainty sources to the uncertainty in the bond quality. A Gaussian process (GP) surrogate model is constructed to compute and include the model discrepancy within the optimization. Physical experiments are conducted for calibration and validation of the physics model and also for validation of the optimum solution. The results show that the proposed formulation for process parameter optimization under uncertainty results in high bond quality between adjoining filaments of the FFF product.

</details>


### [240] [Multi-Level Bayesian Calibration of a Multi-Component Dynamic System Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18430)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Sankaran Mahadevan,Shunsaku Matsumoto,Yoshitomo Miyagi,Daigo Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多层级贝叶斯校准方法，用于融合异质信息源并量化建模与测量中的不确定性，适用于时变多组件系统，并通过燃气涡轮发动机转子叶片的热力学行为案例验证了离线与在线两种校准场景的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变多组件系统中存在组件级和系统级两类参数，且来自不同组件、不同时间点的异构测量数据被分散采集，传统校准方法难以同时有效处理跨组件、跨时间的不确定性融合以及两级参数的协同校正问题。

Method: 开发了多层级（multi-level）贝叶斯校准方法，包含两个核心要素：（1）通过融合所有可用信息量化组件级和系统级的不确定性；（2）校正模型预测。该方法按顺序消费在不同时间实例下不同系统组件获得的异构测量数据，并采用迭代策略在两级之间进行参数校准。该方法被实现为离线和在线两种场景——离线使用全部时间步的累积数据进行校准，在线则在每个时间步获取新测量数据时进行实时校准。

Result: 以燃气涡轮发动机转子叶片的热力学行为作为分析模型和观测数据来源，验证了所提出的多层级贝叶斯校准方法在离线与在线两种场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 多层级贝叶斯校准方法能够有效融合异构信息源，在组件级与系统级两个层面上同时量化不确定性和校正模型预测，并具备离线批量校准与在线实时校准两种灵活的部署能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a multi-level Bayesian calibration approach that fuses information from heterogeneous sources and accounts for uncertainties in modeling and measurements for time-dependent multi-component systems. The developed methodology has two elements: quantifying the uncertainty at component and system levels, by fusing all available information, and corrected model prediction. A multi-level Bayesian calibration approach is developed to estimate component-level and system-level parameters using measurement data that are obtained at different time instances for different system components. Such heterogeneous data are consumed in a sequential manner, and an iterative strategy is developed to calibrate the parameters at the two levels. This calibration strategy is implemented for two scenarios: offline and online. The offline calibration uses data that is collected over all the time-steps, whereas online calibration is performed in real-time as new measurements are obtained at each time-step. Analysis models and observation data for the thermo-mechanical behavior of gas turbine engine rotor blades are used to analyze the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [241] [Component-wise hyperreduction for nonlinear solid mechanics problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18868)
*Stephan Ritzert,Jannick Kehls,Erik Faust,Hagen Holthusen,Stefanie Reese,Tim Brepols*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于组件的超降阶（hyperreduction）方法，将非线性固体力学组件离线生成可复用的降阶构件，通过POD和ECSW相结合，并利用mortar网格连接进行子结构组装，实现跨装配体、边界条件、网格、材料参数和本构模型的迁移复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的全阶非线性固体力学仿真在大规模装配体中计算成本高昂，且已有的降阶模型通常针对单一特定装配体训练，缺乏跨场景的迁移能力。本文旨在构建可离线生成、可复用、模块化的降阶构件，以提升非线性固体力学仿真的效率和可扩展性。

Method: 采用组件级POD（本征正交分解）降基，结合ECSW（能量守恒采样与加权）进行超降阶，在单一组件仿真中离线计算POD模态和ECSW权重/单元；子结构间通过mortar网格连接实现耦合；为每个子结构额外添加12个刚体模态以处理有限刚体运动。

Result: 数值实验表明，大型准静态装配体的误差低于1%，而仅需评估不到10%的单元。组件基函数和ECSW单元在仅基于弹性Neo-Hookean组件仿真训练的情况下，成功复用于有限应变粘弹性动力学问题。

Conclusion: 组件级超降阶方法能够提供可复用的模块化降阶构件，支持跨装配体、边界条件、网格、材料参数及本构模型的迁移，为模块化非线性固体力学仿真提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Hyperreduced nonlinear solid-mechanics components can be generated offline and reused as transferable building blocks across different assemblies, boundary conditions, meshes, material parameters, and constitutive models. We use proper orthogonal decomposition (POD) and energy conserving sampling and weighting (ECSW) on the component level and connect the substructures by mortar mesh tying. The POD modes and the ECSW weights and elements are computed offline from simulations of single components and the finite rigid body motions are treated by 12 additional rigid body modes per substructure. The numerical examples demonstrate errors below 1 \% for large quasi-static assemblies while evaluating less than 10 \% of the elements. The same component bases and ECSW elements are successfully reused for finite-strain viscoelastic dynamics, although they were trained only on elastic Neo-Hookean component simulations. These results indicate that component-wise hyperreduction can provide reusable reduced building blocks for modular nonlinear solid-mechanics simulations.

</details>


### [242] [Image Generation Techniques for Urban Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18908)
*Katharina Roth,Eva Hagen,Alexander Bartscher,Inga Scheler,Nicolas R. Gauger*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于掩码加权条件流匹配（MWCFM）的生成式AI模型，用于自动生成城市环境中建筑表现图，通过上下文掩码实现精确特征聚焦，并结合风格准则的损失函数进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑师在城市规划中通常需要创建展示拟建建筑及其城市背景的表现图像，这一过程耗时且专业要求高。作者旨在开发一个能够自动生成此类表现图的GenAI模型，并着重于模型优化，以提高生成图像的质量和相关性与城市规划应用的相关性。

Method: 提出了Mask-based Weighted Conditional Flow Matching (MWCFM)，扩展了Flow Matching方法，通过引入上下文掩码实现精确的特征聚焦，使模型能够针对城市规划中关键的空间要素进行定向训练。模型在城市街道视角数据上训练，同时将特定的风格准则通过损失函数集成到训练过程中。

Result: 模型性能使用与应用相关的指标进行评估，这些指标来源于表现图像的风格准则，验证了所提方法在生成符合城市规划和建筑表现要求的图像方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过结合上下文掩码和风格准则约束，在Flow Matching框架上实现了面向城市规划场景的自动化建筑表现图生成，为城市设计领域的GenAI应用提供了模型优化方案。

Abstract: In the context of urban planning, architects are normally instructed with creating presentation images that visualize proposed buildings within their urban context. This work aims to develop a GenAI model for automatically generating architectural presentation images in urban scenes, with emphasis on model optimization. To achieve this, we developed Mask-based Weighted Conditional Flow Matching (MWCFM), which extends Flow Matching by introducing contextual masks for precise feature focusing. This enables targeted training on critical spatial elements relevant to urban planning. Our trained model learns from urban street-view data while adhering to specific style-guidelines, which are integrated into training through the loss function. Furthermore, the model's performance is evaluated using application related metrics, derived from presentation image style guidelines.

</details>


### [243] [U.S. National Liquefaction Hazard Maps and their Implications for Engineering Practice and Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19137)
*Morgan D. Sanger,Victoria P. Zdanovski,Brett W. Maurer*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于力学信息的空间机器学习模型的美国国家级液化灾害图（NLHMs），以约90米分辨率绘制美国本土的液化风险，并探讨了震级选择、条件与非条件灾害公式差异以及液化与社会经济脆弱性的关联三个工程实践问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的液化灾害评估通常依赖单一场景地图，缺乏全国范围内的连续空间域评估；同时需要考虑不同地震灾害公式（条件与非条件）对工程决策和建筑规范的影响，以及液化风险在弱势群体中的分布公平性问题。

Method: 采用力学知识引导的地理空间机器学习模型代理现行液化评估模型，利用大量地理空间预测因子推断地下条件，并以原位测试数据锚定。将模型与2023年美国国家地震灾害模型卷积，利用高性能计算进行高分辨率震级解聚，在约90米分辨率下生成条件（2475年设计事件）和非条件（地面破坏重现期）两种公式的液化灾害图。

Result: 生成了美国本土的条件与非条件液化灾害图，揭示了震级选择（模态值与平均值）对灾害计算结果的显著影响；非条件地图揭示了单一场景地图（当前建筑规范常用）所掩盖的重要空间差异；发现液化暴露与社会经济脆弱性之间存在微弱但统计显著的正梯度关系。

Conclusion: 该NLHMs可用于土地利用政策制定、初步场地评估、区域尺度地震模拟与响应规划及监管筛选工具。同时，研究结果表明非条件灾害公式比当前规范中广泛使用的单一场景地图更为合理，且液化灾害与弱势群体的暴露不平等问题值得政策关注。

Abstract: This study introduces U.S. national liquefaction hazard maps (NLHMs) developed using a mechanics-informed, geospatial machine learning model which surrogates state-of-practice liquefaction models, exploits a large library of geospatial predictors to infer subsurface conditions, and is anchored to measured conditions with in-situ test data. By convolving this geospatial liquefaction model with the 2023 U.S. national seismic hazard model, liquefaction hazard is mapped across the contiguous U.S. at ~90 m resolution within both conditional (2,475-year design event) and unconditional (return period of ground failure) formulations using high-performance computing for the high-resolution magnitude-disaggregation. The resulting NLHMs provide insights for land-use policy, preliminary site assessment, regional-scale earthquake simulation and response planning, and screening tools for regulatory enforcement, among other applications. Beyond quantifying and visualizing liquefaction hazard, the NLHMs are used herein to examine three questions of engineering practice and policy across a continuous spatial domain: (i) the effect of selecting modal versus mean magnitude in conditional analyses; (ii) the differences between conditional and unconditional hazard formulations; and (iii) the extent to which liquefaction hazard compounds with socioeconomic vulnerability. Results elucidate where and how the choice of magnitude alters computed hazards; that unconditional maps reveal important spatial deviations suppressed by single-scenario maps, which are convenient and widely used in current building codes, but less than completely rational; and that modest but statistically significant socioeconomic gradients in liquefaction exposure exist.

</details>


### [244] [Network-Scale Road Disruption from Liquefaction in Cascadia Subduction Zone Earthquakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19143)
*M. D. Sanger,O. Blaze-Smith,B. W. Maurer,L. Wotherspoon,M. O. Eberhard,J. W. Berman*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个力学-informed的数据驱动框架，用于模拟Cascadia俯冲带9级地震引发的液化对公路网络的中断影响。该框架结合基于37,000余次锥探试验的地理空间液化模型、基于物理的破裂情景地面运动、经验脆弱性关系及空间相关蒙特卡洛模拟，评估了公路网络的封闭与服务退化概率，并识别出沿海低地、河口和河谷为高影响集中区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cascadia俯冲带存在发生9级大地震的潜在风险，地震引发的液化将严重中断公路网络，影响应急救援和医疗服务可达性。然而以往基于地质筛查的评估过于保守，需要更精确、数据驱动的框架来指导交通基础设施韧性规划和资产优先级排序。

Method: 采用多步骤集成方法：(1) 使用超过37,000次锥探试验(CPTs)训练的地理空间液化预测模型，并以地貌、水文、气候和地表地质等空间数据为条件；(2) 基于物理的CSZ破裂情景集合生成地面运动；(3) 利用经验脆弱性关系估计路段封闭和服务退化概率；(4) 通过空间相关的蒙特卡洛模拟在美国国家公路系统中传播不确定性。

Result: 结果表明影响高度集中于沿海低地、河口和河流河谷，U.S. Route 101沿线暴露突出。华盛顿州Pacific和Grays Harbor县的聚焦分析显示医疗服务孤立的概率较高。与基于地质筛查的先前全州评估相比，仅由液化导致的桥梁封闭预测低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 尽管存在不确定性，该框架为Cascadia地区的交通韧性规划和资产优先级排序提供了可辩护的工具，通过更精细的地下条件表征和破坏机制模拟，显著改进了对液化引起的基础设施中断的预测精度。

Abstract: This paper presents a mechanics-informed, data-driven framework for modeling liquefaction-induced disruption of roadway networks following a magnitude-9 earthquake on the Cascadia Subduction Zone (CSZ). Liquefaction hazard is predicted using a geospatial liquefaction model trained on more than 37,000 cone penetration tests (CPTs) and conditioned on spatial data describing geomorphology, hydrology, climate, and surficial geology. Ground motions are derived from physics-based ensembles of CSZ rupture scenarios. Segment-level probabilities of closure and service degradation are estimated using empirically derived fragility relationships and propagated through the National Highway System using spatially correlated Monte Carlo simulation. Results indicate strong concentration of impacts in coastal lowlands, estuaries, and river valleys, with pronounced exposure along U.S. Route 101. Focused analysis in Pacific and Grays Harbor Counties, Washington, shows elevated probability of healthcare isolation. Compared with prior statewide assessments based on geologic screening, predicted bridge closures due solely to liquefaction are an order of magnitude lower, reflecting improved representation of subsurface conditions and damage mechanisms. Despite uncertainties, the framework provides a defensible tool for transportation resilience planning and asset prioritization in Cascadia.

</details>


### [245] [Sweep-based, implicit solutions of the multidimensional BGK equation on unstructured grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19150)
*Thomas M. Evans,Ryan Glasby,Cory Hauck,Stefan R. Schnake,Kyle J. Schwiebert,Lawton Shoemake,Stuart Slattery*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于求解多维非结构化网格上BGK动力学方程的节点间断伽辽金方法，采用隐式扫描求解器和保矩Maxwell源投影实现时间高阶精度。方法在连续极限下通过Sod激波问题验证正确性，展示了三阶B稳定对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法的L2稳定性，并通过Kokkos库实现CPU/GPU混合并行，在NVIDIA H100上相比64核AMD EPYC 9654 CPU实现了超20倍加速。在Frontier超算上成功执行了具有2.77万亿相空间自由度的边界值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决BGK动力学方程在多维非结构化网格上的高效高精度求解问题。传统显式方法受限于边界层和几何诱导特征带来的严格时间步长约束，需要开发一种能够避免该限制的高阶时间精度隐式方法，同时解决大规模并行计算中的性能可移植性问题。

Method: 该方法基于节点间断伽辽金（nodal DG）离散框架，核心创新包括：(1) 采用隐式、基于扫描（sweep-based）的求解器避免显式时间步长限制；(2) 通过保矩投影（moment-preserving projection）处理Maxwell源项以获得时间高阶精度；(3) 采用B稳定的三阶对角隐式Runge-Kutta方法保证了线性L2稳定性；(4) 开发基于图的新型扫描算法配合Kokkos性能可移植库实现CPU/GPU混合并行，采用空间域分解策略。

Result: 方法在2D和3D非结构化网格上通过Sod激波问题得到正确验证，与闭式解和高阶解吻合。在NVIDIA H100 GPU上实现了相比64核AMD EPYC 9654 CPU超过20倍的加速比。在Frontier超算上使用1536个节点、6144块AMD MI250X GPU成功执行了具有2.77万亿相空间自由度的边界值问题。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了所提出的方法在精度、稳定性和大规模并行可扩展性方面均表现优异，特别适用于处理具有边界层和其他几何诱导特征的多维动力学问题。通过Kokkos库实现的平台可移植性表明该方法能够在不同硬件架构上灵活部署，为大规模BGK方程求解提供了实用的高性能解决方案。

Abstract: We present a nodal discontinuous Galerkin method for solving the Bhatnagar-Gross-Krook (BGK) kinetic equation on multi-dimensional, unstructured grids. The method uses implicit, sweep-based solvers and a moment-preserving projection of the Maxwellian source to enable high-order accuracy in time while avoiding restrictive time steps imposed by boundary layers and other geometry-induced features. We verify that the method is correct in the continuum limit by comparing to closed-form and high-order solutions of the Sod shock problem on 2 and 3D unstructured grids. Linear L2 stability is demonstrated for a B-stable diagonally implicit Runge-Kutta method of third order. The solver uses a hybrid parallel scheme based on spatial domain decomposition with local sweeps performed on CPU and GPU hardware. Platform-portability is demonstrated through the development of new GPU-friendly, graph-based sweep algorithms that are implemented using the Kokkos performance portability library and achieve greater than 20 times speedup on NVIDIA H100 GPUs compared to 64-core AMD EPYC 9654 CPUs. Finally, we show results on the Frontier supercomputer at the Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility for a boundary value problem with 2.77 trillion phase space degrees of freedom that executed on 1536 nodes utilizing 6144 AMD MI250X GPUs.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Entropy-Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18147)
*Ran Ben Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Michael Mitzenmacher,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵约束自适应随机量化（ECASQ）问题，在无偏约束和熵预算下联合选择自适应量化值以最小化MSE，给出了最优动态规划算法（O(sd²)时间，O(d²)空间）和GPU友好的近似动态规划算法（O(sd²)时间，O(d)空间），并提供迭代精化过程以获得近最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无偏量化方法（包括ASQ）在选择量化值时未考虑后续的无损熵编码阶段，导致精度损失。本文旨在将熵编码纳入量化值选择过程，填补这一空白。

Method: 形式化ECASQ问题（在熵预算和无偏约束下联合优化量化值以最小化MSE）；提出最优动态规划算法（O(sd²)时间，O(d²)空间）；提出GPU友好的近似动态规划算法（O(sd²)时间，O(d)空间），保证近似解的MSE不会超过用少一位熵的最优解；提供迭代精化过程提升近似解质量。

Result: 近似算法提供理论保证（MSE不超过少一位熵的最优解），迭代精化在实验中达到近最优结果，同时相比最优求解器保持显著的速度优势。

Conclusion: 通过在设计阶段引入熵约束，ECASQ能显著提升量化性能，在模型、梯度、KV缓存压缩及最近邻搜索等场景中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Adaptive stochastic quantization (ASQ) is a recently introduced quantization approach that optimizes the Mean Squared Error (MSE) for a given input while preserving unbiasedness. It is designed to alleviate the communication and memory bottlenecks of modern data and machine learning workloads, including model, gradient, and KV-cache compression and nearest-neighbor search. Further, practical systems can then compress quantized data with a lossless entropy encoder. However, existing unbiased methods, including ASQ, choose their quantization values without considering this later encoding stage, leaving accuracy on the table.
  We formulate the Entropy Constrained Adaptive Stochastic Quantization (ECASQ) problem, which jointly selects adaptive quantization values to minimize MSE under an entropy budget and an unbiasedness constraint. We give an optimal dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d^2)$ space for a length-d vector and at most s quantization values, and a GPU-friendly approximate dynamic program with $O(sd^2)$ time and $O(d)$ space. The approximation guarantees that the solution has an MSE no larger than the optimal solution that uses one fewer bit of entropy per entry. We also provide an iterative refinement procedure for the approximation solution that, in our experiments, yields near-optimal results while retaining a substantial speed advantage over our solver for the optimal solution.

</details>


### [247] [Towards Reversible Forgetting: Managing Obsolete Knowledge in Continual Enterprise AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18177)
*Nilutpaul Sarker Yash,Tirtho Roy,Ushashi Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"可逆遗忘"（reversible forgetting）概念框架，用于企业AI代理在非平稳环境中的知识管理。该框架包含三个操作记忆状态（活跃、休眠、退役）及一个再激活转换机制，通过建立滞后可逆记忆控制器（Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller）来降低过时知识的影响，同时避免将暂时抑制与永久删除混为一谈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的持续学习将遗忘视为失败，致力于保留先前习得的知识。但作者认为这一目标对企业AI代理不完整，因为在非平稳环境中客户、政策、工具、工作流、法规和市场条件不断变化，不加区分地保留知识会让过时信息影响决策，产生负迁移和运营风险。

Method: 提出可逆遗忘概念框架，包含三个操作记忆状态（active活跃、dormant休眠、retired退役）和再激活转换机制。将其实现为滞后可逆记忆控制器（Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller），该控制器通过累积相关性证据、利用非对称阈值防止状态振荡、在影子模式下测试再激活、以及通过策略门控退役。

Result: 该框架能降低过时信息的影响，同时不将暂时抑制与永久擦除相混淆。以金融领域为例说明：在一种市场制度下有用的知识在另一种制度下可能有害，但当类似条件再次出现时又会重新发挥作用。

Conclusion: 可逆遗忘框架为企业AI代理提供了更精细的记忆管理机制，在保持知识灵活性的同时规避过时信息带来的负迁移风险，核心价值在于区分"暂时抑制"与"永久擦除"。

Abstract: Continual learning has traditionally treated forgetting as a failure, emphasizing preservation of previously acquired knowledge as environments evolve. We argue that this objective is incomplete for enterprise AI agents operating in non-stationary environments, where customers, policies, tools, workflows, regulations, and market conditions change over time. Indiscriminate retention can allow obsolete knowledge to influence decisions, creating negative transfer and operational risk. We therefore propose reversible forgetting: a conceptual framework with three operational memory states: active, dormant, and retired, and a reactivation transition that can restore dormant knowledge when its relevance returns. We instantiate the framework as a Hysteretic Reversible Memory Controller that accumulates relevance evidence, uses asymmetric thresholds to prevent state oscillation, tests reactivation in shadow mode, and gates retirement through policy. The framework reduces the influence of obsolete information without conflating temporary suppression with permanent erasure. Finance illustrates the idea: knowledge useful under one market regime may become harmful under another yet regain relevance when similar conditions recur.

</details>


### [248] [Multi-stage neural operator learning with application for convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18851)
*Zhiping Mao,Zhenye Wen,Yong Zhang,Xiaofei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种多阶段神经算子学习框架（DCNO和DGNO）用于快速精确计算卷积积分，通过分阶段构建基算子来逐步细化近似空间，在卷积问题上达到接近单精度机器精度的准确性，并显著提升多查询或参数变化场景下的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积积分在众多应用中广泛存在，传统求解器在处理大量查询或参数变化时计算成本高昂，需要一种既能保证高精度又能实现快速计算的算子学习方法。

Method: 提出两种多阶段神经算子学习框架：(1) DCNO（Deep Collocation Neural Operator），一种基于输入-输出数据对的监督学习方法，通过迭代学习残差来细化算子近似；(2) DGNO（Deep Galerkin Neural Operator），一种无监督框架，适用于目标算子可由PDE表示的情况，利用PDE残差的弱形式进行训练。两种方法均通过多个训练阶段逐步构建基算子来丰富近似空间，并提供理论上的逼近能力分析。

Result: 大量数值实验表明，两种方法在卷积问题上均达到高精度，接近单精度浮点下的机器精度；相比传统求解器，在大量查询或参数变化场景下具有显著效率优势。框架还可扩展至多输入算子学习场景，即同时处理卷积密度和核的变化。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练策略相比一次性算子学习显著提升了精度，DCNO和DGNO为卷积积分的高精度快速计算提供了有效且可扩展的解决方案，并在理论上得到了逼近能力的保障。

Abstract: Convolution integrals widely exist in applications, and to enable fast and accurate computations, this paper introduces two general multi-stage neural operator learning frameworks. The first, Deep Collocation Neural Operator (DCNO), is a supervised approach that iteratively refines the operator approximation by learning residuals from input-output data pairs. The second, Deep Galerkin Neural Operator (DGNO), is an unsupervised framework applicable when the target operator can be represented by a PDE, leveraging the weak form of the PDE residual for training. Both methods progressively construct basis operators through multiple training stages to enrich the approximation space, leading to significantly improved accuracy over standard one-shot operator learning. We provide theoretical analysis for their approximation capabilities and implement them for learning convolutions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that both DCNO and DGNO achieve high accuracy, approaching machine precision under single float for convolution problems, and offer substantial efficiency gains for numerous queries or parametric variations compared to traditional solvers. We also extend these frameworks to handle multi-input operator learning scenarios involving variations in both the density and kernel of a convolution.

</details>


### [249] [Accelerating Visual On-Policy Distillation with Batched Speculative Jacobi Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18183)
*Bingqi Shan,Zhehao Yu,Kenhong Lin,Baoquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HB-SJD是一种用于视觉在策略蒸馏(OPD)的批量推测式雅可比解码回滚后端，它允许每个图像根据自身解码进度独立推进，同时仍以批量模型前向进行验证，并随图像完成而动态切换Full与Compact执行，在不改变教师网络、蒸馏目标和优化流程的前提下显著减少回滚及端到端训练时间，同时保持蒸馏学生的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉在策略蒸馏(OPD)虽然在紧凑视觉自回归模型的训练中有效，但其训练流程依赖当前学生逐token自回归解码生成在线回滚轨迹，这为每个在策略训练步骤带来了高昂的额外成本。虽然推测式雅可比解码(SJD)无需辅助草稿模型即可并行处理多个token，但该方法是针对单序列推理设计的，无法直接用于批量训练场景。

Method: 提出HB-SJD作为视觉OPD的批量SJD回滚后端。其核心设计包括：(1) 允许每张图像根据自身解码进度独立推进；(2) 不同序列位置的图像仍通过批量模型前向进行验证；(3) 当部分图像完成时，动态在Full与Compact执行之间切换以降低后续回滚轮次的成本；(4) 仅替换学生回滚后端，教师网络、蒸馏目标函数与优化流程保持不变。

Result: 基于LlamaGen的实验中，HB-SJD大幅降低了回滚时间和端到端训练时间，同时保持了蒸馏学生的生成质量。

Conclusion: HB-SJD通过批量化的推测式解码有效解决了视觉OPD中在线回滚的高成本问题，在不改变蒸馏框架的前提下获得了显著的训练加速，为大规模视觉自回归模型的蒸馏训练提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation (OPD) improves the training of compact visual autoregressive models by learning from trajectories generated by the current student. However, these online rollouts are still produced token by token with autoregressive decoding, which adds substantial cost to every on-policy training step. Speculative Jacobi Decoding (SJD) provides an alternative because it can process multiple tokens in parallel without an auxiliary draft model, but the original method is designed for single-sequence inference. We introduce HB-SJD, a batched SJD rollout backend for visual OPD. HB-SJD allows each image to advance independently according to its own decoding progress, while images at different sequence positions are still verified in batched model forwards. As images finish, HB-SJD switches between Full and Compact execution to reduce the cost of later rollout rounds. HB-SJD only replaces the student rollout backend and leaves the teacher, distillation objective, and optimization procedure unchanged. Experiments with LlamaGen show that HB-SJD substantially reduces rollout and end-to-end training time while preserving the generation quality of the distilled student.

</details>


### [250] [H$^2$EDL: Hyper Evidential Deep Learning for Hierarchical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18185)
*Yuanye Liu,Xiahai Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出H²EDL，一种基于树结构超意见（tree-structured hyper-opinion）的层次细粒度识别框架。它利用标签分类学本身的子树和叶单元作为线性大小的焦点族，在每个分支节点上放置一个局部Dirichlet意见，从而以闭式方式导出所有复合质量。H²EDL既可作为保持层次一致性的层次分类器，也可作为有效的树结构超意见概率模型。在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345上，与交叉熵基线相比，校准误差约降低一半，且层次越深、训练预算越大时改进越显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度识别常涉及层次标签空间，模型可能对粗粒度概念有信心但对细分类不确定。现有工具各只捕获一半：平坦的认知分类器仅用叶框架上的单一空洞量表示总无知，层次分类器则传播点概率而无证据概念。需要一种统一表示，既能捕获细粒度类别又能捕获中间概念的不确定性。

Method: 观察到分类学本身即缺失的超域。其子树和叶单元构成线性大小的焦点族，每个分支节点上的一个局部Dirichlet意见可以闭式导出所有复合质量。该模型H²EDL可用同一组参数进行两种互补解释：预测视角下作为保持跨层次一致性的层次分类器；概率视角下定义有效的树结构超意见，节点质量表示到达该节点但不足以进一步具体化为后代的信念。

Result: 在FGVC-Aircraft和DERM12345数据集上，H²EDL相比交叉熵基线将校准误差降低约一半，且改进在更深的层次和更大的训练预算下更加显著。

Conclusion: H²EDL通过利用分类学的树结构，将层次一致性与基于证据的不确定性量化统一在同一个框架中，解决了先前方法的根本局限（指数复杂度、依赖标注或非结构化权重模式），在细粒度识别中实现了显著更优的校准性能。

Abstract: Fine-grained recognition often involves hierarchical label spaces, where a model may be confident about a coarse semantic concept while remaining uncertain among its descendant classes. Such structured ambiguity requires uncertainty representations that capture both fine-grained classes and intermediate concepts. However, existing tools each capture only half of it: flat evidential classifiers quantify total ignorance with a single vacuity on the leaf frame, and hierarchical classifiers propagate point probabilities with no notion of evidence. Hyper-opinions would unify the two, but their general form is exponential in the label count, and existing hyper-evidential networks either require composite labels to be supplied in the training data or read them off an unstructured weight pattern, with no principled notion of which composites deserve mass. We observe that the taxonomy itself is the missing hyperdomain. Its subtrees and leaf singletons form a linear-size focal family, and one local Dirichlet opinion per branching node induces every composite mass in closed form. The resulting model, H$^2$EDL, can be interpreted in two complementary ways using the same set of parameters. From a prediction perspective, it functions as a hierarchical classifier that preserves consistency across different levels of the label tree. From a probabilistic perspective, it defines a valid tree-structured hyper-opinion, where the mass assigned to each node represents the belief that reaches that node but does not provide sufficient confidence to further specialize into its descendants. On FGVC-Aircraft and DERM12345, H$^2$EDL reduces calibration error by approximately half compared with cross-entropy baselines, with the improvement becoming more pronounced at deeper hierarchy levels and under larger training budgets.

</details>


### [251] [What Can Artificial Intelligence Learn from Medicine? Generative Analogies and Reliable Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18186)
*Emanuele Ratti,Lena Zuchowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Hesse的类比理论，将临床转化过程与机器学习系统构建过程之间的平行关系刻画为一种生成性类比（generative analogy），以此将临床转化的认识论和方法论依据（以可靠性主义解释）类比性地迁移到ML领域，提出一种新的ML可靠性主义，与现有AI哲学中的可靠性主义方案既有区别又兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来机器学习在医学中广泛应用，但其认识论和方法论依据尚不明确。文献中已有将医学与ML进行类比的讨论，但该类比的性质及其可以承载的认识论启示未被精确刻画，作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 借鉴Hesse关于类比的理论工具，将临床转化与ML系统构建之间的平行关系明确定义为一种生成性类比；从可靠性主义（reliabilist）角度阐释临床转化的认识论与方法论依据，并通过类比逻辑将这些依据系统地映射到ML语境中，从而提出一种新的ML可靠性主义框架。

Result: 作者成功（1）刻画了该平行关系作为生成性类比的本质；（2）明确化了临床转化的认识论与方法论依据细节；（3）从可靠性主义视角展示了这些依据如何类比性地适用于ML；（4）提出了一种区别于现有AI哲学中可靠性主义方案的新形式ML可靠性主义。

Conclusion: 该研究为ML的认识论正当性提供了新的理论框架，通过类比临床翻译的可靠性标准，提出了ML可靠性主义的新路径，既丰富了AI认识论的讨论，也为ML在医学等高风险领域中的实践提供了更坚实的哲学基础。

Abstract: In the past few years, machine learning (ML) has been widely (and to an extent, successfully) implemented in medicine. However, uncertainties surrounding ML have made it difficult to establish the bases of its epistemic and methodological warrants. In the literature, a parallel has been drawn between medicine and ML, suggesting that we should model epistemic and methodological standards for ML on the standards of clinical translation. By developing tools from Hesse work, we characterise the nature of this parallel as a generative analogy between the process of clinical translation and the process of building ML systems. We identify more precisely the epistemic and methodological warrants of clinical translation that are typically only mentioned when appealing to the analogy, and we show in which sense such warrants apply analogically to the context of ML. In particular, we interpret warrants of clinical translation in reliabilist terms, and we show how this can inform a new form of ML reliabilism, which is distinct from (though compatible with) existing reliabilist accounts in philosophy of AI.

</details>


### [252] [A systematic review of machine learning techniques to address diagnosis and treatment of autism: challenges and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18188)
*Rafael Muñoz-Terol,Jesús Peral,Sandra Amador,David Gil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对2017至2023年间55项关于机器学习应用于自闭症谱系障碍（ASD）的研究进行了系统性综述，重点关注ML技术在ASD诊断和治疗中的应用趋势、技术与数据集。研究发现监督学习方法占据主导地位，深度学习方法因数据可获取性提升而不断扩展，未来混合方法（结合无监督、深度学习和模糊逻辑）有望成为新的发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASD的病因尚不明确，识别相关特征和隐藏关联对于早期诊断至关重要，因此需要系统性地梳理近年来机器学习在ASD研究中的应用现状，以指导未来的研究方向和实践。

Method: 对2017至2023年间发表的55项研究进行系统性综述（systematic review），分析ML技术应用于ASD诊断和治疗的研究趋势、所采用的技术方案以及使用的数据集。

Result: 监督学习方法主导ASD研究领域；深度学习的作用随着数据量的增加而不断扩大；混合方法（集成无监督学习、深度学习与模糊逻辑）被视为有前景的未来发展方向。

Conclusion: 未来ML模型需整合多模态数据（如遗传与临床信息），并融入可穿戴设备和生物传感器等创新数据源以实现持续、非侵入式的监测；解决当前挑战需要跨学科合作和针对ASD定制的扩展数据集。

Abstract: Autism spectrum disorder (ASD) is a developmental disability characterized by challenges in social interaction and communication. As the causes of ASD remain unclear, identifying relevant features and hidden correlations is crucial for early diagnosis. This systematic review evaluates 55 studies from 2017 to 2023 on the application of machine learning (ML) techniques to ASD. The primary objective is to examine recent ML applications in ASD research, identifying trends, techniques, and datasets that enhance diagnosis and treatment. Supervised learning methods dominate, as they align well with ASD diagnostic needs; however, the role of deep learning is expanding with greater data availability. Emerging techniques based on hybrid methods, where unsupervised, deep learning, and fuzzy logic could be included, will be interesting to observe in the future. The review highlights key challenges and opportunities, particularly the need for models that can integrate complex data -such as genetic and clinical information- to improve diagnostic accuracy and treatment outcomes. Additionally, incorporating innovative data sources, like wearable devices and biometric sensors, could enable continuous and non-intrusive monitoring, providing a more holistic understanding of ASD. Findings emphasize that addressing current challenges requires interdisciplinary collaboration and expanded datasets tailored to ASD. Future ML models will benefit from broader multimodal data integration, enabling researchers to more comprehensively address the complexities of ASD.

</details>


### [253] [Safe Domain Adaptation for Physics: Overcoming Nuisances, Label Shifts, and Simulation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18190)
*Ivan Kharuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究域自适应在物理场景中的应用问题，指出当模拟与实验的物理分布本身不同时，标准对抗性域自适应会错误地对齐目标分布，产生基于模拟先验的偏差。作者提出自适应域自适应（adaptive domain adaptation）方法，通过重新加权模拟事件，使域自适应聚焦于真实的物理失配，并提供无标签模型选择规则来选取接近最优的工作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域自适应广泛应用于将模拟训练的网络迁移到实验数据，但其隐含前提是两域仅存在干扰性差异且目标量分布相同。然而在物理应用中这两个假设均不成立：模拟可能对物理本身存在错误，且目标量（如能谱、红移分布）的分布往往正是待测量的对象。因此需要研究这种失配的后果并提出更合适的自适应方法。

Method: 构建一个玩具气簇射基准测试，可分别或同时开关三种失配源：探测器响应的干扰性差异、物理模拟偏移、能谱偏移。设计自适应域自适应方法，通过对模拟事件重新加权，使对抗性域自适应聚焦于真实的物理失配而非分布差异。同时提出无标签模型选择规则，用于在预测谱依赖模型训练配置的情况下选择接近最优的工作点。

Result: 标准对抗性自适应能够处理条件偏移，但当模拟与实验的能谱不同时，它会错误地对齐两域的谱，从而用一个锚定在模拟先验上的偏差替代原本无约束的偏差。所提出的自适应域自适应方法可避免这一问题，并且无标签模型选择规则能够选出接近最优的工作点。

Conclusion: 物理应用中域自适应需区分"干扰性差异"与"物理真实差异"：前者应被对齐消除，后者则不应被对齐，而应作为测量对象保留。自适应域自适应通过加权实现了这一区分，并提供了实用的模型选择策略。

Abstract: Domain adaptation is widely used to make neural networks trained on simulations applicable to experimental data. Its premise is that the two domains differ only in nuisances, and that the quantity of interest is distributed identically in both. In physics neither assumption holds: simulations can be wrong about the physics, and the distribution of the target quantity - an energy spectrum, a redshift distribution - is often the measurement itself. We study the consequences of such mismatches on a toy air-shower benchmark in which a detector-response nuisance, a physical simulation shift, and an energy-spectrum shift can be switched on separately or together. Standard adversarial adaptation handles the conditional shifts, but once the two spectra differ it aligns them, replacing an uncontrolled bias by one anchored on the simulation prior. We present adaptive domain adaptation, which reweights the simulated events so as to focus domain adaptation on the genuine physical mismatch alone. Since the predicted spectrum depends on model training configuration, we provide a label-free model selection rule for selecting the near-the-best operation point.

</details>


### [254] [ChiroEcho: extending automated bat vocalisation classification beyond the learned taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18191)
*Burooj Ghani,Welmoed Eversteijn,Milan van Hirtum,Juan Sebastián Cañas,Vincent J. Kalkman,Dan Stowell,A. Leonie Baier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度学习框架，联合预测蝙蝠的物种和属，并在推理阶段结合属预测与地理物种分布信息，使分类器能够在属内仅有一种物种时解析出训练分类学中不存在的物种，从而将地理信息从约束转化为扩展分类器有效分类学的手段。实验覆盖35种欧洲蝙蝠物种，地理扩展将操作覆盖范围从35种提升至41种（占48种欧洲本土蝙蝠的85%），是目前报道的覆盖范围最广的自动化欧洲蝙蝠分类系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蝙蝠是欧洲受保护的生态系统健康指标物种，可靠的种群监测是保护优先事项。其隐蔽的夜行生活方式使得被动声学监测至关重要，但回声定位叫声因行为和环境的差异而多变且物种间重叠，自动化识别仍然困难。现有分类器受限于训练分类学，无法识别未见过的物种，需要一种方法利用地理信息扩展分类器的有效分类范围。

Method: 构建深度学习框架，联合预测物种和属两个层级；推理阶段将属的预测结果与地理物种分布信息结合，当预测属在所在区域仅有一个物种存在时，即可解析出训练分类学中未包含的物种。研究评估了闭集分类性能、对稀疏代表物种的性能估计不稳定性进行了检验，并进行了受控的留出（held-out）概念验证实验。

Result: 地理解析将操作覆盖范围从35种扩展到41种（覆盖率从73%提升至85%），覆盖了48种欧洲本土蝙蝠中的41种，这是目前报道的自动化欧洲蝙蝠分类中最广的操作覆盖范围。稀有物种分析表明有限评估数据可能掩盖物种级性能；留出实验证明属预测与地理位置可以恢复物种头（species head）无法获得的标签。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了通过粗粒度预测与透明外部约束结合来解析未见过的细粒度类别的概念验证，不仅适用于蝙蝠监测，也为更广泛的生态声学分类任务提供了可推广的方法范式。地理信息被重新定义为分类器有效分类学的扩展手段而非约束条件。

Abstract: Bats are key indicators of ecosystem health and are protected throughout Europe, making reliable population monitoring a conservation priority. Their cryptic nocturnal lifestyle makes passive acoustic monitoring essential, yet automated identification remains difficult as echolocation calls vary with behaviour and environment and overlap among species. We present a deep learning framework that jointly predicts species and genus and combines genus predictions with geographic species distributions at inference. When only one species of a predicted genus occurs in a region, the framework can resolve species absent from the learned taxonomy. This reframes geographic information as a means of extending, rather than constraining, a classifier's effective taxonomy. Using recordings spanning 35 European bat species, we evaluate closed-set classification, examine the instability of performance estimates for sparsely represented species, and conduct a controlled held-out proof-of-principle experiment. The rare-species analysis shows how limited evaluation data can obscure species-level performance, while the held-out experiment shows that genus predictions and location can recover labels unavailable to the species head. Geographic resolution extends operational coverage from 35 to 41 of the 48 native European bat species, increasing coverage from 73% to 85%. To our knowledge, this is the broadest operational coverage reported for automated European bat classification. More broadly, the bat framework provides proof of principle for resolving unseen fine-grained classes by combining coarse predictions with transparent external constraints.

</details>


### [255] [Think Shallow, Solve Deep: Controlling Recurrent Dynamics for Reliable Test-Time Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18222)
*Ivan Viakhirev,Kirill Borodin,Amirah Almutairi,Serguei Barannikov,Maxim Abramov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度推理模型在测试时迭代更新的行为，提出可通过测量算子的有限时间动力学状态（settling/marginal/drifting）来预测额外迭代是提升、保持还是降低答案质量，并给出深度安全性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归深度推理模型通过测试时更多迭代来解决更难的问题，但额外的迭代可能改善、保持不变或降低答案质量，缺乏对深度扩展行为的可预测性与理论基础。

Method: 1）提出测量训练后算子的有限时间动力学状态（settling、marginal、drifting）；2）给出深度安全性的充分条件：当单步位移相对解码器间隔足够小，则解码答案在进一步迭代下不变；3）在算法任务上用800个未增强样本训练；4）引入单一终端不动点目标来调节动力学状态与深度行为；5）给出四个用于测试时深度有效性的操作标准。

Result: 在算法任务上，settling算子在增加深度时不会退化，部分任务上还能提升更难未见实例的准确率（如数独从0.19提升到0.34，超过训练范围）。移除终端不动点目标会导致漂移并消除增益；添加它到通用递归中可在进位传播上实现深度安全的外推。对Huginn-3.5B的测量显示其属于非settling族。

Conclusion: 算子的有限时间动力学状态是预测深度推理行为的关键可测指标，终端不动点目标能统一调控动力学状态与深度行为，并提供了实用的操作准则来识别深度有用的条件及故障模式。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth reasoners aim to solve harder problems by iterating their update longer at test time, but additional iterations can improve, preserve, or degrade an answer. We show that a measurable property of the trained operator, its finite-time dynamical regime (estimated as settling, marginal, or drifting), indicates which of these occurs. We give a sufficient condition for depth-safety: once an operator's per-step displacement is small relative to the decoder margin, the decoded answer cannot change under further iterations. Empirically, on algorithmic tasks trained from $800$ unaugmented examples per difficulty tier, settling operators do not degrade with added depth, and on some tasks convert it into higher accuracy on harder unseen instances (Sudoku, $0.19$ to $0.34$ past the training horizon). A single terminal fixed-point objective moves the regime and the depth behavior together: removing it induces drift and removes the gains, and adding it to a generic recurrence yields depth-safe extrapolation on carry propagation. We give four operational criteria for useful test-time depth, use them to catalogue failure modes, and, as a consistency check, apply the same measurements to Huginn-3.5B, which falls in the non-settling family.

</details>


### [256] [Converting Expert Deliberation into Financial Signals Through A Context-Aware NLP Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18911)
*Vivek Batra,Kristin Chen,Sanjiv Das,Samuel Judge,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Amir Nasrollahzadeh,Daniel Ostrov,Jacob Sisk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CDSP（上下文条件性审议信号管线），将投资委员会会议记录转化为结构化预测特征。该管线使用LLM进行资产类别上下文标注、预定义财务关键词分类、情绪极性和提及频率特征构建。在48个月度会议数据集上，预测全球股票与债券的相对表现，最佳模型（句子嵌入+CDSP特征）准确率达73%，优于基准（60.4%），并将专家审议中的前瞻性信息通过上下文感知NLP提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投资委员会会议的审议讨论可能包含前瞻性市场信息，但非结构化的会议转写文本难以被量化模型直接使用。作者希望构建一个特征工程管线，将自然语言讨论转换为结构化、可预测的金融特征，以提升对全球股票与债券相对表现的预测能力。

Method: CDSP管线包含四个步骤：(1)将会议转写文本分割为主题块；(2)使用大语言模型（LLM）为每个块分配资产类别上下文标签；(3)将金融关键词映射到预定义的标签分类体系；(4)构建互补特征——情绪极性和提及频率。在48个月度投资委员会会议上分别测试了原始文本嵌入、CDSP工程特征以及两者组合的预测性能。

Result: 预测准确率范围为62%-73%，而基准（始终选择股票）的正确率为60.4%。最佳模型（句子嵌入+CDSP特征）达到73%准确率和0.73 F1分数，但这一提升在统计上不显著。情绪信号在多个分类类别中比提及频率携带更强的预测信号。

Conclusion: 专家审议讨论中确实包含可通过上下文感知NLP提取的前瞻性信息。CDSP工程特征与嵌入表示的组合提供了最佳的预测性能，但需要更大规模的数据或更精细的方法来达到统计显著性。情绪比提及频率更具信息价值，为后续该领域的研究提供了方向。

Abstract: We introduce the CDSP (context-conditional deliberation signal pipeline), converting an investment committee's meeting transcripts into structured predictive features. CDSP segments the meeting transcripts into topical chunks, assigns asset-class context labels using a large language model (LLM), maps financial keywords to a pre-determined taxonomy of labels, and constructs complementary features: sentiment polarity and mention frequency. This feature engineering framework is applied to a dataset spanning 48 monthly committee meetings to predict if global equities will perform better or worse than global bonds in the following month. In experiments with engineered features, raw transcript text, sentence embeddings, and combined representations, the prediction accuracy ranges from 62% to 73%, compared to always choosing stocks, which outperforms bonds 60.4% of the time. The best (73% accurate) model combines sentence embeddings with engineered CDSP features, achieving a 0.73 F1 score (although this is not statistically significant compared to always choosing stocks). Sentiment carries a stronger signal than mention frequency for several taxonomy categories. These findings suggest that experts' deliberations may contain forward-looking information that context-aware NLP can extract.

</details>


### [257] [Allocating Recurrent Compute in Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18230)
*Ruhai Lin,Yiyang Guo,Rui-Jie Zhu,Hao Ye,Jason K. Eshraghian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MixerLoop架构，仅循环Gated DeltaNet mixer层而将密集FFN只执行一次，在15M和110M参数规模下超越或接近全块循环（FullLoop）的性能，同时将循环骨干投影的FLOPs降低45.9%，证明了循环深度的收益无需重复执行密集FFN即可保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环语言模型通常重复整个层堆栈，但mixer和密集FFN执行不同操作且成本不同。作者提出更精细的问题：到底应该循环什么？通过信息论视角（ITER划分累积影响轨迹，边际ITER描述非冗余贡献）来论证循环的价值在于暴露新的跨位置影响方向。

Method: 作者提出了迭代传输秩（ITR）框架来衡量循环应用的影响轨迹，并据此设计MixerLoop架构：仅循环Gated DeltaNet mixer层，密集FFN只执行一次。通过有界上下文干预测试验证后期mixer应用是否在最终语言模型读出端产生独特、非平凡且有益的变更，并与无循环和全块循环在相同数据、初始化和架构条件下进行对比。

Result: MixerLoop在15M参数下超越FullLoop的聚合CORE指标，在110M参数下保留了FullLoop 41.5%的CORE改进，同时将循环骨干投影的FLOPs减少了45.9%。

Conclusion: 循环深度的收益可以通过仅循环mixer层而非整个层块来保留，无需重复执行昂贵的密集FFN，这一发现为设计更高效的循环语言模型提供了理论和实证依据。

Abstract: Looped language models improve reasoning and knowledge manipulation by applying shared computation repeatedly. Existing systems usually repeat an entire layer stack, although a mixer and a dense feed-forward network (FFN) perform different operations and have different costs. We ask a narrower question: what should loop? We view recurrence as repeated composition of a state update and argue that an application is valuable when it exposes a new cross-position influence direction that remains observable at the task readout. Iterative Transport Rank (ITR) describes the cumulative influence trajectory; marginal ITR describes the nonredundant influence contributed by successive applications. This view motivates MixerLoop, which repeats each Gated DeltaNet mixer while applying its dense FFN once. We compare MixerLoop with no recurrence and full-block recurrence at 15M and 110M parameters under the same data, initialization, and architecture. A finite context-off intervention tests whether later mixer applications produce distinct, non-negligible, and beneficial changes at the final language-model readout. MixerLoop surpasses FullLoop on aggregate CORE at 15M and retains 41.5% of its CORE improvement at 110M while reducing recurrent-backbone projection FLOPs by 45.9%. These results show that the benefits of recurrent depth can be retained without repeatedly executing the dense FFN.

</details>


### [258] [Training Chemical Plausibility-Aware Large Language Models for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18940)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Maksim Kuznetsov,Mathieu Reymond,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Top-K提示作为训练与推理范式，以捕捉逆合成分析中天然的一对多特性。构建约4560万条已验证反应的超大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL，训练化学约束一致语言模型C3LM，结合ChemCensor和面向新颖性的奖励微调，在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到SOTA，并发现LLM与传统模型探索互补的反应空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步逆合成分析是计算机辅助合成规划的核心，但其本质上是一对多（one-to-many）的问题，即一个目标分子可能对应多种合理的反应路径。现有的评估和基准测试协议仅采用单一正确答案的评估方式，无法充分捕捉这一多样性特征，导致模型评估不全面、不公正。

Method: 1) 提出Top-K提示（Top-K prompting）作为训练和推理范式，用于捕捉多样且合理的反应预测。2) 构建包含约4560万条已验证反应的超大规模数据集CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL。3) 训练C3LM（Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model）模型。4) 集成ChemCensor（化学约束审查）和面向新颖性的奖励信号进行微调。

Result: 在OOD（分布外）URSA-expert-2026基准上取得SOTA（最先进）性能。反应唯一性分析表明，LLMs与传统方法探索互补的反应空间，支持集成式逆合成系统的构建。

Conclusion: Top-K、可感知合理性的训练范式为未来基于LLM的合成规划提供了实用且稳健的新方向，同时支持集成式逆合成框架以充分利用互补探索空间。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis is a central component of computer-aided synthesis planning, yet its intrinsically one-to-many nature is poorly captured by single-answer evaluation and benchmarking protocols. To address this, we introduce Top-K prompting as a robust training and inference paradigm to better capture diverse, plausible reaction predictions. We compile CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL, an ultra-large-scale dataset of ~45.6 million verified reactions to train the C3LM (Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model). By integrating fine-tuning with ChemCensor-based and novelty-oriented rewards, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OOD URSA-expert-2026 benchmark. Further analysis of reaction uniqueness shows that LLMs and conventional models explore complementary reaction spaces, motivating ensemble-based retrosynthesis systems. Overall, our results establish Top-K, plausibility-aware training as a practical new direction for robust future LLM-based synthesis planning.

</details>


### [259] [Classifying Directional Trajectories Near Criticality in the Three-State Majority-Vote Model with Deep Belief Networks and Bidirectional GRUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18235)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度信念网络（DBN）和双向门控循环单元（Bi-GRU）是否能区分三态多数投票模型（MV3）中四种动态上不同的轨迹类型。DBN以无监督方式在静态平衡样本上预训练，将格点快照编码为81维潜在向量；t-SNE分析显示其对四类轨迹仅有部分区分能力。基于DBN编码序列的双层Bi-GRU分类器则实现了四类轨迹的近乎完美分离，并能通过滑动窗口实时感知系统的动态状态，为基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中的临界转换检测提供了一种分层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线舆论、社会物理学等复杂系统中，识别和理解系统从一种状态向另一种状态的临界转变是一个重要但困难的问题。传统方法依赖于静态配置的统计量，难以捕捉方向性的时间结构。本文旨在探索深层表示学习（DBN）与时序建模（Bi-GRU）的层次化组合能否更精确地分类和检测MV3模型中的动态轨迹类型，从而为复杂系统中的临界相变检测提供新方法。

Method: 首先使用高斯-伯努利受限玻尔兹曼机（Gaussian-Bernoulli RBM）作为输入层，以无监督方式在静态平衡样本上预训练DBN（架构784→4096→225→81），将每个格点快照编码为81维潜在向量。随后在DBN编码的50帧序列上训练一个两层Bi-GRU分类器，用于区分四种轨迹类型：从无序接近、从有序接近、向无序离开、向有序离开。利用t-SNE对DBN和Bi-GRU潜在空间进行可视化分析，并通过滑动窗口方式将训练好的Bi-GRU应用于连续MV3动力学序列，测试其实时感知系统动态状态的能力。

Result: DBN的潜在空间仅显示四类轨迹的部分分离，说明仅从静态配置训练的模型不能完全解析方向性的时间结构。而两层Bi-GRU分类器在训练集和测试集上的隐藏状态空间中均实现了四类轨迹的近乎完美分离（t-SNE可视化确认）。滑动窗口的实时应用表明Bi-GRU能在连续动力学中实时感知系统当前的动态状态。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了一种分层架构，将无监督的静态表示学习（DBN）与监督的时序建模（Bi-GRU）相结合，能够有效检测和分类基于智能体的舆论动力学模型中的临界转换，为复杂系统中的实时动态监测和临界相变预警提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate whether the latent representations learned by a Deep Belief Network (DBN) and a Bidirectional Gated Recurrent Unit (Bi-GRU) can discriminate among four dynamically distinct trajectory types in the three-state majority vote model (MV3): approach from disorder, approach from order, departure to disorder, and departure to order. The DBN, pre-trained in an unsupervised manner on static equilibrium samples via a Gaussian-Bernoulli Restricted Boltzmann Machine input layer and architecture $784 \to 4096 \to 225 \to 81$, encodes each lattice snapshot into an 81-dimensional latent vector. A t-SNE analysis of the DBN latent space reveals only partial separation of the four trajectory types, reflecting the fact that a model trained on static configurations cannot fully resolve directional temporal structure. A two-layer Bi-GRU classifier, trained on sequences of DBN-encoded snapshots of length $T = 50$, achieves near-perfect separation of all four trajectory types in its hidden state space, as confirmed by t-SNE visualization on both training and test sets. Furthermore, a sliding-window application of the trained Bi-GRU to continuous MV3 dynamics demonstrates its ability to sense the system's current dynamical regime in real-time. These results establish a principled hierarchical architecture for detecting and classifying critical transitions in agent-based opinion dynamics models.

</details>


### [260] [ClosureBench: A Constructive Benchmark for Compositional Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18242)
*Stefano Goria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ClosureBench，一个基于程序验证的组合图关系推理构造性基准，可即时生成实例以防范数据污染。评估1.5B到前沿模型后发现：(1)基准能直接测量记忆化，微调模型在新旧实例上准确率差距达19.3个百分点；(2)图规模和查询深度增大会导致性能下降，最前沿模型也因误读图描述而从原子查询退化为组合查询；(3)4B模型微调为生成可执行程序（而非答案）后，在组合层级上性能几乎持平，接近前沿水平（94.3%），且计算成本远低于前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定测试集基准易受数据污染影响，无法区分模型是真正推理还是记忆；同时缺乏能够精确测量图关系推理能力、控制诸多难度维度并提供机器验证正确性参考答案的基准。

Method: ClosureBench基于Ein张量逻辑语言执行程序来计算每个实例的参考答案，确保机器验证正确性。基准含26个任务类别、三个组合层级（L1-L3），难度沿图大小、边密度、查询深度三个独立轴控制。评估涵盖1.5B开源到o3、GPT-4.1等前沿模型，并通过对4B模型进行程序合成（Ein和Python+NetworkX两种目标语言）微调来验证程序生成方案。

Result: (1) 微调模型在已见测试集与新鲜实例上的准确率差距为19.3个百分点，静态基准无法揭示此记忆化现象；(2) 图大小和查询深度增加导致准确率下降且两因素交互，所有模型（包括最强前沿模型）都因从自然语言描述中误读图而后在错误图上正确推理而退化，且该瓶颈在JSON边列表/邻接矩阵输入下依然存在；(3) 4B模型微调为输出可执行程序后，跨组合层级性能几乎持平，在保留实例上达94.3%，接近前沿模型且计算成本低得多；该现象在Ein和Python+NetworkX两个程序目标上都成立。

Conclusion: 图关系推理的主要瓶颈不在推理本身，而在于模型从输入描述中正确构建图结构的能力；验证性程序合成（而非直接输出答案）是提升组合推理鲁棒性的有效范式，其效果与具体编程语言无关；ClosureBench的即时实例生成和程序验证机制为基准测试提供了抗污染的范式。

Abstract: We introduce ClosureBench, a constructive benchmark for compositional graph-relational reasoning with programmatically verified ground truth. Unlike fixed-test-set benchmarks vulnerable to data contamination, ClosureBench generates instances on demand: each task's reference answer is computed by executing a program in the Ein tensor-logic language, ensuring machine-verified correctness. The benchmark spans 26 task categories at three compositional levels (L1-L3), with difficulty controlled along three independent axes: graph size, edge density, and query depth.
  We evaluate models from 1.5B open weights to frontier systems (o3, GPT-4.1, Gemini 2.5, Claude Sonnet 4) and report three findings. First, because the benchmark can always supply fresh instances, it measures memorisation directly: a model fine-tuned on a fixed test set shows a 19.3 percentage-point gap between its accuracy on seen and on fresh instances, which a static test set cannot reveal. We scope this to supervised fine-tuning on answer pairs, not pretraining contamination. Second, accuracy falls as graph size and query depth increase, and the two interact: models misread the graph from its natural-language description and then reason correctly over the wrong graph, so even the strongest frontier model degrades from atomic to compositional queries. This bottleneck is a property of the reasoning rather than the input format: it persists when the graph is given as a JSON edge list or an adjacency matrix instead of prose. Third, a 4B model fine-tuned to emit executable programs rather than answers stays nearly flat across compositional levels and approaches frontier accuracy (94.3% on held-out instances) at a fraction of the token cost. This holds for two program targets, Ein and Python+NetworkX, so it is a property of verified program synthesis rather than of one language.

</details>


### [261] [Bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18244)
*Samuel Kostousov,Abhinn Kaushik,Brokoslaw Laschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过频谱正则化框架系统地调节表征几何，证明可以显著提升生物与人工神经网络之间的双向对齐（bidirectional alignment），在自监督对比视觉模型上实现了双向可预测性55%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明生物与人工神经网络之间的表征对齐是不对称的：模型表征预测神经反应的能力远强于神经反应预测模型表征的能力。这种不对称性引出了一个关键问题——表征几何是否会影响双向对齐。作者假设在训练过程中引导表征几何可以系统地影响双向对齐，以弥补这一不对称性。

Method: 作者开发了一个将频谱正则化（spectral regularization）与双向可预测性分析相结合的计算框架。在自监督对比视觉模型上，通过训练过程中引导学习表征的频谱几何（spectral geometry），系统性地调节表征的谱结构。分析指标包括前向可预测性（forward predictivity）、反向可预测性（reverse predictivity）以及双向可预测性，并考察了有效维度（effective dimensionality）与共享表征子空间的重组情况。

Result: 引导频谱几何后，反向可预测性显著提升，同时前向可预测性仅有适度下降，最终实现了双向可预测性55%的相对提升。这些改善伴随着有效维度的降低以及共享表征子空间的重新组织，在中间频谱指数（spectral exponents）下，前向和反向可预测性趋于近似对称。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明，表征几何可以被系统性地引导以调节生物与人工神经网络之间的双向表征对齐，为理解并改善跨物种神经表征匹配提供了新途径。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks is asymmetric: model representations predict neural responses much better than neural responses predict model representations. This asymmetry raises the question of whether representational geometry contributes to bidirectional representational alignment. We hypothesized that steering representational geometry during training can systematically influence bidirectional alignment. To test this hypothesis, we developed a computational framework that integrates spectral regularization with bidirectional predictivity analyses. As an initial demonstration, we evaluated our framework using self-supervised contrastive vision models. Steering the spectral geometry of the learned representations substantially increased reverse predictivity with modest reductions in forward predictivity, yielding a 55% relative improvement in bidirectional predictivity. These improvements were accompanied by reduced effective dimensionality and a reorganization of the shared representational subspace, within which forward and reverse predictivity became approximately symmetric at intermediate spectral exponents. Overall, these findings demonstrate that representational geometry can be systematically steered to modulate bidirectional representational alignment between biological and artificial neural networks.

</details>


### [262] [SIGMA: Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive Control for Robust, Dependable Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18263)
*Pratham Payra,Jagadish B,Tanmay Sen,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SIGMA是一个结合大语言模型和多目标强化学习的交通信号控制系统，通过自然语言应急指令生成动态优先级向量，并利用旋转增强和离线到在线学习提升泛化能力与稳定性，在苏姆（SUMO）仿真中显著优于传统控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制强化学习方法存在三个关键缺陷：目标函数固定不变，无法适应动态优先级变化（如应急车辆通行需求）；在几何相似的交叉口间泛化能力不足；且依赖繁琐的人工奖励工程。本文旨在解决这些局限性，提出一个能够自适应调节目标、跨交叉口泛化且具备可靠性的交通控制框架。

Method: 提出SIGMA框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 利用LLM将自然语言应急命令转换为多目标actor-critic控制器所需的优先级向量，避免人工奖励工程；(2) 采用旋转增强（rotational augmentation）实现方向不变学习，提高四向交叉口间的迁移能力；(3) 采用离线到在线（offline-to-online）学习策略，实现稳定初始化并逐步适应交通变化。同时定义了涵盖应急服务水平、LLM故障下优雅降级及需求敏感性的可靠性属性，并通过bootstrap统计进行验证。

Result: 在SUMO仿真平台上的四个加尔各答城市交叉口进行评测，与固定配时、感应控制和DQN控制器对比，SIGMA降低了平均/应急等待时间和队列长度，并提升了通行能力。消融实验证实了其对组件故障和几何旋转的鲁棒性。统计可靠性保证验证了各可靠性属性。

Conclusion: SIGMA提供了一个可靠、语言引导的多目标交通控制系统，兼具统计可靠性保证，在动态优先级适应、跨交叉口泛化和系统韧性方面相较现有方法均有显著改进。

Abstract: Traffic signal control is a complex sequential decision-making problem requiring real-time adaptation and trade-offs among throughput, delay fairness, signal stability, and emergency vehicle priority. Existing RL methods often fix objectives, ignore dynamic priority changes, and fail to generalize across geometrically similar intersections.We propose SIGMA (Symmetry-aware, Intelligent, Geometric, Multi-objective Adaptive traffic control), an RL framework enhanced with a large language model (LLM) for adaptive objective tuning and orientation-invariant learning. SIGMA converts natural-language emergency commands into priority vectors for a multi-objective actor-critic controller, avoiding manual reward engineering. Rotational augmentation improves transferability across four-way intersections, while offline-to-online learning ensures stable initialization and gradual adaptation to changing traffic.We define reliability properties covering emergency service levels, graceful degradation under LLM failures, and demand sensitivity, validated via bootstrap statistics. Evaluated in SUMO on four Kolkata-based urban intersections against fixed-time, actuated, and DQN controllers, SIGMA reduces average/emergency waiting times and queue lengths, and boosts throughput. Ablation studies confirm robustness to component failures and geometric rotations. Overall, SIGMA offers a reliable, language-guided, multi-objective traffic control system with statistical reliability assurance.

</details>


### [263] [Rethinking Privileged Information in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18271)
*Samyak Shrestha,Alexander Tessier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，性能提升是否真正来自特权参考信息，还是仅反映了基础模型已有的推理行为恢复。通过Qwen3系列模型（1.7B-8B）在科学和数学数据集上的实验，作者将参考信息引起的监督与无参考的教师监督分离，发现正确参考并未带来一致的性能收益，学生的预测更偏向基础模型的思考行为而非参考信息，且性能提升与分布对齐之间没有可靠关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD训练中，学生从自身响应中学习，并以特权参考信息为条件的token级监督。现有文献常假设性能提升表明学生学到了参考中的信息，但这一假设未被严格验证。作者旨在回答：OPSD的收益究竟是学生真正学到了参考信息，还是仅仅恢复了基础模型中已有的推理行为？

Method: 采用分析框架将参考信息引发的监督与无参考的教师监督分离，分别测量两者与学生预测变化的对齐程度。在Qwen3系列（1.7B-8B）上，对科学和数学数据集执行OPSD实验，并构建来自其他问题的对照实验以区分参考信息特有的影响。

Result: （1）正确参考在教师生成模式、模型规模和训练数据集之间未提供一致的性能收益；（2）无正确参考时学生也能提升，且来自其他问题的解答在多个数学推理基准上可优于正确解答；（3）学生预测与基础模型思考行为的对齐强于与参考引发监督的对齐，但其他问题的对照可复现大部分对齐；（4）更强的正确参考对齐并不稳定对应更大的性能收益。

Conclusion: 仅凭性能增益和分布对齐无法确定特权参考信息在OPSD中对学生学习的贡献方式，这要求对OPSD中的知识转移机制进行更严格的归因研究。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own responses using token-level supervision from the same model conditioned on privileged reference information. We investigate whether performance gains from OPSD show that the student learned the information in the reference or instead reflect recovery of reasoning behavior already present in the base model. We perform OPSD experiments on science and mathematics datasets using Qwen3 models ranging from 1.7B to 8B. Our analysis framework separates the supervision induced by the reference from the supervision provided by the teacher without the reference and measures how each aligns with changes in the student's predictions. The correct reference does not provide a consistent performance benefit across teacher generation modes, model sizes, and training datasets. Students can improve without the correct reference, and a solution from another problem can outperform the correct solution on several mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The student's predictions align more strongly with the base model's thinking behavior than with the supervision induced by the reference, but controls constructed from other problems reproduce much of both alignments. Moreover, stronger alignment attributable to the correct reference does not reliably coincide with a greater performance benefit from the reference. Performance gains and distributional alignment alone therefore cannot determine how privileged reference information contributes to student learning in OPSD.

</details>


### [264] [SingularClip: Preventing Spectral Collapse to Maintain Plasticity in Continual and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18319)
*Tyler Kastner,Nimrod De La Vega,Amir-massoud Farahmand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现神经网络在非平稳任务训练中因权重矩阵奇异值的各向异性增长而丧失可塑性，并提出SingularClip方法——周期性裁剪所有权重矩阵的奇异值以缓解该问题，在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的多种任务中表现优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在非平稳任务（持续学习/强化学习）中训练时经常丧失适应新目标的能力（即可塑性丧失）。现有研究尚未充分揭示权重矩阵奇异值各向异性增长这一具体的可塑性丧失来源，作者旨在从理论和实证角度识别并解决该问题。

Method: 1) 实证分析权重矩阵奇异值在训练过程中的各向异性增长与可塑性丧失之间的关联；2) 从理论上分析该现象；3) 提出SingularClip方法，即周期性对所有权重矩阵的奇异值进行裁剪（clipping），防止奇异值过度发散导致各向异性。

Result: SingularClip在持续监督学习和深度强化学习的一系列任务上，与多种基线方法相比均取得了更强的性能，验证了其对可塑性丧失的缓解有效性。

Conclusion: 权重矩阵奇异值的各向异性增长是导致可塑性丧失的一个新来源，通过SingularClip对奇异值进行周期性裁剪可有效缓解该问题，为持续学习与强化学习中的可塑性维护提供了一种简单而有效的正则化手段。

Abstract: Neural networks trained on nonstationary tasks frequently lose the ability to fit new targets, a phenomenon referred to as loss of plasticity. We identify a novel source of plasticity loss due to the growing anisotropy of weight matrices' singular values during training, and analyze this phenomenon both empirically and theoretically. To mitigate this issue, we introduce SingularClip, a procedure that periodically clips the singular values of all weight matrices. We show that SingularClip performs strongly against baselines across a range of tasks in both continual supervised learning and deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [265] [When Does Dynamic Ensembling Pay Off? Diagnosing Regionwise Gains in Regression under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18330)
*Tianxin Zhou,Ruixi Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D_hat_CF5诊断量，利用小规模标注目标域探针数据来预测"区域级动态回归模型组合是否优于静态混合"，通过交叉拟合估计区域凸组合相对最优静态凸组合的真实收益。在12/16组数据集-漂移对上分别达到Spearman +0.98和+0.83的预测精度，并用于构建Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector，在预注册前瞻批次中12次运行全部达到或超过静态基线，其中两次部署分别降低11%和16%的测试风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入依赖（动态）回归模型池组合是否优于最佳静态混合取决于数据漂移类型，且部署前通常未知。是否能用少量标注的目标域探针数据判断"跨输入空间区域重新分配信任"是否值得，是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出D_hat_CF5，从探针数据中通过交叉拟合估计区域级凸组合相对最优静态凸组合的增益（可实现的区域信任重分配价值）。配合Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector：在静态仿射堆叠器与动态实现器之间选择，仅当留出下置信界超过静态凸基线时才部署候选模型。还将此方法集成到可复现的评估框架OpenRegShift中。

Result: 在12个数据集-漂移对（空间、时间、领域、特征簇）冻结套件上，D_hat_CF5预测区域级测试增益的数据集级Spearman相关系数达+0.98（95% CI [+0.83, +1.00]，p=5×10^-5），推翻了两项预注册预期；16对敏感性分析中相关系数为+0.83，而替代探针诊断最高仅+0.66。区域凸组合增益相关性为+0.98，而协变量依赖堆叠经仿射校正后仅为+0.01，isolates了区域信任重分配效应。受控生成器显示动态增益来自漂移异质性与局部能力的交互，随漂移严重度增加，且在测试网格中128-256个探针标签时变为可实现。前瞻批次中12次运行全部匹配或改善静态基线，两次部署降低11%和16%风险，门控机制拒绝了未加门控会带来超过30倍静态损失的候选。

Conclusion: 小规模探针数据足以可靠预测动态区域级组合的可实现增益，该诊断量可驱动安全的模型选择门控机制，在分布漂移下实现可验证的收益，并可通过OpenRegShift框架复现验证。

Abstract: Whether input-dependent ("dynamic") combination of a regression model pool beats the best static blend depends on the shift and is rarely known before deployment. Can a small labeled target-domain probe tell us when reallocating trust across regions of the input space will pay off? We answer this with $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$, which estimates from the probe the cross-fitted gain of the regionwise convex combination over the best static convex blend: the realizable value of deciding, region by region, whom to trust. Across a frozen suite of 12 dataset-shift pairs (spatial, temporal, domain, feature-cluster), $\widehat{D}_{\mathrm{CF5}}$ predicts realized regionwise test gains with dataset-level Spearman $+0.98$ (95% CI $[+0.83, +1.00]$; $p=5\times10^{-5}$), including two cases overturning preregistered expectations. The relationship holds in a 16-pair sensitivity analysis (Spearman $+0.83$), whereas alternative probe diagnostics reach at most $+0.66$. This contrast isolates regional trust reallocation: correlation is $+0.98$ for regionwise-convex gain, but $+0.01$ for smooth covariate-dependent stacking after affine correction. A controlled generator shows dynamic gains arise from the interaction of shift heterogeneity and local competence, increase with shift severity, and become realizable between 128 and 256 probe labels in the tested grid. The Probe-Validated Ensemble Selector chooses among a static affine stacker and dynamic realizers, deploying a candidate only when a held-out lower confidence bound clears the static-convex floor. In a preregistered prospective batch, it matched or improved the floor in all 12 runs; two deployments reduced test risk by 11% and 16%, while the gate rejected a candidate whose un-gated deployment incurred $>30\times$ the static loss. We release OpenRegShift, a reproducible evaluation harness for regression ensembles under distribution shift.

</details>


### [266] [Selection, Recombination, or a Fresh Solve? A Candidate-Free Control for Single-Pass Test-Time Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18379)
*Guiv Farmanfarmaian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多候选聚合（多数投票类）场景下，当候选答案全部错误或部分正确时，条件化于候选池的聚合求解是否优于直接重新生成（fresh solve）。通过在相同输出token预算下引入缺失的候选无关对照组，并在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025基准上使用Qwen3-4B模型，发现：多候选正确时候选条件化提升准确率(+0.290)，全部候选错误时反而降低准确率(-0.123)，单一正确候选情景结论不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时推理（test-time reasoning）研究缺少一个关键的对照实验：候选条件化（candidate conditioning）带来的收益是否仅源于额外的生成次数（additional generation pass），而非候选上下文本身提供了有效信息。此前没有在相同最大输出token预算下引入"候选无关"（candidate-free）的控制条件，因此无法区分聚合的效用来源于重组（recombination）、重新求解还是额外计算。

Method: 在相同最大输出token限制下引入缺失的候选无关对照组，并按正确候选数量（c0、c1、c2+）分层比较。实验使用Qwen3-4B模型在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025两个数学基准上评估，同时进行适应性双基准流程的保守校正、原始格式匹配与安慰剂（placebo）实验，并单独设计结构化干预：显式答案字段强制引导输出，以及答案遮蔽（masking）条件下的准确率对比。

Result: 候选条件化在多个候选正确时带来显著提升（c2+：+0.290）；全部候选错误时导致准确率下降（c0：-0.123）；单一正确候选情形结论未定。上述c2+与c0结论在适应性双基准保守校正后依然成立。在反事实框架下，全部错误候选上的聚合恢复实际上不如直接重新求解。结构化干预表明显式答案字段会因果性地将输出拉向该字段值，遮蔽后无显著准确率提升，且与原始格式的等价性未建立。

Conclusion: 候选池中若包含多个正确答案，聚合受益；若所有候选均错误，条件化反而有害，此时"重新求解"优于"在错误候选上聚合"。研究强调了对照组设计在评估test-time reasoning方法中的必要性，并指出证据边界：仅限Qwen3-4B单一模型族、两个数学基准、首答截断的候选片段及单次采样提示聚合。

Abstract: When every candidate is wrong, correct-candidate selection is unavailable, yet the aggregation call can still solve the problem afresh. A correct aggregate answer may therefore reflect recombination, fresh solving, or both. For efficient test-time reasoning, the relevant question is whether candidate context adds value beyond the additional generation pass. We introduce the missing candidate-free control under the same maximum output-token allowance and stratify by the number of correct candidates. Across AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025 with Qwen3-4B, candidate conditioning improves accuracy when multiple candidates are correct ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c2+) = +0.290), lowers accuracy when every candidate is wrong ($Δ_{\mathrm{cand}}$(c0) = -0.123), and remains unresolved in the one-correct regime. The c2+ and c0 conclusions survive a conservative correction for the adaptive two-benchmark procedure. Under this counterfactual, the interpretation of all-wrong recovery reverses at this scale: conditioning on an all-wrong candidate pool lowers accuracy relative to a fresh solve. Original-format matching and placebo results characterize the failures descriptively but leave their mechanism unresolved. Within a separate structured intervention, explicit answer fields causally steer outputs toward their values; masking yields no measurable accuracy improvement, and equivalence with the original format was not established. The evidence is limited to one Qwen3-4B family, two mathematics benchmarks, first-answer-truncated candidate fragments, and single-pass prompted aggregation.

</details>


### [267] [Vector Symbolic Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18404)
*Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,SungHeon Jeong,Hyunwoo Oh,Raheeb Hassan,Pietro Mercati,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出向量符号策略梯度（VSPG），一种基于超向量表示的离散动作actor，通过将每个动作表示为单位范数超向量并与编码状态进行相似度比较来评分。VSPG在标准softmax策略梯度代理下被证明其更新等同于优势加权超向量绑定后归一化，且每个训练后的动作超向量是一个固定大小的压缩核记忆，能存储优势加权的核扩展并在状态间传递证据，同时提供量化鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索向量符号架构（VSA）在强化学习中的潜力，回答如何将动作记忆表示为超向量并实现样本高效学习的问题。

Method: VSPG将每个动作编码为单位范数超向量，通过相似度评分生成离散动作策略。在标准softmax策略梯度框架下推导其精确更新规则为优势加权超向量绑定+归一化。同时证明动作超向量本质上是固定大小的压缩核记忆，并针对双极性动作记忆给出随机比特翻转下的稳定性证明。

Result: 证明了VSPG更新等同于优势加权超向量绑定后的归一化，支持标准优势估计器；每个动作超向量是固定大小压缩核记忆，可存储优势加权核扩展并依据编码器诱导相似度传递证据；对双极性动作记忆，贪心动作选择在随机比特翻转下稳定，失败概率随超向量维度指数衰减。

Conclusion: VSPG统一了VSA动作记忆、log线性策略梯度和核策略搜索三个方向，同时提供量化鲁棒性保证，在不增加推理内存的情况下支持样本高效学习。

Abstract: We answer this question with Vector-Symbolic Policy Gradient (VSPG), a discrete-action actor that represents each action by a unit-norm hypervector and scores it by similarity to the encoded state. Under the standard softmax policy-gradient surrogate, we prove that its update is exactly advantage-weighted hypervector bundling followed by normalization, and therefore supports standard advantage estimators. We further show that each trained action hypervector is a fixed-size compressed kernel memory, storing an advantage-weighted kernel expansion over visited states and transferring evidence according to the encoder-induced similarity. This provides a concrete mechanism that can support sample-efficient learning without increasing inference-time memory. Finally, for bipolar action memories, we prove that greedy action selection is stable under random bit flips, with failure probability decaying exponentially in the hypervector dimension. VSPG thus connects VSA action memories, log-linear policy gradients, and kernel policy search while providing a quantitative robustness guarantee.

</details>


### [268] [Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18410)
*Wei Jiang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RoleSub（Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing），一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的子令牌压缩方法。在视觉令牌缩减后，RoleSub将保留令牌的值表示在正交空间中分组，并通过轻量级路由器根据令牌表示、潜在角色表示和语言上下文决定保留哪些组，同时可应用于语言值表示。在OpenVLA-OFT-7B上的LIBERO基准测试中，RoleSub在36个设置中的33个超越了仅令牌剪枝的基线，可将总KV缓存压缩至原始的9.2%-11.3%仍保持良好控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型处理长多模态令牌序列，推理在内存和计算上代价高昂。现有效率方法主要依赖视觉令牌剪枝，但激进剪枝会丢弃整个令牌表示而显得脆弱。子令牌压缩提供了一种互补方案，能保留更多令牌同时减少其值宽度。然而直接将其应用于VLA策略效果不佳，因为感知、语言理解和控制所需的信息在多模态表示中分布不同，需要一种能感知多模态角色差异的压缩方法。

Method: RoleSub包含三个关键设计：（1）在视觉令牌缩减后，将每个保留令牌的值表示在正交空间中分割为多个子组；（2）使用轻量级路由器决定保留哪些子组，路由决策以令牌表示、学习到的潜在角色表示和语言上下文为条件；（3）同一机制也可扩展应用于语言值表示，从而在不额外删除令牌的情况下压缩视觉和语言表示。

Result: 在OpenVLA-OFT-7B上的四个LIBERO数据集评估中：（1）在匹配的视觉KV预算下，RoleSub在36个设置中的33个优于仅令牌剪枝的训练控制方法，且在激进压缩条件下收益最大；（2）结合视觉和语言压缩可将总KV降至原始的9.2%-11.3%，同时在大多数任务上保持强控制性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在保留令牌内部减少表示宽度是对令牌剪枝的有效补充，能够实现激进的VLA模型压缩。通过在子令牌级别进行角色条件化的路由压缩，可以在大幅降低内存和计算开销的同时维持良好的控制性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models process long multimodal token sequences, making inference expensive in both memory and computation. Existing efficiency methods mainly reduce visual tokens, but aggressive token pruning becomes fragile because removing a token discards its entire representation. Sub-token compression provides a complementary alternative by retaining more tokens while reducing their value width. However, directly applying sub-token compression to VLA policies is less effective because information important for perception, language understanding, and control is distributed differently across the multimodal representation.
  We introduce Role-Conditioned Sub-Token Routing (RoleSub), which learns how to compress the value representations of retained tokens. After visual token reduction, RoleSub partitions each retained value representation into groups in an orthogonal space and uses a lightweight router to determine which groups should be preserved. The routing decision is conditioned on the token representation, a learned latent role representation, and language context. The same mechanism can also be applied to language values, allowing visual and language representations to be compressed without removing additional tokens.
  We evaluate RoleSub on OpenVLA-OFT-7B across the four LIBERO suites. At matched visual-KV budgets, RoleSub outperforms a trained token-only control in 33 of 36 settings, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. Combining visual and language compression reduces total KV to 9.2--11.3% of the original while retaining strong control performance on most tasks. These results show that reducing the representation within retained tokens provides an effective complement to token pruning for aggressive VLA compression.

</details>


### [269] [The Road Taken: The Role of Optimizers at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18415)
*Jaerin Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现有"稳定边缘"理论（基于最大Hessian特征值和学习率）在多类一阶优化器上严重偏离实际稳定性边界（偏差可达×21.1），提出了基于方向Hessian和梯度对齐得分的"实现稳定边缘"新框架，能消除优化器相关偏差并提供更一致的稳定性阈值预测，同时揭示了优化器在时空预算平衡中的独特作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稳定边缘理论仅以最大Hessian特征值和学习率刻画优化稳定性阈值，但实验发现梯度下降等多种一阶方法实际稳定性显著违反该边界（偏差高达21.1倍），且该偏差具有系统性并高度依赖底层优化器类型，说明现有表述无法捕捉真实优化行为，亟需新理论框架。

Method: 从方向Hessian（directional Hessian）和梯度对齐得分（gradient-alignment score）出发，以优化器实际采取的更新方向而非最大曲率方向为基准重新推导稳定性阈值，由此构建"实现稳定边缘"（realized edge of stability）的新公式体系。

Result: 新公式消除了不同优化器间系统性的偏移，提供更一致的稳定阈值预测；同时引入了新的诊断工具，揭示了优化器在一阶优化中主动平衡时间与空间预算（temporal and spatial budgets）的独特作用。

Conclusion: 基于实际更新方向重新表述的稳定边缘理论比传统的最大特征值表述更能刻画一阶优化器的真实稳定性行为，并为理解优化器内在动态提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The edge of stability refers to a phenomenon in deep learning with gradient-based optimizers where the Hessian eigenvalues of the loss remain stable above a threshold that the classical descent lemma predicts to be unstable. Previous works formulate the edge of stability with respect to the maximum Hessian eigenvalue and the learning rate. However, we observe that many first-order methods, including gradient descent, significantly violate the stability bound predicted by these theories by a factor as large as $\times 21.1$. Moreover, this deviation turns out to be systematic and highly dependent on the underlying optimizer, which is not captured by previous formulations. This calls for a new formulation of the stability threshold, which we derive from the directional Hessian and the gradient-alignment score with respect to the actual update taken by the optimizer, rather than the maximum curvature mode. Our new formulation of the realized edge of stability not only removes optimizer-dependent offsets and provides more consistent predictions of the stability threshold, but also introduces new diagnostic tools that reveal the unique role of the optimizer in actively balancing between the temporal and spatial budgets in first-order optimization.

</details>


### [270] [Mechanistic Interpretability of Structure-Aware Numerical Reasoning in LLaMA 3.1 8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18419)
*Rahul Chowdhury,Timothy A Rupprecht,Senhao Cao,Jiahao Liu,Octavia Camps,David Bau,Pu Zhao,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这项工作从机制可解释性角度研究Llama 3.1-8B在数值序列建模任务中的能力，发现模型通过内部表征计算和存储一阶差分，并通过类似归纳电路的机制检索相关差分并将其加到当前值上，首次识别出这种概念归纳形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究表明LLM在时间序列预测中表现出强大的数值序列建模和上下文学习能力，但其背后的机制尚不清楚。作者想探究LLM是否真正理解序列的底层结构（至少需要对序列中数字的一阶差分进行推理），从而从机制可解释性的角度揭示其内部工作原理。

Method: 作者设计了一个无法通过表面线索解决的序列建模任务：采样n个随机数并加上偏移量重复出现。使用Llama 3.1-8B进行实验，采用探测实验（probing）评估模型内部表征是否计算和存储一阶差分，并使用激活修补（activation patching）进行反事实分析来揭示模型检索相关一阶差分并加到当前值的具体机制。

Result: Llama在任务上表现强劲，说明它能捕捉到底层结构。探测实验表明模型无需显式监督即可在内部表征中计算和存储一阶差分。激活修补揭示模型通过类似归纳电路的机制检索相关的一阶差分，并将其添加到当前数值上。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在LLM中识别出概念归纳（concept induction）这种形式，为理解LLM如何进行数值序列推断和时间序列预测提供了机制层面的洞察，展示了LLM并非简单地记忆或模式匹配，而是能够内部构建结构性推理。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong numerical sequence modeling capabilities and show promise in time-series prediction.
